##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—recited by us
- तत् (*tat*)—this praise
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- आपः (*āpaḥ*)—the waters
- ओषधीः (*oṣadhīḥ*)—the herbs
- वनिनः (*vaninaḥ*)—the trees
- जुषंते (*juṣaṃte*)—may they accept
- (वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we)
- मरुतां (*marutāṃ*)—of the Maruts
- उपस्थे (*upasthe*)—abiding in the presence
- शर्मन् (*śarman*)—in a happy house
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be
- (O gods) यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पाते (*pāte*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Indra, Varuṇa, Mitra, Agni, the waters, the herbs and the trees accept this praise recited by us. May we, abiding in the presence of the Maruts, be in a happy house. O gods, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
May Indra, Varuna, Mitra, Agni, the waters, the herbs, the trees, be pleased by our (praise): may we, (reclining) on the lap of the Maruts, enjoy felicity: and do you ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 35 (Maṇḍala 7)
The thirty-fifth sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शं न इंद्राग्नी इति पंचदशर्चं द्वितीयं सूक्तं । आत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका ।
> शं नः पंचोना शांतिरिति । वसिष्ठ ऋषिः । त्रिष्टुप् छंदः । वैश्वदेवं हेत्युक्तत्वादिदमपि वैश्वदेवं ॥ महानाम्नीव्रत एतत्सूक्तं जप्यं । तथा च सूत्रितं । भद्रं कर्णेभिः शृणुयाम देवाः शं न इंद्राग्नी भवतामवोभिः । आ. ८-१४॥ इति । एव....सु॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta शं न इंद्राग्नी (*śaṃ na iṃdrāgnī*) of fifteen Ṛks is the second sūkta. The Anukramaṇikā says: शं नः पंचोना शांतिरिति (*śaṃ naḥ paṃconā śāṃtiriti*). Vasiṣṭha is the seer, the metre is Triṣṭubh. Since it was said that the deity is Vaiśvadeva, this too is Vaiśvadeva. In the Mahānāmnī vrata this sūkta is to be muttered (japya). So it is laid down in the sūtra: भद्रं कर्णेभिः शृणुयाम देवाः शं न इंद्राग्नी भवतामवोभिः (*bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devāḥ śaṃ na iṃdrāgnī bhavatāmavobhiḥ*) (Ā. 8-14). The quotation then ends in the abridgement एव....सु (*eva....su*).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta शं न इंद्राग्नी (*śaṃ na iṃdrāgnī*) is the second sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has fifteen Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: शं नः पंचोना शांतिः (*śaṃ naḥ paṃconā śāṃtiḥ*). The seer of this sūkta is Vasiṣṭha, the deity the Viśvedevas, the metre Triṣṭubh. That this sūkta is to be muttered in the vrata called Mahānāmnī (a rite), one of the four vratas taught in the domestic rites, is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, भद्रं कर्णेभिः शृणुयाम देवाः शं न इंद्राग्नी भवतामवोभिः (*bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devāḥ śaṃ na iṃdrāgnī bhavatāmavobhiḥ*) (Ā. 8-14).

### Printed p. 167
Sūkta 35. Maṇḍala 7. Anuvāka 3. Sūkta 35. Aṣṭaka 5. Adhyāya 3. Vargas 28, 29, 30. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 15. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Viśvedevas. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं न॑ इंद्रा॒ग्नी भ॑वता॒मवो॑भिः॒ शं न॒ इंद्रा॒वरु॑णा रा॒तह॑व्या ।  
> शमिंद्रा॒सोमा॑ सुवि॒ताय॒ शं योः शं न॒ इंद्रा॑पू॒षणा॒ वाज॑सातौ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । इंद्राग्नी इति । भवतां । अवःऽभिः । शं । नः । इंद्रावरुणा । रातहव्या ।  
> शं । इंद्रासोमा । सुविताय । शं । योः । शं । नः । इंद्रापूषणा । वाजसातौ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्माकमस्मभ्यं वेंद्राग्नी अवोभिः रक्षणैः शं शांत्यै भवतां । रातहव्या रातहव्यौ यजमानैर्दत्तहविष्काविंद्रावरुणेंद्रावरुणावपि नोऽस्मभ्यं शं शांत्यै भवतां । इंद्रासोमेंद्रासोमावपि नः शं शांत्यै सुविताय कल्याणाय च भवतां । शं शांत्यै सुखाय च । पुनरुक्तिरादरार्था । अथवा शं शमनहेतुकं सुखं योर्विषययोगनिमित्तं सुखमित्यपुनरुक्तिः । इंद्रापूषणेंद्रापूषणावपि वाजसातौ युद्धेऽन्नलाभे निमित्ते वा नः शं शांत्यै भवतामित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May Indra and Agni, with their protections, be for our peace (शं (*śaṃ*)). May Indra and Varuṇa, रातहव्या (*rātahavyā*), to whom the sacrificers have given oblations, also be for our peace. May Indra and Soma also be for our peace and for our welfare (सुविताय (*suvitāya*)). शं योः (*śaṃ yoḥ*): for peace and for happiness; the repetition is for emphasis. Or else शं (*śaṃ*) is happiness arising from the cessation of evil, and योः (*yoḥ*) is happiness arising from contact with what is desired, so there is no repetition. May Indra and Pūṣan also be for our peace in the वाजसातौ (*vājasātau*), in battle or in the winning of food, or on that occasion.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्राग्नी (*iṃdrāgnī*)—the deities Indra and Agni
- अवोभिः (*avobhiḥ*)—with their protections
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- भवतां (*bhavatāṃ*)—may they be
- रातहव्या (*rātahavyā*)—to whom oblation has been offered
- इंद्रावरुणा (*iṃdrāvaruṇā*)—the deities Indra and Varuṇa
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be for peace
- इंद्रासोमा (*iṃdrāsomā*)—the deities Indra and Soma
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for our peace
- सुविताय (*suvitāya*)—and for welfare, may they be
- शं योः (*śaṃ yoḥ*)—may they strive for the establishment of peace and the removal of sorrow
- इंद्रापूषणा (*iṃdrāpūṣaṇā*)—the deities Indra and Pūṣan
- वाजसातौ (*vājasātau*)—in the battle fought for food
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be givers of happiness

### Printed p. 168

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Indra and Agni, with their protections, be givers of peace to us. May Indra and Varuṇa, to whom the oblation is offered, be givers of peace to us. May Indra and Soma bring us peace and welfare. May they bring us welfare through the removal of our sorrow. May Indra and Pūṣan be givers of happiness to us in the battle fought for food.

##### English translation
May Indra and Agni be (with us) with their protections for our happiness: may Indra and Varuna, to whom oblations are offered, (be with us) for our happiness: may Indra and Soma be (with us) for our happiness, our prosperity, our good: may Indra and Pushan be (with us) in battle for our triumph.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॒ भगः॒ शमु॑ नः॒ शंसो॑ अस्तु॒ शं नः॒ पुरं॑धिः॒ शमु॑ संतु॒ रायः॑ ।  
> शं नः॑ स॒त्यस्य॑ सु॒यम॑स्य॒ शंसः॒ शं नो॑ अर्य॒मा पु॑रुजा॒तो अ॑स्तु ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । भगः । शं । ऊं इति । नः । शंसः । अस्तु । शं । नः । पुरंधिः । शं । ऊं इति । संतु । रायः ।  
> शं । नः । सत्यस्य । सुयमस्य । शंसः । शं । नः । अर्यमा । पुरुजातः । अस्तु ॥२॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भगो देवोऽस्तु । भवतु । नोऽस्माकं शमु शांत्या एव शंसो नराशंसोऽस्तु । भवतु । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै पुरंधिर्बहुधीरप्यस्तु । रायो धनान्यपि शमु शांत्या एव संतु । नोऽस्माकं सुयमस्य शोभनयमयुक्तस्य सत्यस्य शंसो वचनमपि शमस्तु । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै पुरुजातो बहुप्रादुर्भावोऽर्यमा देवोऽप्यस्तु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May the god Bhaga be for our peace. May Narāśaṃsa be for our peace alone. May Purandhi, the one of many thoughts, be for our peace. May riches too be for peace alone. May the utterance (शंसः (*śaṃsaḥ*)) of truth, which is well restrained (सुयमस्य (*suyamasya*), endowed with fair restraint), be for our peace. May the god Aryaman, पुरुजातः (*purujātaḥ*), of many manifestations, also be for our peace.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- भगः (*bhagaḥ*)—Bhaga
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शमु (*śamu*)—for peace alone
- शंसः (*śaṃsaḥ*)—Narāśaṃsa (may he be)
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- पुरंधिः (*puraṃdhiḥ*)—Purandhi

The Pratipadārtha of the previous Ṛk continues:

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- *puraṃdhiḥ*—the deity named Purandhi, may he favour (us).
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—riches also
- शमु (*śamu*)—let them be for our peace alone
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- सुयमस्य (*suyamasya*)—endowed with excellent restraint
- सत्यस्य (*satyasya*)—of the nature of truth
- शंसः (*śaṃsaḥ*)—the speech also
- शं (*śaṃ*)—let it be for happiness alone
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- पुरुजातः (*purujātaḥ*)—having manifold manifestation

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Bhaga be gracious for our peace. May Narāśaṃsa also desire our peace alone. May Purandhi also establish our peace alone. May riches also be for our peace alone. May our speech, which is endowed with excellent restraint and is of the nature of truth, be for our happiness alone. May Aryaman, who has manifold manifestation, also favour us for peace.

##### English translation
May Bhaga (promote) our happiness : may Shansa be our happiness : may Purandhi be (with us for) our happiness : may riches be (a source of) happiness : may the benediction of the true and virtuous yield us happiness : may the variously-manifested Aryaman be (with us) for our felicity.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॑ धा॒ता शमु॑ ध॒र्ता नो॑ अस्तु॒ शं न॑ उरू॒ची भ॑वतु स्व॒धाभिः॑ ।  
> शं रोद॑सी बृह॒ती शं नो॒ अद्रिः॒ शं नो॑ दे॒वानां॑ सु॒हवा॑नि संतु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । धाता । शं । ऊं इति । धर्ता । नः । अस्तु । शं । नः । उरूची । भवतु । स्वधाभिः ।  
> शं । रोदसी इति । बृहती इति । शं । नः । अद्रिः । शं । नः । देवानां । सुहवानि । संतु ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै धाता देवोऽस्तु । नोऽस्माकं शमु शांत्या एव धर्ता पुण्यपापानां विधारयिता वरुणो देवोऽप्यस्तु । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या उरूची विवर्तगमना पृथिव्यपि स्वधाभिरन्नैः सहास्तु । बृहती महत्यौ रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यावपि शं भवतां । अद्रिः पर्वतोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतु । शं शांत्यै नोऽस्माकं देवानां सुहवानि सुह्वयतयो संतु । भवंतु ॥
> 22

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Let Dhātā, the god, be for our peace. Let Dhartā, Varuṇa, the upholder who sustains merits and sins, be for our peace alone. Let Urūcī, the earth of wide course, be with her foods (svadhā) for our peace. Let the two great ones, Rodasī, heaven and earth, be for peace. Let the mountain (adri) be for our peace. Let the good invocations (suhavāni) of the gods be for our peace.

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the creator god be for our peace. May Varuṇa, who apportions merits and sins, also be for our peace alone. May the earth of extended form be full with her foods for our peace. May heaven and earth, of great form, be for our happiness. May the mountain also be for our peace. May our excellent praises addressed to the gods also be effective for peace.

##### English translation
May the creator be to us for happiness : may the discriminator (between virtue and vice Varuna), be (with us) for our happiness : may the wide earth (contribute) with sustenance to our happiness : may the vast heaven and earth be (to us for) happiness : may the mountains (yeild) us happiness : may our pious invocations of the gods secure us happiness.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॑ अ॒ग्निर्ज्योति॑रनीको अस्तु॒ शं नो॑ मि॒त्रावरु॑णाव॒श्विना॒ शं ।  
> शं नः॑ सु॒कृतां॑ सुकृ॒तानि॑ संतु॒ शं न॑ इषि॒रो अ॒भि वा॑तु॒ वातः॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । सुकृतां । सुकृतानि । संतु । शं । नः । इषिरः । अभि । वातु । वातः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ज्योतिरनीको ज्योतिर्मुखोऽग्निर्नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । भवतु । मित्रावरुणा मित्रावरुणावपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतां । अश्विनावश्विनावपि शं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, whose face is light, be for our peace. Mitra and Varuṇa also be for our peace. The two Aśvins also be for peace.

### Printed p. 171

> भवतां । सुकृतां पुण्यकर्मणां पुरुषाणां सुकृतानि पुण्यकर्माण्यपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै संतु । भवंतु । इषिरो गमनशीलोऽपि वातो वायुरपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अभि वातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The good deeds of the doers of good, men of meritorious acts, be for our peace. The restless (iṣira) wind also blow around for our peace.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ज्योतिरनीकः (*jyotiranīkaḥ*)—having a face of the nature of light
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be
- मित्रावरुणौ (*mitrāvaruṇau*)—Mitra and Varuṇa also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—let them be for peace
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin deities also
- शं (*śaṃ*)—let them be for our peace
- सुकृतां (*sukṛtāṃ*)—of those who have meritorious deeds
- सुकृतानि (*sukṛtāni*)—the excellent deeds also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- संतु (*saṃtu*)—let them be
- इषिरः (*iṣiraḥ*)—the moving
- वातः (*vātaḥ*)—the wind also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- अभि वातु (*abhi vātu*)—let it blow all around

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Agni, whose face is of the nature of light, be for our peace. May Mitra and Varuṇa also be for our peace. May the Aśvin deities also be givers of peace to us. May the excellent deeds of those of meritorious acts also bring about our peace. May the moving wind also blow all around for our peace.

##### English translation
May Agni, whose countenance is light be (with us) for our happiness: may Mitra and Varuna, may the Aswins be (present) for our felicity : may the virtuous be (promotive of ) our happiness : may the restless wind blow for our happiness.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॒ द्यावा॑पृथि॒वी पू॒र्वहू॑तौ॒ शमं॒तरि॑क्षं दृ॒शये॑ नो अस्तु ।  
> शं न॒ ओष॑धीर्व॒निनो॑ भवंतु॒ शं नो॒ रज॑स॒स्पति॑रस्तु जि॒ष्णुः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । द्यावापृथिवी इति । पूर्वहूतौ । शं । अंतरिक्षं । दृशये । नः । अस्तु ।  
> शं । नः । ओषधीः । वनिनः । भवंतु । शं । नः । रजसः । पतिः । अस्तु । जिष्णुः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै द्यावापृथिवी द्यावापृथिव्यौ पूर्वहूतौ प्रथमाह्वाने भवतां । अंतरिक्षमपि नोऽस्माकं दृशये दर्शनाय शमस्तु । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

For our peace let heaven and earth be present at the first invocation. The firmament also be for our seeing (dṛśaye). For our peace

### Printed p. 172

> ओषधीरोषधयोऽपि भवंतु । वनिनो वृक्षाश्च शं भवंतु । जिष्णुर्जयशीलो रजसो लोकस्य पतिरिंद्रोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

let the herbs also be so, and the trees be for peace. Jiṣṇu, the victorious Indra, lord of the world (rajas), also be for our peace.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- द्यावापृथिवी (*dyāvāpṛthivī*)—heaven and earth
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringers of peace
- पूर्वहूतौ (*pūrvahūtau*)—let them be ready at the very first invocation
- अंतरिक्षं (*aṃtarikṣaṃ*)—the firmament also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- दृशये (*dṛśaye*)—for excellent sight
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as a bringer of happiness
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let it be
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- ओषधीः (*oṣadhīḥ*)—the herbs
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—let them be effective
- वनिनः (*vaninaḥ*)—let the trees also be effective for our peace
- जिष्णुः (*jiṣṇuḥ*)—the victorious
- रजसः (*rajasaḥ*)—of the whole world

- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let him be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May heaven and earth, as bringers of our peace, be ready at the very first invocation. May the firmament also be a source of happiness for our excellent sight. May the herbs and trees be bringers of our peace. May Indra, the victorious lord of the whole world, be the establisher of our peace.

##### English translation
May heaven and earth, the first invoked, (promote) our happiness : may the firmament be happiness to our view : may the herbs, the trees, (yield) us happiness : may the victorious lord of the world, (Indra), be (favourable to) our felicity.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं न॒ इंद्रो॒ वसु॑भिर्दे॒वो अ॑स्तु॒ शमा॑दि॒त्येभि॒र्वरु॑णः सु॒शंसः॑ ।  
> शं नो॑ रु॒द्रो रु॒द्रेभि॒र्जला॑षः॒ शं न॒स्त्वष्टा॒ ग्नाभि॑रि॒ह शृ॑णोतु

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । इंद्रः । वसुभिः । देवः । अस्तु । शं । आदित्येभिः । वरुणः । सुशंसः ।  
> शं । नः । रुद्रः । रुद्रेभिः । जलाषः । शं । नः । त्वष्टा । ग्नाभिः । इह । शृणोतु ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवो द्योतनादिगुणयुक्त इंद्रो वसुभिर्देवैः सार्धं नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतु । सुशंसः शोभनस्तुतिर्वरुणो देव आदित्येभिरादित्यैर्देवैः सार्धं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indra, the god endowed with the quality of shining and the like, be for our peace together with the Vasus, the gods. Varuṇa, the well-praised (suśaṃsa), together with the Ādityas, the gods,

### Printed p. 173

> शं शांत्या अस्तु । भवतु । जलाषो रुद्रो दुःखद्रावको देवो रुद्रेभी रुद्रैः सार्धं शं शांत्यै नोऽस्माकं भवतु । इह यज्ञे त्वष्टा देवो ग्नाभिर्देवपत्नीभिः सार्धं नः शं शांत्यै भवतु । इह यज्ञे नः स्तोत्रं शृणोतु च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

be for peace. Jalāṣa Rudra, the god who removes sorrow, together with the Rudras, be for our peace. Here at the sacrifice let the god Tvaṣṭṛ, together with the Gnās, the wives of the gods, be for our peace, and let him hear our praise here at the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवः (*devaḥ*)—of divine nature
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- वसुभिः (*vasubhiḥ*)—together with the deities named Vasus
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—let him be a bringer of peace
- सुशंसः (*suśaṃsaḥ*)—worthy of excellent praise
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- आदित्येभिः (*ādityebhiḥ*)—together with the Ādityas
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringer of our peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let him be
- जलाषः (*jalāṣaḥ*)—destroyer of sorrow
- रुद्रः (*rudraḥ*)—Rudra
- रुद्रेभिः (*rudrebhiḥ*)—together with the Rudras
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—let him be a bringer of peace
- इह (*iha*)—in this sacrifice
- त्वष्टा (*tvaṣṭā*)—Tvaṣṭṛ
- ग्नाभिः (*gnābhiḥ*)—together with the wives of the gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringer of peace
- शृणोतु (*śṛṇotu*)—let him hear our praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Indra, of divine nature, together with the deities named Vasus, be a bringer of peace to us. May Varuṇa, worthy of excellent praise, together with the Ādityas, be a bringer of our peace. May Rudra, the destroyer of sorrow, together with the Rudras, be a bringer of our peace. In this sacrifice may Tvaṣṭṛ, together with the wives of the gods, be a bringer of our peace, and may he hear our praise.

##### English translation
May the divine (Indra), with the Vasus, grant us happiness : may the justly-praised Varuna with the Adityas, be (friendly to) our happiuess : may the grief-assuaging Rudra, with the Rudras. be (for) our happiness : may Twashtri, with the wives of the gods, be (with us) for our happiness : and hear us at this solemnity.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नः॒ सोमो॑ भवतु॒ ब्रह्म॒ शं नः॒ शं नो॒ ग्रावा॑णः॒ शमु॑ संतु य॒ज्ञाः ।  
> शं नः॒ स्वरू॑णां मि॒तयो॑ भवंतु॒ शं नः॑ प्र॒स्व१॒ः॑ शम्व॑स्तु॒ वेदिः॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । सोमः । भवतु । ब्रह्म । शं । नः । शं । नः । ग्रावाणः । शं । ऊं इति ।  
> संतु । यज्ञाः ।  
> शं । नः । स्वरूणां । मितयः । भवंतु । शं । नः । प्रस्वः । शं । ऊं इति । अस्तु । वेदिः ॥

### Printed p. 174

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै सोमो भवतु । ब्रह्म स्तोत्रमपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतु । ग्रावाणोऽभिषवसाधनभूताः पाषाणा अपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । यज्ञाश्च नः शमु शांत्या एव संतु । स्वरूणां यूपानां मितय उन्मानान्यपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । प्रस्व ओषधयोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । वेदिरपि नः शमु शांत्या एवास्तु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Let Soma be for our peace. The brahma, the praise, also be for our peace. The Grāvans, the stones that are the means of pressing, also be for our peace. Let the sacrifices be for our peace alone. The measures (mitayaḥ) of the Svarus, the sacrificial posts, also be for our peace. The Prasvas, the herbs, also be for our peace. The altar also be for our peace alone.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—let Soma
- भवतु (*bhavatu*)—be offered
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—let the praise also be recited
- ग्रावाणः (*grāvāṇaḥ*)—the pressing stones also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace (let them press the Soma)
- यज्ञाः (*yajñāḥ*)—the sacrifices also
- शमु (*śamu*)—for our peace alone
- संतु (*saṃtu*)—let them be performed
- स्वरूणां (*svarūṇāṃ*)—of the sacrificial posts
- मितयः (*mitayaḥ*)—the measures also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace alone
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—let them be
- प्रस्वः (*prasvaḥ*)—the herbs also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—let them be for peace alone
- वेदिः (*vediḥ*)—the sacrificial altar also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शमु (*śamu*)—for peace alone
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Soma be offered for our peace alone. May the praise also be recited for our peace alone. May the pressing stones press the Soma for our peace. May the sacrifices be performed for our peace alone. May the measures of the sacrificial posts also be for our peace alone. May the herbs also bring about our peace. May the sacrificial altar also produce peace for us.

##### English translation
May the Soma be (offered for) our happiness: may the prayer be (uttered for) our happiness : may the stones (grind the Soma), the sacrifice be (solemnized for) our happiness : may the measured lengths of the sacrificial posts be (conducive to) our felicity : may the sacred grass be (strewn)for our happiness : may the altar be (raised for) our happiness.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नः॒ सूर्य॑ उरु॒चक्षा॒ उदे॑तु॒ शं न॒श्चत॑स्रः प्र॒दिशो॑ भवंतु ।  
> शं नः॒ पर्व॑ता ध्रु॒वयो॑ भवंतु॒ शं नः॒ सिंध॑वः॒ शमु॑ सं॒त्वापः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 175

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । सूर्यः । उरुऽचक्षाः । उत् । एतु । शं । नः । चतस्रः । प्रदिशः । भवंतु ।  
> शं । नः । पर्वताः । ध्रुवयः । भवंतु । शं । नः । सिंधवः । शं । ऊं इति । संतु ।  
> आपः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै सूर्य उरुचक्षा विस्तीर्णतेजाः सन्नुदेतु । उदयं प्राप्नोतु । चतस्रः प्रदिशो महादिशोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै पर्वता ध्रुवयो ध्रुवा भवंतु । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै सिंधवो नद्योऽपि भवंतु । आपश्च नः शमु शांत्या एव संतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

For our peace (शं (*śaṃ*)) may the Sun rise, his radiance spread wide, that is, may he attain his rising. May the four directions, the great quarters, also be for our peace. May the mountains be firm for our peace. May the rivers also be for our peace. May the waters also be for our peace alone.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- सूर्यः (*sūryaḥ*)—the Sun
- उरुचक्षाः (*urucakṣāḥ*)—having radiance spread wide
- उदेतु (*udetu*)—may he rise
- चतस्रः (*catasraḥ*)—four in number
- प्रदिशः (*pradiśaḥ*)—the great quarters
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringers of peace
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—may they be
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- पर्वताः (*parvatāḥ*)—the mountains
- ध्रुवयः (*dhruvayaḥ*)—as enduring
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—may they be
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringing peace
- सिंधवः (*siṃdhavaḥ*)—the rivers (may they flow)
- आपः (*āpaḥ*)—the waters
- शमु (*śamu*)—for our peace alone
- संतु (*saṃtu*)—may they flow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

For the gaining of our peace, may the Sun rise with his radiance spread wide. May all the great quarters be bringers of our peace. May the mountains also attain a lasting state that gives us peace. May the rivers and the waters flow so as to give us peace.

##### English translation
May the wide-seeing sun rise (for) our happiness : may the four quarters of the horizon (exist for) our felicity : may the firm-set mountains be (for) our happiness : may the rivers, may the waters, be (diffused) for our happiness.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॒ अदि॑तिर्भवतु व्र॒तेभिः॒ शं नो॑ भवंतु म॒रुतः॑ स्व॒र्काः ।  
> शं नो॒ विष्णुः॒ शमु॑ पू॒षा नो॑ अस्तु॒ शं नो॑ भ॒वित्रं॒ शम्व॑स्तु वा॒युः ॥ ९ ॥

### Printed p. 176

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । अदितिः । भवतु । व्रतेभिः । शं । नः । भवंतु । मरुतः । सुऽअर्काः ।  
> शं । नः । विष्णुः । शं । ऊं इति । पूषा । नः । अस्तु । शं । नः । भवित्रं । शं । ऊं इति । अस्तु । वायुः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अदितिर्देवी व्रतेभिर्व्रतैः कर्मभिः सार्धं नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतु । स्वर्काः शोभनस्तुतयो मरुतोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै संतु । विष्णुर्व्यापको नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । पूषा देवोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शमु शांत्या एवास्तु । भवित्रं भुवनमंतरिक्षमुदकं वा नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । वायुरपि नः शमु शांत्या एवास्तु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The goddess Aditi, together with her observances (व्रतेभिः (*vratebhiḥ*)), that is, her rites, be for our peace. The Maruts, of fair praise (स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*)), be also for our peace. Viṣṇu, the all-pervading, be for our peace. The god Pūṣan be for our peace alone. भवित्रं (*bhavitraṃ*) is the world, the atmosphere or water; may it be for our peace. Vāyu too be for our peace alone.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अदितिः (*aditiḥ*)—Aditi, who is of divine nature
- व्रतेभिः (*vratebhiḥ*)—with her own rites
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringer of peace
- भवतु (*bhavatu*)—may she be
- स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*)—the Maruts, worthy of excellent praise
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Maruts
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be bringers of peace
- विष्णुः (*viṣṇuḥ*)—Viṣṇu
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may he be a giver of peace
- पूषा (*pūṣā*)—the god Pūṣan
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शमु (*śamu*)—as a giver of peace alone
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be
- भवित्रं (*bhavitraṃ*)—the primal principle of beings
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may it be a bringer of peace
- वायुः (*vāyuḥ*)—Vāyu also
- शमु (*śamu*)—for peace alone
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Aditi, of divine nature, with her rites bring us peace. May the Maruts, worthy of excellent praise, be bringers of our peace. May Viṣṇu be a giver of peace to us. May the god Pūṣan be a bringer of peace to us. May the primal principle of beings produce our peace. May Vāyu be for our peace.

##### English translation
May Aditi, with holy observances, be (for) our happiness : may the glorified Maruts be (friendly to) our felicity ; may Vishnu, may Pushan, be (promoters of) our happiness : may the firmament be propitious to us ; may Vayu (blow for) our happiness.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॑ दे॒वः स॑वि॒ता त्राय॑माणः॒ शं नो॑ भवंत्वू॒षसो॑ विभा॒तीः ।

### Printed p. 177

> शं नः पर्जन्यो भवतु प्रजाभ्यः शं नः क्षेत्रस्य पतिरस्तु शंभुः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । देवः । सविता । त्रायमाणः । शं । नः । भवंतु । उषसः । विऽभातीः ।  
> शं । नः । पर्जन्यः । भवतु । प्रऽजाभ्यः । शं । नः । क्षेत्रस्य । पतिः । अस्तु । शंऽभुः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवः क्रीडनादिगुणयुक्तः सविता त्रायमाणो रक्षन्नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतु । विभातीर्व्यच्छंत्य उषसोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । नोऽस्माकं प्रजाभ्यः पर्जन्योऽपि शं भवतु । शंभुः सुखस्य भावयिता क्षेत्रस्य पतिर्नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The god Savitṛ, endowed with qualities such as playfulness, protecting (त्रायमाणः (*trāyamāṇaḥ*)) us, be for our peace. The shining (विभातीः (*vibhātīḥ*)) Dawns, as they break forth, be also for our peace. Parjanya also be peace for our offspring. Śaṃbhu, the producer of happiness, the lord of the field, be for our peace.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवः (*devaḥ*)—of divine nature
- सविता (*savitā*)—Savitṛ
- त्रायमाणः (*trāyamāṇaḥ*)—protecting
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may he be a bringer of peace
- विभातीः (*vibhātīḥ*)—radiant
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the Dawns also
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—may they be
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- प्रजाभ्यः (*prajābhyaḥ*)—for the offspring
- पर्जन्यः (*parjanyaḥ*)—Parjanya
- शं भवतु (*śaṃ bhavatu*)—may he be a giver of peace
- शंभुः (*śaṃbhuḥ*)—the producer of happiness
- क्षेत्रस्य पतिः (*kṣetrasya patiḥ*)—the lord of the field
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the god Savitṛ, protecting us, be a bringer of our peace. May the radiant Dawns also be for our peace. May Parjanya be a giver of peace to our offspring. May the lord of the field, the producer of happiness, be for our peace.

##### English translation
May the divine preserving Savitri be (radiant for) our happiness : may the opening dawns (break for) our happiness : may Parjanya be (the granter of happiness) to our posterity : may Shambhu, the lord of strength, be (the conferrer of) happiness upon us.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॑ दे॒वा वि॒श्वदे॑वा भवंतु॒ शं सर॑स्वती स॒ह धी॒भिर॑स्तु ।

### Printed p. 178

> शमुभिषाचः शमु रातिषाचः शं नो दिव्याः पार्थिवाः शं नो अप्याः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । देवाः । विश्वऽदेवाः । भवंतु । शं । सरस्वती । सह । धीभिः । अस्तु ।  
> शं । अभिऽसाचः । शं । ऊं इति । रातिऽसाचः । शं । नः । दिव्याः । पार्थिवाः । शं । नः । अप्याः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विश्वदेवा बहुस्तोत्रका देवा नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । सरस्वती च धीभिः स्तुतिभिः कर्मभिर्वा सह नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । अभिषाचो यज्ञमभितः सेवमानाश्च नः शं शांत्यै भवंतु । रातिषाचो दानं सेवमाना अपि शमु शांत्या एव भवंतु । दिव्या दिवि भवाश्च नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । पार्थिवाः पृथिव्यां संभूताश्च नः शं भवंतु । अप्या अप्स्वंतरिक्षे भवाश्च । आकाशमाप इत्यंतरिक्षनामसु पाठात् । नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Viśvedevas, the gods of many praisers, be for our peace. Sarasvatī, with the thoughts (धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*)), that is, praises or rites, be for our peace. The अभिषाचो (*abhiṣāco*), who attend the sacrifice on every side, be for our peace. The रातिषाचः (*rātiṣācaḥ*), who attend upon giving, be for our peace alone. The celestial ones, those born in heaven, be for our peace. The terrestrial ones, born on the earth, be for our peace. The अप्याः (*apyāḥ*), those of the waters in the atmosphere, be for our peace; आकाशमाप इत्यंतरिक्षनामसु पाठात् (*ākāśamāpa ityaṃtarikṣanāmasu pāṭhāt*) (since आपः (*āpaḥ*) is read among the names of the atmosphere).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वदेवाः (*viśvadevāḥ*)—worthy of many praises
- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—the gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringers of peace
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—may they be
- सरस्वती (*sarasvatī*)—Sarasvatī
- धीभिः सह (*dhībhiḥ saha*)—with her own rites
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for our peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may she favour
- अभिसाचः (*abhisācaḥ*)—the helpers in the sacrifice
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be for our peace
- रातिषाचः (*rātiṣācaḥ*)—those worthy of giving
- शमु (*śamu*)—may they be for our happiness
- दिव्याः (*divyāḥ*)—those born in the heavenly world
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be for peace
- पार्थिवाः (*pārthivāḥ*)—those born of the earth, may they be bringers of happiness to us
- अप्याः (*apyāḥ*)—the gods of the atmosphere
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they produce happiness

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the gods, worthy of many praises, be bringers of our peace. May Sarasvatī with her rites show favour for our peace. May the helpers in the sacrifice be for our peace. May those worthy of giving be bringers of our happiness. May the gods born in heaven, on the earth and in the atmosphere produce happiness for us.

##### English translation
May the devine universal gods be (favourable) to our felicity : may Saraswati, with holy rites, be happiness : may those who assist at sacrifices, those who are liberal of gifts, be (conducive to) our happiness : may celestial, terrestrial, and acquatic things be (subservient to) our happiness.

### Printed p. 179

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नः॑ स॒त्यस्य॒ पत॑यो भवंतु॒ शं नो॒ अर्वं॑तः॒ शमु॑ संतु॒ गावः॑ ।  
> शं न॑ ऋ॒भवः॑ सु॒कृतः॑ सु॒हस्ताः॒ शं नो॑ भवंतु पि॒तरो॒ हवे॑षु ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । सत्यस्य । पतयः । भवंतु । शं । नः । अर्वंतः । शं । ऊं इति । संतु । गावः ।  
> शं । नः । ऋभवः । सुऽकृतः । सुऽहस्ताः । शं । नः । भवंतु । पितरः । हवेषु ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सत्यस्य पतयः पालकाः सत्यशीला देवा नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । अर्वंतोऽश्वाश्च नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु । गावोऽपि नः शं शांत्यै भवंतु । सुकृतः सुकर्माणः सुहस्ताः शोभनहस्ता ऋभवोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै संतु । हवेषु स्तोत्रेषु सत्सु पितरोऽपि नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The lords of truth, the gods who guard truth and are true in character, be for our peace. The horses (अर्वंतः (*arvaṃtaḥ*)) be for our peace. The cattle also be for our peace. The Ṛbhus, of good works (सुकृतः (*sukṛtaḥ*)) and fair hands (सुहस्ताः (*suhastāḥ*)), be for our peace. At the invocations (हवेषु (*haveṣu*)), that is, when praises are being offered, the Fathers also be for our peace.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सत्यस्य पतयः (*satyasya patayaḥ*)—the gods who guard truth
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं भवंतु (*śaṃ bhavaṃtu*)—may they be for peace
- अर्वंतः (*arvaṃtaḥ*)—the horses
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be for peace
- गावः (*gāvaḥ*)—the cattle
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be for our peace
- सुकृतः (*sukṛtaḥ*)—those of good works
- सुहस्ताः (*suhastāḥ*)—and of fair hands
- ऋभवः (*ṛbhavaḥ*)—the Ṛbhus
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may they be for peace
- हवेषु (*haveṣu*)—at the time of our praise
- पितरः (*pitaraḥ*)—the Fathers
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—may they be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the gods who guard truth be bringers of our peace. May the horses and the cattle be for our peace. May the Ṛbhus, of good works and fair hands, be for our peace. At the time of our praise may the Fathers be for our peace.

##### English translation
May the lords of truth be (propitious to) our happiness : may horses, may cattle, (contribute to) our happiness : may the virtuous, the dexterous Ribhus, be to us (for) felicity : may the Progenitors be (promoters of) our happiness at the seasons of worship.

### Printed p. 180

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॑ अ॒ज एक॑पाद्दे॒वो अ॑स्तु॒ शं नोऽहि॑र्बु॒ध्न्य१॒ः॑ शं स॑मु॒द्रः ।  
> शं नो॑ अ॒पां नपा॑त्पे॒रुर॑स्तु॒ शं नः॒ पृश्नि॑र्भवतु दे॒वगो॑पाः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । अजः । एकऽपात् । देवः । अस्तु । शं । नः । अहिः । बुध्न्यः । शं । समुद्रः ।  
> शं । नः । अपां । नपात् । पेरुः । अस्तु । शं । नः । पृश्निः । भवतु । देवऽगोपाः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अज एकपादज एकपान्नामधेयो देवो नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । अहिर्बुध्न्यश्च नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । समुद्रोऽपि नः शं शांत्या अस्तु । पेरुरुपद्रवेभ्यः पारयितापां नपादपांनपान्नामधेयोऽपि देवो नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्या अस्तु । देवगोपा देवा गोपयितारो यस्यां सा पृश्निर्मरुतां माता नोऽस्माकं शं शांत्यै भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The god named Aja Ekapād be for our peace. Ahirbudhnya also be for our peace. The ocean also be for our peace. पेरुः (*peruḥ*) is the one who carries across from calamities; the god named Apāṃ Napāt also be for our peace. Pṛśni, the mother of the Maruts, in whom the gods are the protectors (देवगोपाः (*devagopāḥ*)), be for our peace.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अज एकपात् (*aja ekapāt*)—named Aja Ekapāt
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the god
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as bringer of peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be
- अहिर्बुध्न्यः (*ahirbudhnyaḥ*)—the god Ahirbudhnya
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may he be a bringer of peace
- समुद्रः (*samudraḥ*)—the ocean
- शं (*śaṃ*)—may it be a bringer of peace
- पेरुः (*peruḥ*)—who rescues from calamities
- अपां नपात् (*apāṃ napāt*)—the god named Apāṃ Napāt
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for peace
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be
- देवगोपाः (*devagopāḥ*)—who protects the gods
- पृश्निः (*pṛśniḥ*)—Pṛśni
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—as a giver of happiness
- भवतु (*bhavatu*)—may she be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the god named Aja Ekapāt be a bringer of peace to us. May the god Ahirbudhnya be a bringer of peace to us. May the ocean also be a bringer of peace to us. May the god named Apāṃ Napāt, who rescues from calamities, be for our peace. May Pṛśni, who protects the gods, be a giver of happiness to us.

##### English translation
May the divine Aja-ekapad be (fevourable to) our happiness : May Ahirbudhnya, may the firmament, be (promotive of) our happiness : may the

### Printed p. 181
grandson of the waters, the protector, be (the securer of) our felicity : may Prishni, of whom the gods are the guardians, be to us (granter of) happiness.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ॒दि॒त्या रु॒द्रा वस॑वो जुषंते॒दं ब्रह्म॑ क्रि॒यमा॑णं॒ नवी॑यः ।  
> शृ॒ण्वंतु॑ नो दि॒व्याः पार्थि॑वासो॒ गोजा॑ता उ॒त ये य॒ज्ञिया॑सः ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आदित्याः । रुद्राः । वसवः । जुषंत । इदं । ब्रह्म । क्रियमाणं । नवीयः ।  
> शृण्वंतु । नः । दिव्याः । पार्थिवासः । गोऽजाताः । उत । ये । यज्ञियासः ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नवीयो नवतरमस्माभिः क्रियमाणमिदं ब्रह्म स्तोत्रमादित्या दिव्याः । अदितिर्द्यौः । ऋ. १-८९-१० । इति श्रुतेः । रुद्रा अंतरिक्षा वसवः पार्थिवाश्च जुषंते । जुषंतां । सेवंतां । अन्ये दिव्या दिवि भवाः पार्थिवासः पार्थिवा गोजाता गोः पृश्नेर्जाताः । नाको गौरिति साधारणनामसु पाठात् । उतापि च ये यज्ञियासो यज्ञार्हाः ते सर्वेऽपि नोऽस्माकं हवं शृण्वंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The new praise (brahma) now being made by us is to be enjoyed by the Ādityas, the celestial ones; the Śruti says "Aditi is the sky" (Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-89-10). The Rudras, of the atmosphere, and the Vasus, of the earth, are to enjoy and serve it. The others, the celestial and the terrestrial, and those "born of the cow", that is, of Pṛśni (since *nāko gauḥ* is read among the common names), and all who are worthy of sacrifice (yajñiya), are to hear our invocation.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- क्रियमाणं (*kriyamāṇaṃ*)—made by us
- नवीयः (*navīyaḥ*)—and new
- इदं ब्रह्म (*idaṃ brahma*)—this praise
- आदित्याः (*ādityāḥ*)—the Ādityas, born in the heavenly world
- रुद्राः (*rudrāḥ*)—the Rudras, dwelling in the atmosphere
- वसवः (*vasavaḥ*)—the Vasus, dwelling on the earth
- जुषंत (*juṣaṃta*)—may they enjoy
- दिव्याः (*divyāḥ*)—those born in the heavenly world
- पार्थिवासः (*pārthivāsaḥ*)—those dwelling on the earth
- गोजाताः (*gojātāḥ*)—the sons of Pṛśni
- उत (*uta*)—and
- ये (*ye*)—who
- यज्ञियासः (*yajñiyāsaḥ*)—those worthy of sacrifice, all of them
- नः (*naḥ*)—our praise
- शृण्वंतु (*śṛṇvaṃtu*)—may they hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the Ādityas born in the heavenly world, the Rudras of the atmosphere and the Vasus of the earth enjoy this new praise made by us. May those born in the heavenly world, those of the earth, the sons of Pṛśni, and all who are worthy of sacrifice hear our praise.

### Printed p. 182

##### English translation
May the Adityas, the Rudras, the Vasus, be gratified by this new and now repeated praise : may celestial and terrestrial (beings), the progeny of the cow, (Prishni), and those who were entitled to worship, hear our (invocations).

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ये दे॒वानां॑ य॒ज्ञिया॑ य॒ज्ञिया॑नां॒ मनो॒र्यज॑त्रा अ॒मृता॑ ऋत॒ज्ञाः ।  
> ते नो॑ रासंतामुरुगा॒यम॒द्य यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ये । देवानां । यज्ञियाः । यज्ञियानां । मनोः । यजत्राः । अमृताः । ऋतऽज्ञाः ।  
> ते । नः । रासंतां । उरुऽगायं । अद्य । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिऽभिः । सदा । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यज्ञियानां यजनीयानां देवानामपि यज्ञिया यजनीया मनोः प्रजापतेश्च यजत्रा यजनीया अमृता मरणरहिता ऋतज्ञाः सत्यज्ञा ये देवाः संति ते सर्व उरुगायं बहुकीर्तिं पुत्रमद्य नोऽस्मभ्यं रासंतां । प्रयच्छंतु । सिद्ध एवोत्तमः पादः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Those gods who are worthy of worship even among the worshipful gods, who are worthy of worship also for Manu, that is Prajāpati, who are immortal and know the truth (ṛta), let all of them give us today a son of wide fame. रासंतां (*rāsaṃtāṃ*) means "let them give". The last pāda is self-evident.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यज्ञियानां (*yajñiyānāṃ*)—of those worthy of sacrifice
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—among the gods
- यज्ञियो (*yajñiyo*)—those who are worthy of worship
- मनोः (*manoḥ*)—for Prajāpati
- यजत्राः (*yajatrāḥ*)—the worshipful
- अमृताः (*amṛtāḥ*)—those free from the nature of death
- ऋतज्ञाः (*ṛtajñāḥ*)—knowers of truth
- ये (*ye*)—whatever gods there are
- ते (*te*)—all those gods
- उरुगायं (*urugāyaṃ*)—a son of great fame
- अद्य (*adya*)—now
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- रासंतां (*rāsaṃtāṃ*)—may they give
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you (all the gods)
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

### Printed p. 183

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Whatever gods there are who are worthy of worship even for the gods worthy of sacrifice, worthy of worship for Prajāpati, free from mortality and knowers of truth, may all those gods now give us a son worthy of great fame. O all you gods, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
May those who are the most adorable of the adorable divinitievs, those who were the adored of Manu, those who are immortal, the observers of truth, bestow upon us this day (a son) of widely-spread renown : and do you ever cherish us with blessings.

Closing verses: वेदार्थस्य प्रकाशेन तमो हार्दं निवारयन् । पुमर्थांश्चतुरो देयाद्विद्यातीर्थमहेश्वरः ॥ (*vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan | pumarthāṃścaturo deyādvidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ ‖*) Thus, in the Mādhavīya Vedārthaprakāśa, the commentary on the Ṛk-Saṃhitā composed by Sāyaṇācārya, bearer of the burden of the empire of the illustrious Vīra Bukka Bhūpāla, promoter of the Vedic path, supreme lord over kings, the third chapter of the fifth Aṣṭaka is concluded. ओं तत्सत् (*oṃ tatsat*). Here ends the third chapter in the fifth Aṣṭaka of the Ṛgveda Saṃhitā, with Sāyaṇa's commentary and Kannada translation.
यदक्षरपदभ्रष्टं मात्राहीनं तु यद्भवेत् । तत्सर्वं क्षम्यतां देव वागीश्वर नमोऽस्तु ते ॥ (*yadakṣarapadabhraṣṭaṃ mātrāhīnaṃ tu yadbhavet | tatsarvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ deva vāgīśvara namo 'stu te ‖*) May it be auspicious. Blessings.

Ṛgveda Saṃhitā with Sāyaṇa's commentary (with Kannada translation and explanation). The fourth chapter in the fifth Aṣṭaka. Seventh Maṇḍala. The hymns of the seventh Maṇḍala. The thirty-sixth hymn.

## Sūkta 36 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अथ पंचमाष्टके चतुर्थोऽध्याय आरभ्यते । तत्र षडनुवाकात्मकस्य वासिष्ठस्य सप्तमस्य मंडलस्य तृतीयेऽनुवाके द्वाविंशति सूक्तानि । तत्र प्र ब्रह्मेति नवर्चं तृतीयं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं पूर्ववद्वैश्वदेवं । अनुक्रांतं
> च । प्र ब्रह्म नवेति ॥ विनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The fourth chapter of the fifth Aṣṭaka now begins. In the seventh, Vāsiṣṭha Maṇḍala, which consists of six anuvākas, the third anuvāka has twenty-two hymns. Among them the third hymn, प्र ब्रह्म (*pra brahma*), of nine Ṛks, has Vasiṣṭha as seer, the Viśvedevas as deity, and the Triṣṭubh metre, as before. The Anukramaṇī says प्र ब्रह्म नव (*pra brahma nava*). The use (viniyoga) is according to the linga.

##### Anuvāda

The explanation of the fourth chapter of the fifth Aṣṭaka is now begun. The seventh Maṇḍala of the Ṛgveda Saṃhitā has Vasiṣṭha as seer. This Maṇḍala has six anuvākas. Of these, the first two anuvākas, and the first two hymns of the third anuvāka, which has twenty-two hymns, have been explained in the previous chapter. Now this hymn beginning प्र ब्रह्म (*pra brahma*)

### Printed p. 186
is the third hymn in the third anuvāka. It has nine Ṛks. Its seer is Vasiṣṭha himself, the seer of the Maṇḍala; the deity is the Viśvedevas; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as प्र ब्रह्म नव (*pra brahma nava*). The use is according to the linga.

Hymn 36. Maṇḍala 7, Anuvāka 3, Sūkta 36. Aṣṭaka 5, Adhyāya 4, Varga 1, 2. Number of Ṛks in the hymn: 9. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Viśvedevas. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र ब्रह्मै॑तु॒ सद॑नादृ॒तस्य॒ वि र॒श्मिभिः॑ ससृजे॒ सूर्यो॒ गाः ।  
> वि सानु॑ना पृथि॒वी स॑स्र उ॒र्वी पृ॒थु प्रती॑क॒मध्येधे॑ अ॒ग्निः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । ब्रह्म । एतु । सदनात् । ऋतस्य । वि । रश्मिऽभिः । ससृजे । सूर्यः । गाः ।  
> वि । सानुना । पृथिवी । सस्रे । उर्वी । पृथु । प्रतीकं । अधि । आ । ईधे । अग्निः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋतस्य यज्ञस्य सदनात् स्थानाद्देवयजनदेशाद्ब्रह्म स्तोत्रं स्तुत्यान् सूर्यादीन्प्रैतु । प्रकर्षेण गच्छतु । किं तद्ब्रह्मेति तदाह । सूर्यः सर्वस्य प्रेरकः शोभनवीर्यो वा देवो रश्मिभिरात्मीयैः किरणैर्गा अपो वृष्ट्युदकानि वि ससृजे । विसृजति । विमुंचति । प्रवर्षति । श्रूयते हि । याभिरादित्यस्तपति रश्मिभिस्ताभिः पर्जन्यो वर्षतीति । स्मृतिश्च भवति । आदित्याज्जायते वृष्टिर्वृष्टेरन्नं ततः प्रजा इति । अत ईदृशं माहात्म्यं सूर्यस्यैव विद्यते नान्यस्य चिदित्यनेन पादेन सूर्यः स्तूयते । अपि च पृथिवी प्रथिता भूमिः सानुना समुच्छ्रितेन पर्वतादिनोर्वी विस्तीर्णा सती वि सस्रे । विसरति । व्याप्नोति । तथाग्निः पृथु विस्तीर्णं प्रतीकं पृथिव्या अवयवं देवयजनलक्षणं स्थानमधि ॥ अधिपरी अनर्थकावित्यधेः कर्मप्रवचनीयसंज्ञायां कर्मप्रवचनीययुक्त इति सप्तम्यर्थे द्वितीया ॥ ईदृशे स्थान ईधे । आदीप्यते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

From the seat of ṛta, the sacrifice, that is the place of the gods' sacrifice, let the praise (brahma) go forth to the praiseworthy Sūrya and the others. What is that praise? Sūrya, the impeller of all, or the god of fair vigour, sends out with his own rays the "cows", that is the waters, the rain-waters; he lets them loose, he rains. It is heard: "By the rays with which Āditya heats, by those Parjanya rains." And a Smṛti says: "From Āditya arises rain, from rain food, from that creatures." Such greatness belongs to Sūrya alone, to no other; with this pāda Sūrya is praised. Moreover, Pṛthivī, the broad earth, spreads wide with her uplifted ridge, the mountain and the like, pervading. So Agni, on the broad "front" of the earth, a part of it, the place marked as the site of divine worship, is kindled there. अधि (*adhi*) is a karmapravacanīya, and the second case stands in the sense of the seventh. In such a place he is kindled, blazes up.

### Printed p. 187

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice
- सदनात् (*sadanāt*)—from the place
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—the hymn of praise
- प्र एतु (*pra etu*)—let it go towards the gods
- सूर्यः (*sūryaḥ*)—Sūrya, the impeller of all
- रश्मिभिः (*raśmibhiḥ*)—with his rays
- गाः (*gāḥ*)—the waters
- वि ससृजे (*vi sasṛje*)—he makes them pour down
- पृथिवी (*pṛthivī*)—the earth
- सानुना (*sānunā*)—with her lofty mountains and the like
- उर्वी (*urvī*)—grown wide
- वि सस्रे (*vi sasre*)—she spreads
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- पृथु (*pṛthu*)—grown wide
- प्रतीकं अधि (*pratīkaṃ adhi*)—in the sacrificial region of the earth
- ईधे (*īdhe*)—he shines

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let the hymn of praise go from the place of sacrifice towards the gods. Sūrya, the impeller of all, makes the waters pour down with his rays. The earth, grown wide with her lofty mountains and the like, spreads. Agni, grown strong in the sacrificial region, shines.

##### English translation
Let the prayer proceed from the hall of the sacrifice, for Surya with his rays lets loose the waters : the spacious earth spreads (studded) with mountains, and Agni blazes on the extensive plains.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the first half of this Ṛk Sūrya is praised. In the sentence वि रश्मिभिः ससृजे सूर्यो गाः (*vi raśmibhiḥ sasṛje sūryo gāḥ*) it is said that Sūrya, the impeller of all, pours rain-water on the earth through his rays. What is the connection between Sūrya and rain? According to the Śruti sentence याभिरादित्यस्तपति रश्मिभिस्ताभिः पर्जन्यो वर्षति (*yābhirādityastapati raśmibhistābhiḥ parjanyo varṣati*) (Tai. Ā. 10-63), Sūrya, shining with his rays, draws up through them the water in the sea and other reservoirs. That water turns into vapour and becomes clouds in the atmosphere. Parjanya (the deity presiding over rain) pours the water in those clouds on the earth as rain. Since Sūrya's rays are the cause of the sea's waters becoming clouds in the form of vapour, that very matter is described in the present Ṛk. Besides, the Smṛti sentence also says आदित्याज्जायते वृष्टिः वृष्टेरन्नं ततः प्रजाः (*ādityājjāyate vṛṣṭiḥ vṛṣṭerannaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ*): from the Āditya comes rain, from rain food and the like, and from these living beings are born. Modern science too says that Sūrya's rays are the cause of rain arising on the earth.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒मां वां॑ मित्रावरुणा सुवृ॒क्तिमिषं॒ न कृ॑ण्वे असुरा॒ नवी॑यः ।

### Printed p. 188

> इनो वामन्यः पदवीरदब्धो जनं च मित्रो यतति ब्रुवाणः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इमां । वां । मित्रावरुणा । सुऽवृक्तिं । इषं । न । कृण्वे । असुरा । नवीयः ।  
> इनः । वां । अन्यः । पदऽवीः । अदब्धः । जनं । च । मित्रः । यतति । ब्रुवाणः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे असुरासुरौ बलवंतौ हे मित्रावरुणा मित्रावरुणौ वां युवाभ्यामिषं न हविरूपमन्नमिव नवीयो नवीयसीमिमामस्मदीयां पुरोवर्तिनीं सुवृक्तिं स्तुतिं कृण्वे । अहं स्तोता करोमि । वां युवयोरन्योऽन्यतर इनः प्रभुरदब्धः शत्रुभिरहिंसितो वरुणः पदवीः पदस्य स्थानस्य प्रजनयिता । वरुणो हि धर्माधर्मयोर्धारयितेति पदवीरित्युच्यते । ब्रुवाणोऽस्माभिः स्तूयमानो मित्रश्च जनं सर्वं प्राणिजातं यतति । यातयति । प्रवर्तयति । तथा च श्रूयते । मित्रो जनान्यातयति ब्रुवाणः । ऋ. ३-५९-१ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Mitra and Varuṇa, you powerful ones, I, the singer, make for you this new, excellent, present praise of ours, like food in the form of an oblation. Of you two, the other one, Varuṇa, is the lord, unharmed by enemies, and the begetter of the 'path' (पदवीः (*padavīḥ*)), the place; Varuṇa is called पदवीः (*padavīḥ*) because he upholds righteousness and unrighteousness. Mitra, being praised by us, urges all living beings to action (यतति (*yatati*) = he causes to proceed)." So it is heard: मित्रो जनान्यातयति ब्रुवाणः (*mitro janānyātayati bruvāṇaḥ*) (Ṛ. 3-59-1).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- असुरा (*asurā*)—you who are possessed of power
- मित्रावरुणा (*mitrāvaruṇā*)—O deities Mitra and Varuṇa
- वां (*vāṃ*)—addressing you
- इषं न (*iṣaṃ na*)—as one prepares an oblation
- नवीयः (*navīyaḥ*)—new
- इमां सुवृक्तिं (*imāṃ suvṛktiṃ*)—this excellent praise
- कृण्वे (*kṛṇve*)—I compose
- वां (*vāṃ*)—of you two
- अन्यः (*anyaḥ*)—one, Varuṇa
- इनः (*inaḥ*)—the lord of the world
- अदब्धः (*adabdhaḥ*)—unobstructed
- पदवीः (*padavīḥ*)—the upholder of righteousness and unrighteousness
- ब्रुवाणः (*bruvāṇaḥ*)—praised by us
- मित्रः च (*mitraḥ ca*)—Mitra also
- जनं (*janaṃ*)—all classes of living beings
- यतति (*yatati*)—he makes them active

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O powerful Mitra and Varuṇa, as one prepares an oblation, I compose for you this excellent and new praise. Of you two, Varuṇa is the lord of the world, unobstructed, and the upholder of righteousness and unrighteousness. Mitra also, praised by us, makes all classes of living beings engage in action.

##### English translation
Powerful Mitra and Varuna, to you I offer this new praise as if it were (sacrificial) food : one of you, (Varuna), the invincible lord, is the guide to the path (of virtue) : Mitra, when praised, animates men to exertion.

### Printed p. 189

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

पदवीः—पदस्य स्थानस्य प्रजनयिता । वरुणो हि धर्माधर्मयोर्धारयि-तेति पदवीरित्युच्यते । (*padavīḥ—padasya sthānasya prajanayitā | varuṇo hi dharmādharmayordhārayi-teti padavīrityucyate |*) पदवी (*padavī*) means a place. Varuṇa is the one who ordains that living beings give up unrighteousness and walk the path of righteousness. This is why many places speak of वरुणस्य व्रतानि (*varuṇasya vratāni*). That is, the various creatures and men have many duties to perform; it is Varuṇa's work to see that they do these properly and keep those rules. Because Varuṇa holds this authority, it is expressed here by the word पदवी (*padavī*).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ वात॑स्य॒ ध्रज॑तो रंत इ॒त्या अपी॑पयंत धे॒नवो॒ न सूदाः॑ ।  
> म॒हो दि॒वः सद॑ने॒ जाय॑मा॒नोऽचि॑क्रदद्वृष॒भः सस्मि॒न्नूध॑न् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । वातस्य । ध्रजतः । रंते । इत्याः । अपीपयंत । धेनवः । न । सूदाः ।  
> महः । दिवः । सदने । जायमानः । अचिक्रदत् । वृषभः । सस्मिन् । ऊधन् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ध्रजतो गच्छतो वातस्य वायोरित्या गतय आ रंते । अभितो रमंते । तथा सूदाः ॥ सूद प्रेरण इति धातुः ॥ क्षीरस्य प्रेरयित्र्यो धेनवो न । नेति चार्थे । गावश्चापीपयंत । प्यायंते । विधंते । अपि च महो महतो दिवो द्योतमानस्यादित्यस्य सदने स्थानेंतरिक्षे जायमान उत्पद्यमानो वृषभो वर्षणशीलः पर्जन्यः सस्मिन्नूधन् तस्मिन्नंतरिक्षेऽचिक्रदन् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The movements (इत्या (*ityā*) = goings) of the moving wind sport all around. Likewise the cows (धेनवो (*dhenavo*)), the yielders of milk (सूदाः (*sūdāḥ*), from the root सूद (*sūda*) in the sense of impelling), swell and thrive; here न (*na*) has the sense of "and". Moreover Parjanya, the showerer, born in the dwelling, the atmosphere, of the great, shining Āditya, has roared in that same atmosphere (सस्मिन्नूधन् (*sasminnūdhan*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ध्रजतः (*dhrajataḥ*)—of the moving
- वातस्य (*vātasya*)—of the wind
- इत्याः (*ityāḥ*)—the movements
- आ रंते (*ā raṃte*)—are sportive
- सूदाः (*sūdāḥ*)—milk-giving
- धेनवः न (*dhenavaḥ na*)—the cows also
- अपीपयंत (*apīpayaṃta*)—become well nourished
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—of the great
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the Āditya
- सदने (*sadane*)—in the atmosphere, his place
- जायमानः (*jāyamānaḥ*)—born
- वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*)—Parjanya, the causer of rain
- सस्मिन् ऊधन् (*sasmin ūdhan*)—in that same atmosphere
- अचिक्रदत् (*acikradat*)—makes a sound

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The movements of the wind, which is ever moving by nature, are sportive. The milk-giving cows also become well nourished. Parjanya, the causer of rain, born in the atmosphere, the place of the great Āditya, makes a sound in that same atmosphere.

### Printed p. 190

##### English translation
The movements of the restless wind sport around : the milk-yielding cows are in good condition : the showerer generated in the dwelling of the mighty sun has cried aloud in that his place of abiding, the (firmament).

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> गि॒रा य ए॒ता यु॒नज॒द्धरी॑ त॒ इंद्र॑ प्रि॒या सु॒रथा॑ शूर धा॒यू ।  
> प्र यो म॒न्युं रिरि॑क्षतो मि॒नात्या सु॒क्रतु॑मर्य॒मणं॑ ववृत्यां ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> गिरा । यः । एता । युनजत् । हरी इति । ते । इंद्र । प्रिया । सुरथा । शूर । धायू इति ।  
> प्र । यः । मन्युं । रिरिक्षतः । मिनाति । आ । सुक्रतुं । अर्यमणं । ववृत्यां ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अस्याः पूर्वोऽर्धर्च इंद्रस्तुतिः परोऽर्यम्णः स्तुतिः । हे शूर विक्रांतेंद्र ते तव प्रियौ प्रियौ सुरथा सुष्ठुरंहणौ धायू धारकावेतौ हरी त्वदीयोवश्वौ यो जनो गिरा स्तुतिरूपया वाचा युनजत् रथे युंज्यात् हे इंद्र त्वमस्य यागमायाहीति शेषः । योऽर्यमा रिरिक्षतो हिंसितुमिच्छतः शत्रोः संबंधिनं मन्युं कोपं प्र मिनाति प्रकर्षेण हिनस्ति सुक्रतुं शोभनकर्माणमर्यमणमा ववृत्यां । स्तुत्यावर्तयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The first half of this Ṛk is a praise of Indra, the latter a praise of Aryaman. "O heroic, valiant Indra, these your dear, swift-running, upholding horses—whoever yokes them to the chariot with his song of praise, O Indra, come to that man's sacrifice" (this last is to be supplied). Aryaman, who destroys utterly the wrath of an enemy who wishes to do harm, the doer of good deeds, him I turn hither (आ ववृत्यां (*ā vavṛtyāṃ*)) with praise.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शूर (*śūra*)—valiant
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- प्रिया (*priyā*)—dear
- सुरथा (*surathā*)—moving well
- धायू (*dhāyū*)—upholding
- एता हरी (*etā harī*)—these horses
- यः (*yaḥ*)—whichever sacrificer
- गिरा (*girā*)—with his words in the form of praise
- युनजत् (*yunajat*)—yokes to the chariot—go to his sacrifice
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Aryaman deity
- रिरिक्षतः (*ririkṣataḥ*)—of the enemy who does harm
- मन्युं (*manyuṃ*)—the wrath
- प्र मिनाति (*pra mināti*)—destroys completely, such
- सुक्रतुं (*sukratuṃ*)—of excellent action
- अर्यमणं (*aryamaṇaṃ*)—Aryaman
- आ ववृत्यां (*ā vavṛtyāṃ*)—I worship with praise

### Printed p. 191

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O valiant Indra, go to the sacrifice of that sacrificer who with his words in the form of praise yokes to the chariot these your dear, swift-moving, upholding horses. I worship with praise the deity Aryaman, of excellent action, who completely destroys the wrath of the harmful enemy.

##### English translation
Hero, Indra, (come to the sacrafice of the man) who, by his adoration has harnessed (to your car) these your favourite, graceful, and vigorous horses : may I bring hither Aryaman, the doer of good deeds. who baffles the wrath of the malevolent.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> युजं॑ते अस्य स॒ख्यं वय॑श्च नम॒स्विनः॒ स्व ऋ॒तस्य॒ धाम॑न् ।  
> वि पृक्षो॑ बाबधे॒ नृभिः॒ स्तवा॑न इ॒दं नमो॑ रु॒द्राय॒ प्रेष्यं ॥ ५ ॥॑

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यजंते । अस्य । सख्यं । वयः । च । नमस्विनः । स्वे । ऋतस्य । धामन् ।  
> वि । पृक्षः । बाबधे । नृभिः । स्तवानः । इदं । नमः । रुद्राय । प्रेष्ठं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनया रुद्रः स्तूयते । नमस्विनो हविर्लक्षणान्नवंतः स्वे स्वकीये ऋतस्य यज्ञस्य धामन् धामनि स्थाने । स्वकीये यज्ञगृहे स्थिता इत्यर्थः । वयश्च गंतारः कर्माणि कुर्वाणा यजमाना अस्य रुद्रस्य सख्यं सखित्वमुद्दिश्य युजंते । पूजयंते । नृभिर्नेतृभिः स्तवानः स्तूयमानो रुद्रः पृक्षोऽन्नं स्तोतृषु वि बाबधे । वि बध्नाति । ददातीत्यर्थः । प्रेष्यं रुद्रस्य प्रियतममिदं नमस्तस्मै रुद्राय मया क्रियते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By this Ṛk Rudra is praised. The sacrificers, who possess food in the form of oblations (नमस्विनः (*namasvinaḥ*)) and stand in their own sacrificial house (धामन् (*dhāman*) = place), and who go about performing rites (वयः (*vayaḥ*)), worship Rudra with a view to his friendship. Rudra, praised by the leaders (नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)), binds (वि बाबधे (*vi bābadhe*)), that is, bestows, food (पृक्षः (*pṛkṣaḥ*)) on the singers. This most dear homage (नमः (*namaḥ*)) to Rudra is made by me to that Rudra.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नमस्विनः (*namasvinaḥ*)—those possessing oblations
- स्वे (*sve*)—their own
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice
- धामन् (*dhāman*)—in the place
- वयश्च (*vayaśca*)—and sacrificers engaged in rites
- अस्य (*asya*)—of Rudra
- सख्यं (*sakhyaṃ*)—friendship, with a view to it
- युजंते (*yujaṃte*)—they worship
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the sacrificers
- स्तवानः (*stavānaḥ*)—this Rudra who is praised
- पृक्षः (*pṛkṣaḥ*)—food
- वि बाबधे (*vi bābadhe*)—he fills them with
- प्रेष्ठं (*preṣṭhaṃ*)—most excellent
- इदं नमः (*idaṃ namaḥ*)—this praise
- रुद्राय (*rudrāya*)—is offered to Rudra

### Printed p. 192

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificers, who possess oblations and are engaged in rites each at their own place of sacrifice, worship with a view to Rudra's friendship. This Rudra, praised by the sacrificers, fills them with food. This most excellent praise is offered to Rudra.

##### English translation
Let the offers of adoration, engaging (in pious acts), worship (Rudra) in their own hall of sacrifice, (solicitous) of his friendship : praise by the leaders (of rites), he lavishes food (upon them) : this most acceptable adoration is addressed to Rudra.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ यत्सा॒कं य॒शसो॑ वावशा॒नाः सर॑स्वती स॒प्तथी॒ सिंधु॑माता ।  
> याः सु॒ष्वयं॑त सु॒दुघाः॑ सुधा॒रा अ॒भि स्वेन॒ पय॑सा॒ पीप्या॑नाः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । यत् । साकं । यशसः । वावशानाः । सरस्वती । सप्तथी । सिंधुमाता ।  
> याः । सुस्वयंत । सुदुघाः । सुधाराः । अभि । स्वेन । पयसा । पीप्यानाः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यद्यासां गंगादीनां नदीनां मध्ये सिंधुमातासां मातृभूता सरस्वत्येतदाख्या नदी सप्तथी सप्तमी भवति सुदुघाः कामान् दोग्धुं सुशक्याः सुधाराः शोभनधारोपेताश्च नद्यः सुष्वयंत सुष्वयंते । गतिकर्मैतत् । प्रवहंति । स्वेन स्वकीयेन पयसोदकेनाभि पीप्याना याश्चाभिवर्धयंत्यो यशोऽन्नवत्यो वावशानाः कामयमाना नद्यः साकं युगपदेवा गच्छंतु ॥ आ इत्युपसर्गस्य योग्यक्रियाध्याहारः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Among those rivers, Gaṅgā and the rest, the river named Sarasvatī, mother of the Sindhu, is the seventh. The rivers that are easily milked of desires (सुदुघाः (*sudughāḥ*)) and have fine streams (सुधाराः (*sudhārāḥ*)) flow (सुष्वयंत (*suṣvayaṃta*); this is a verb of motion), swelling with their own water; and those rivers that, increasing, are rich in fame and food and desirous, let them go together at once. The preverb आ (*ā*) requires a suitable verb to be supplied.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत् (*yat*)—among which rivers, Gaṅgā and the rest
- सिंधुमाता (*siṃdhumātā*)—Sarasvatī, like a mother to all waters
- सरस्वती (*sarasvatī*)—the river Sarasvatī
- सप्तथी (*saptathī*)—the seventh, such
- सुदुघाः (*sudughāḥ*)—granting desires
- सुधाराः (*sudhārāḥ*)—having excellent streams, all those rivers
- सुष्वयंत (*suṣvayaṃta*)—flow
- स्वेन (*svena*)—with their own
- पयसा (*payasā*)—water
- अभि पीप्यानाः (*abhi pīpyānāḥ*)—swelling
- याः (*yāḥ*)—

### Printed p. 193
**Pratipadārtha** (continued)
- याव नदिगळु (*yāva nadigaḷu*)—which rivers
- यशसः (*yaśasaḥ*)—endowed with food
- वावशानाः (*vāvaśānāḥ*)—desiring the welfare of the world; those rivers which are so
- साकं (*sākaṃ*)—together
- आ (*ā*) (गच्छंतु (*gacchaṃtu*))—let them flow hither

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Among those rivers, Gaṅgā and the rest, the river Sarasvatī, mother as it were of all waters, is the seventh. Such rivers, granting desired aims and having excellent streams, flow. Those rivers, swollen with their own water, which are endowed with food and desire the welfare of the world, let them flow here together.

##### English translation
May the seventh (stream), Saraswathi, the mother of the Sindhu and those rivers that flow copious and fertilizing, bestowing abundance of food, and nourishing (the people) by their waters, come at once together.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त त्ये नो॑ म॒रुतो॑ मंदसा॒ना धियं॑ तो॒कं च॑ वा॒जिनो॑ऽवंतु ।  
> मा नः॒ परि॑ ख्य॒दक्ष॑रा॒ चरं॒त्यवी॑वृध॒न्युज्यं॒ ते र॒यिं नः॑ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । त्ये । नः । मरुतः । मंदसानाः । धियं । तोकं । च । वाजिनः । अवंतु ।  
> मा । नः । परि । ख्यत् । अक्षरा । चरंती । अवीवृधन् । युज्यं । ते । रयिं । नः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उतापि च मंदसाना मोदमाना वाजिनो वेगवंतस्त्ये ते मरुतो नो
> स्मदीयं धियं यज्ञाख्यं कर्म तोकं चास्मदीयं पुत्रं चावंतु । रक्षंतु ।
> अक्षरा व्याप्ता चरंती वाग्देवता च नोऽस्मान् परि त्यक्त्वास्मद्व्यतिरिक्तानन्यान्मा
> ख्यत् । मा द्राक्षीत् । ते पूर्वोक्ता मरुतो वाक्च युज्यं युक्तमस्मो नोऽस्मदीयं रयिं धनमवीवृधन् । वर्धयंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Maruts, rejoicing and swift, are to protect our sacrifice (धियं (*dhiyaṃ*), the rite called yajña) and our son. The all-pervading goddess Speech (अक्षरा चरंती (*akṣarā caraṃtī*)) is not to abandon us and look upon others, other than us. Let those Maruts and Speech increase our wealth that is fitting for us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उत (*uta*)—and
- मंदसानाः (*maṃdasānāḥ*)—those full of joy
- वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*)—the swift-going
- त्ये (*tye*)—and the famous
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Marut deities
- नः धियं (*naḥ dhiyaṃ*)—our sacrificial rite
- तोकं च (*tokaṃ ca*)—and our sons and the rest
- अवंतु (*avaṃtu*)—let them protect
- अक्षरा (*akṣarā*)—the (imperishable) one
- चरंती (*caraṃtī*)—the all-pervading goddess of speech

### Printed p. 194
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- परि (*pari*)—abandoning
- मा ख्यत् (*mā khyat*)—let her not join others
- ते (*te*)—those Maruts and also the goddess of speech
- युज्यं (*yujyaṃ*)—fit to be with us
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- अवीवृधन् (*avīvṛdhan*)—let them increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let the Marut deities, joyful, swift and famous, protect our sacrificial rite and our sons and the rest. Let the imperishable, all-pervading goddess of speech not abandon us and join others. Let those Maruts and the goddess of speech increase for us wealth fit to be with us.

##### English translation
May these joyous and swift going Maruts protect our sacrifice and our offspring : let not the imperishable goddess of speech, deserting us, speak (kindly) to our (adversaries) : and may both (she and the Maruts) associated augment our riches.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र वो॑ म॒हीम॒रम॑तिं कृणुध्वं॒ प्र पू॒षणं॑ विद॒थ्यं१॒॑ न  ॒
> वीरं ।  
> भगं॑ धि॒यो॑ऽवि॒तारं॑ नो अ॒स्याः सा॒तौ वाजं॑ राति॒षाचं॒ पुरं॑धिं ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । वः । महीं । अरमतिं । कृणुध्वं । प्र । पूषणं । विदथ्यं । न । वीरं ।  
> भगं । धियः । अवितारं । नः । अस्याः । सातौ । वाजं । रातिऽषाचं । पुरंऽधिं ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे स्तोतारः वो यूयमरमतिमरंगतिरहितां महीं महतीं भूमिं
> प्र कृणुध्वं । आह्वयत । तथा विदथ्यं यज्ञार्हं वीरं न सर्वेषां प्रेरकं च
> पूषणमेतन्नामकं देवं प्र कृणुध्वं । तथास्या धियो नोऽस्मदीयस्यास्य कर्मणोऽवितारं रक्षितारं भगं देवं चाह्वयत । अपि च सातावस्मदीये यज्ञे
> युद्धे वा वाजमृभूणामन्यतमं देवमाह्वयत । कीदृशं वाजं । रातिषाचं
> दातृसेवकं पुरंधिं पुराणां धारयितारं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O praisers, invoke the great earth, which is free of अरमति (*aramati*) (lack of movement). Likewise invoke the deity named Pūṣan, worthy of sacrifice, a hero, the impeller of all. Invoke also the god Bhaga, protector of this our rite. Further, in our sacrifice or in battle, invoke Vāja, one of the Ṛbhus. What kind of Vāja? One who attends the giver (रातिषाचं (*rātiṣācaṃ*)), the sustainer of cities (पुरंधिं (*puraṃdhiṃ*)).

### Printed p. 195

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O praisers) वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- अरमतिं (*aramatiṃ*)—the one free of violence
- महीं (*mahīṃ*)—the great Earth goddess
- प्र कृणुध्वं (*pra kṛṇudhvaṃ*)—call with prayer
- विदथ्यं (*vidathyaṃ*)—worthy of sacrifice
- वीरं (*vīraṃ*)—the impeller of the world
- पूषणं न (*pūṣaṇaṃ na*)—Pūṣan also
- प्र (*pra*)—call with prayer
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- अस्याः धियः (*asyāḥ dhiyaḥ*)—of this rite
- अवितारं (*avitāraṃ*)—the protector
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—call the god Bhaga
- सातौ (*sātau*)—in this our sacrifice
- रातिषाचं (*rātiṣācaṃ*)—fond of giving
- पुरंधिं (*puraṃdhiṃ*)—sustainer of cities
- वाजं (*vājaṃ*)—call also Vāja (one of the Ṛbhus)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praisers, call with prayer the Earth goddess, free of violence and great. Call with prayer Pūṣan, worthy of sacrifice and impeller of the world. Call the god Bhaga, protector of this our rite. Call Vāja, fond of giving and sustainer of cities, in this our sacrifice.

##### English translation
Invoke, (worshippers), the nourishing earth, and the adorable hero, Pushan : (invoke) Bhaga, the protector of this our sacrifice and Vaja, the sustainer of old, the liberal of gifts to our solemnity.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अच्छा॒यं वो॑ मरुतः॒ श्लोक॑ ए॒त्वच्छा॒ दिष्णं॑ निषिक्त॒पामवो॑भिः ।  
> उ॒त प्र॒जायै॑ गृण॒ते वयो॑ धुर्यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अच्छ । अयं । वः । मरुतः । श्लोकः । एतु । अच्छ । विष्णुं । निसिक्तपां । अवःऽभिः ।  
> उत । प्रजायै । गृणते । वयः । धुः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यज्ञपुच्छेऽच्छायं व इति चमसिनः स्वं स्वं चमसमभिमृशेयुः । सूत्रितं च । अच्छायं वो मरुतः श्लोक एत्वित्येतयाभिमृशंति । आ. ६-१२ । इति ॥
> हे मरुतः वो युष्मानयं श्लोकोऽस्मदीयमिदं स्तोत्रमच्छैतु । अभिगच्छतु । निषिक्तपां निषिक्तस्य गर्भस्य रक्षितारं । यद्वा । चमसे निषिक्तानां सोमा-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

At the tail of the sacrifice, with अच्छायं वः (*acchāyaṃ vaḥ*) the cup-holders (camasins) are to touch their own cups. It is also taught in the sūtra: अच्छायं वो मरुतः श्लोक एत्वित्येतयाभिमृशंति (*acchāyaṃ vo marutaḥ śloka etvityetayābhimṛśaṃti*) (Ā. 6-12). O Maruts, let this our praise come to you. It is to approach also Viṣṇu, the protector of the embryo that has been poured in (निषिक्तपां (*niṣiktapāṃ*)). Or else: the drinker of the soma poured into the cup, and अवोभिः (*avobhiḥ*)

### Printed p. 196

> नां पातारं अवोभिरस्मद्विषयैरक्षणैर्युक्तं विष्णुं चास्मदीयं स्तोत्रमच्छैतु । उतापि च मरुतो विष्णुश्च गृणते स्तुवते मह्यं प्रजायै पुत्ररूपां प्रजां वयोऽन्नं च धुः । अधुः । दधतु । हे मरुतो हे विष्णो यूयं स्वस्तिभिरविनाशकैर्नोऽस्मान् सदा सर्वदा पात । रक्षत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

(continued) …endowed with protections directed to us. Let our praise approach Viṣṇu too. And let the Maruts and Viṣṇu give to me, the praiser, progeny in the form of sons, and food. O Maruts, O Viṣṇu, always protect us with blessings, which are imperishable.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- वः (*vaḥ*)—to you
- अयं श्लोकः (*ayaṃ ślokaḥ*)—this our praise
- अच्छ एतु (*accha etu*)—let it approach
- निसिक्तपां (*nisiktapāṃ*)—the protector of the embryo
- अवोभिः (*avobhiḥ*)—endowed with protections
- विष्णुं (*viṣṇuṃ*)—Viṣṇu
- अच्छ (*accha*)—let it approach
- उत (*uta*)—and
- गृणते (*gṛṇate*)—to me, the praiser
- प्रजायै (*prajāyai*)—sons and the rest
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food
- धुः (*dhuḥ*)—let them give
- (O Maruts and O Viṣṇu) यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, let this our praise approach you. Let it approach Viṣṇu, protector of the embryo and endowed with protections. Let those deities give sons and the rest and food to me, the praiser. O Maruts and O Viṣṇu, protect us always with your auspicious acts.

##### English translation
May this praise come Maruts, before you : (may it come) before Vishnu, the guardian of the embryo, with his protecting faculties : may they both bestow upon (me), their adorer, progeny and food ; and ever cherish us with blessings.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

At the end of the sacrifice the Ṛtviks, reciting the Ṛk अच्छायं वः (*acchāyaṃ vaḥ*), are to touch their respective camasa vessels; this is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, अच्छायं वो मरुतः श्लोक एत्वित्येतयाभिमृशंति (*acchāyaṃ vo marutaḥ śloka etvityetayābhimṛśaṃti*) (Ā. 6-12).

## Sūkta 37 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The fourth sūkta, आ वो वाहिष्ठ (*ā vo vāhiṣṭha*), of eight Ṛks, is of the seer Vasiṣṭha, has the Viśvedevas as deity and the Triṣṭubh metre. The Anukramaṇī says आ वोऽष्टौ (*ā vo 'ṣṭau*). The use is लैंगिकः (*laiṃgikaḥ*).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta आ वो वाहिष्ठः (*ā vo vāhiṣṭhaḥ*) is the fourth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has eight Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; the Viśvedevas are the deity;

### Printed p. 197
the metre is Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī says आ वोऽष्टौ (*ā vo 'ṣṭau*). The use is लैंगिकः (*laiṃgikaḥ*) (by the indication of the text).

Sūkta 37. Maṇḍala 7. Anuvāka 3. Sūkta 37. Aṣṭaka 5. Adhyāya 4. Vargas 3, 4. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 8. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: the Viśvedevas. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ वो॒ वाहि॑ष्ठो वहतु स्त॒वध्यै॒ रथो॑ वाजा ऋभुक्षणो॒ अमृ॑क्तः ।  
> अ॒भि त्रि॑पृ॒ष्ठैः सव॑नेषु॒ सोम्यै॒र्मदे॑ सुशिप्रा म॒हभिः॑ पृणध्वं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । वः । वाहिष्ठः । वहतु । स्तवध्यै । रथः । वाजाः । ऋभुक्षणः । अमृक्तः ।  
> अभि । त्रिपृष्ठैः । सवनेषु । सोमैः । मदे । सुशिप्राः । महःभिः । पृणध्वं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनेन द्वृचेनर्भवः स्तूयंते । ऋभुक्षणो विस्तीर्णस्य तेजसो निवासभूता हे वाजा ऋभवः वाहिष्ठो वोढृतमः स्तवध्यै स्तोतुमर्होऽमृक्तः केनाप्यहिंसितो युष्मदीयो रथो वो युष्माना वहतु । आ समंतादस्मदीयं यज्ञं प्रापयतु । हे सुशिप्राः शोभनहनवः यूयं तेन रथेनागत्य सवनेष्वस्मदीययज्ञेषु मदे मदनिमित्ते त्रिपृष्ठैः क्षीरदधिसक्तुमिश्रैर्महभिर्महद्भिः सोम्यैरभि पृणध्वं । युष्मदीयं जठरमभिपूरयते । स रथो युष्मानावहतीति पूर्वेणान्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

With this pair of verses the Ṛbhus are praised. O Vājas, Ṛbhus, abode of wide-spread brilliance (ऋभुक्षणः (*ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ*)): let your chariot, best of bearers, worthy of praise, unharmed by anyone, bring you here, to our sacrifice. O fair-jawed ones (सुशिप्राः (*suśiprāḥ*)), coming by that chariot, at our sacrifices fill your bellies, for your exhilaration, with the great soma-offerings mixed with the triple admixture of milk, curds and barley meal (त्रिपृष्ठैः (*tripṛṣṭhaiḥ*)). The construction: that chariot brings you, as in the former sentence.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋभुक्षणः (*ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ*)—O abode of brilliance
- वाजाः (*vājāḥ*)—O Ṛbhus
- वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*)—that can bear well
- स्तवध्यै (*stavadhyai*)—worthy of praise
- अमृक्तः (*amṛktaḥ*)—unharmed
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—your chariot
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- आ वहतु (*ā vahatu*)—let it bring
- सुशिप्राः (*suśiprāḥ*)—O Ṛbhus of excellent cheeks
- सवनेषु (*savaneṣu*)—in our sacrifices
- मदे (*made*)—for your delight
- त्रिपृष्ठैः (*tripṛṣṭhaiḥ*)—accompanied by milk and other substances
- महभिः (*mahabhiḥ*)—of great nature
- सोम्यैः (*somyaiḥ*)—with soma juices
- अभि पृणध्वं (*abhi pṛṇadhvaṃ*)—fill your bellies

### Printed p. 198

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Ṛbhus, abode of brilliance, let your chariot, which can bear you well, is worthy of praise and harmless, bring you here. O Ṛbhus of excellent cheeks, in our sacrifice, for your delight, fill your bellies with the great soma juices accompanied by milk and other substances.

##### English translation
Vajas, possessors of energy, let your capacious, commendable, and unobstructed chariot bring you (hither): be satiated, handsome-chinned, with the copious triply-combined libations (poured out) for your exhilaration at our sacrifices.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk and in the next the Ṛbhus are praised. We have already explained the matter of the Ṛbhus at length before.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यू॒यं ह॒ रत्नं॑ म॒घव॑त्सु धत्थ स्व॒र्दृश॑ ऋभुक्षणो॒ अमृ॑क्तं ।  
> सं य॒ज्ञेषु॑ स्वधावंतः पिबध्वं॒ वि नो॒ राधां॑सि म॒तिभि॑र्दयध्वं ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यूयं । ह । रत्नं । मघवत्सु । धत्थ । स्वःदृशः । ऋभुक्षणः । अमृक्तं ।  
> सं । यज्ञेषु । स्वधावंतः । पिबध्वं । वि । नः । राधांसि । मतिभिः । दयध्वं ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋभुक्षणो हे ऋभवः स्वर्दृशः स्वर्गं पश्यंतो यूयं ह यूयमेव मघवत्सु हविर्लक्षणान्नवत्स्वस्मासु निमित्तेऽस्मभ्यममृक्तमहिंसितं । चोरादिभिर्नापहृतमित्यर्थः ॥ रत्नं रमणीयं दत्थ । धारयथ । तदनंतरं स्वधावंतो बलवंतस्ते यूयं यज्ञेष्वस्मदीययज्ञेषु सं पिबध्वं । सम्यक् सोमं पिबत । अपि च यूयं मतिभिर्धनहेतुभिर्नोऽस्मभ्यं राधांसि धनानि वि दयध्वं । विशेषेण दत्त ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Ṛbhukṣans, Ṛbhus, seeing heaven, you yourselves bestow on us, who have oblations and food (मघवत्सु (*maghavatsu*)), delightful wealth that is अमृक्तं (*amṛktaṃ*), unharmed, that is, not carried off by thieves and others. Thereafter, you, strong (स्वधावंतः (*svadhāvaṃtaḥ*)), drink well the soma at our sacrifices. And with your thoughts (मतिभिः (*matibhiḥ*)), the causes of wealth, distribute wealth (राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*)) specially to us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋभुक्षणः (*ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ*)—O Ṛbhus
- स्वर्दृशः (*svardṛśaḥ*)—who see heaven
- यूयं ह (*yūyaṃ ha*)—you
- मघवत्सु (*maghavatsu*)—for us who have oblations
- अमृक्तं (*amṛktaṃ*)—unharmed
- रत्नं (*ratnaṃ*)—wealth
- धत्थ (*dhattha*)—

### Printed p. 199

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- *dhattha*—give (continued from the preceding word: "give")
- स्वधावंतः (*svadhāvaṃtaḥ*)—you who are endowed with strength
- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—in our sacrifices
- सं पिबध्वं (*saṃ pibadhvaṃ*)—drink the Soma fully
- मतिभिः (*matibhiḥ*)—with your wisdom
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*)—excellent riches
- वि दयध्वं (*vi dayadhvaṃ*)—give abundantly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Ṛbhus, you who behold heaven, give us, who possess the oblation, wealth that is unharmed. You who are endowed with strength, drink Soma fully in our sacrifices. With your wisdom give us excellent wealth in abundance.

##### English translation
For you, Ribhukshins, beholders of heaven, preserve unmolested the precious (treasure) for us who are affluent (in sacrificial offerings) : do you, who are possessed of strength, drink fully at (our) solemnities, and with (favourable) minds bestow upon us riches.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒वोचि॑थ॒ हि म॑घवन्दे॒ष्णं म॒हो अर्भ॑स्य॒ वसु॑नो विभा॒गे ।  
> उ॒भा ते॑ पू॒र्णा वसु॑ना॒ गभ॑स्ती॒ न सू॒नृता॒ नि य॑मते वस॒व्या॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उवोचिथ । हि । मघवन् । देष्णं । महः । अर्भस्य । वसुनः । विभागे ।  
> उभा । ते । पूर्णा । वसुना । गभस्ती इति । न । सूनृता । नि । यमते । वसव्या ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उवोचिथेत्याद्याः पंचर्च इंद्रदेवताकाः । हे मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र त्वं महो महतोऽर्भस्याल्पस्य च वसुनो धनस्य विभागे परिचरणानुकूले दाननिमित्ते देष्णं धनमुवोचिथ हि । सेवसे खलु ॥ उचतिः सेवाकर्मा ॥ तथा ते त्वदीयौ उभौ गभस्ती बाहू वसुना धनेन पूर्णा । संपूर्णौ भवतः । ते त्वदीया सूनृता वाग्वसव्या वसूनि धनानि न नि यमते । न नियच्छति । यद्वा । वसव्या वसुषु धनेषु साधुः सूनृता वाग्वनेन  संपूर्णौ त्वदीयौ बाहू न नियच्छति । नाल्पं प्रदापयतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The five Ṛks beginning उवोचिथ (*uvocitha*) have Indra as deity. O Maghavan, wealthy Indra, in the apportionment of wealth, great or small, you have declared (attended to) wealth fit for giving, conducive to service; उवोचिथ (*uvocitha*) means "you serve", for the root उचति (*ucati*) has the sense of service. Likewise both your arms are full of wealth. Your truthful speech does not hold back riches. Or else: your truthful speech, good in respect of wealth, does not restrain your arms that are full of it; that is, it does not make you give little.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, possessed of wealth
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—of the great
- अर्भस्य (*arbhasya*)—and of the small
- वसुनः (*vasunaḥ*)—of wealth
- विभागे (*vibhāge*)—for the apportionment
- देष्णं (*deṣṇaṃ*)—wealth fit for giving

### Printed p. 200
- उवोचिथ हि (*uvocitha hi*)—you have obtained
- ते (*te*)—your
- उभा गभस्ती (*ubhā gabhastī*)—both arms
- वसुना (*vasunā*)—with wealth
- पूर्णा (*pūrṇā*)—are full
- सूनृता (*sūnṛtā*)—your excellent speech
- वसव्या (*vasavyā*)—riches
- न नियमते (*na niyamate*)—does not hold back

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, possessed of wealth, in the apportionment of wealth, great and small, you have obtained wealth fit for giving. Both your arms are full of wealth. Your excellent speech does not hold back riches.

##### English translation
You Maghavan, have determined what is to be given in the apportionment of much or of little wealth, for both your hands are full of treasures and your sincere (promises) of riches do not restrain them.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk and in the next four Ṛks Indra is praised.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वमिं॑द्र॒ स्वय॑शा ऋभु॒क्षा वाजो॒ न सा॒धुरस्त॑मे॒षि ऋक्वा॑ ।  
> व॒यं नु ते॑ दा॒श्वांसः॑ स्याम॒ ब्रह्म॑ कृ॒ण्वंतो॑ हरिवो॒ वसि॑ष्ठाः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । इंद्र । स्वयशाः । ऋभुक्षाः । वाजः । न । साधुः । अस्तं । एषि । ऋक्वा ।  
> वयं । नु । ते । दाश्वांसः । स्याम । ब्रह्म । कृण्वंतः । हरिवः । वसिष्ठाः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र स्वयशा असाधारणकीर्तिर्ऋभुक्षा ऋभुनिवासक ऋभूणामीश्वरो वा त्वं साधुः साधको वाजो नान्नमिव ऋक्वा ऋक्वणः स्तोतुर्मास्तं गृहमेषि । प्राप्नुहि । त्वद्य  वसिष्ठा एतत्संज्ञका ऋषयो वयं हे हरिवः स्वकीयोऽपेतेंद्र ते त्वदर्थं दाश्वांसो हविर्लक्षणान्नं दत्तवंतो ब्रह्म स्तोत्रं कृण्वंतः कुर्वंतः संतः स्याम । भवेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, of unique renown, dwelling with the Ṛbhus or lord of the Ṛbhus, you, like accomplishing food, come to the house of the praiser. We, the seers named Vasiṣṭha, O lord of bay horses, your own, would be those who give you the oblation-food and make praise for you.

### Printed p. 201

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- स्वयशाः (*svayaśāḥ*)—of unique renown
- ऋभुक्षाः (*ṛbhukṣāḥ*)—abode of radiance
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- साधुः (*sādhuḥ*)—as accomplisher of all works
- वाजः न (*vājaḥ na*)—like satisfying food
- ऋक्वाः (*ṛkvāḥ*)—of me the praiser
- अस्तं (*astaṃ*)—to the house
- एषि (*eṣi*)—be gracious, come
- हरिवः (*harivaḥ*)—O Indra with horses
- नु (*nu*)—now
- वसिष्ठाः (*vasiṣṭhāḥ*)—named Vasiṣṭha
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- ते (*te*)—addressing you
- दाश्वांसः (*dāśvāṃsaḥ*)—offering the oblation
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—praise
- कृण्वंतः (*kṛṇvaṃtaḥ*)—composing
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, of unique renown, abode of radiance, you as accomplisher of all works, come graciously, like satisfying food, to the house of us, your praisers. O Indra with horses, may we, named Vasiṣṭha, now be offering the oblation to you and composing praise.

##### English translation
