Do you, Indra, who are Ribhukshin, and of especial renown, who, like food, are the fulfiller (of wants), come to the dwelling of the worshipper; lord of bay horses, may we, Vashisthas, be today the donors (of the offerings) to you, the celebrators of your praise.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सनि॑तासि प्र॒वतो॑ दा॒शुषे॑ चि॒द्याभि॒र्विवे॑षो हर्यश्व धी॒भिः ।  
> व॒व॒न्मा नु ते॒ युज्या॑भिरू॒ती क॒दा न॑ इंद्र रा॒य आ द॑शस्येः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सनिता । असि । प्रवतः । दाशुषे । चित् । याभिः । विवेषः । हरिअश्व । धीभिः ।  
> ववन्म । नु । ते । युज्याभिः । ऊती । कदा । नः । इंद्र । रायः । आ । दशस्येः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे हर्यश्व हरिनामकाश्वेंद्र त्वं याभिर्धीभिरस्मदीयाभिः स्तुतिभिर्विवेषः व्याप्नोषि स त्वं दाशुषे चिद्धविर्दत्तवते यजमानायोपि प्रवतः प्रवणस्य धनस्य सनितासि । दाता भवसि । अपि च हे इंद्र त्वं नोऽस्मभ्यं कदा कस्मिन्काले रायो धनान्या दशस्येः । प्रयच्छेः । त्वद्य  ते तव युज्याभिर्योग्याभिरूतिभि रक्षाभिर्ववन्म । त्वां संभजेम ॥
> २६

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, whose horses are named Hari, you with whose praises of ours you pervade (are reached), you are the giver of abundant wealth even to the sacrificer who has given the oblation. And, O Indra, when will you bestow riches on us? We would serve you with your fitting protections.

### Printed p. 202

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हर्यश्व (*haryaśva*)—O Indra, who have horses named Hari
- याभिः (*yābhiḥ*)—by which
- धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*)—praises of ours
- विवेषः (*viveṣaḥ*)—you are pervaded (reached), such as you
- दाशुषे चित् (*dāśuṣe cit*)—to the sacrificer who gives the oblation
- प्रवतः (*pravataḥ*)—of abundant wealth
- सनिता असि (*sanitā asi*)—you are the giver
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- कदा (*kadā*)—at what time
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—riches
- आ दशस्येः (*ā daśasyeḥ*)—will you give
- नु (*nu*)—now
- ते (*te*)—your
- युज्याभिः (*yujyābhiḥ*)—fitting
- ऊती (*ūtī*)—together with protections
- ववन्म (*vavanma*)—we worship you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, who have horses named Hari, by our praises you are the giver of abundant wealth to the sacrificer who gives the oblation. O Indra, at what time will you give us wealth? Now, together with your fitting protections, we worship you.

##### English translation
Lord of bay horses, you are the giver of the descending (wealth) to the donor (of the oblation), by whose sacred rites you are magnified : when do you bestow upon us riches : when may we be secure by your appropriate protections.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वा॒सय॑सीव वे॒धस॒स्त्वं नः॑ क॒दा न॑ इंद्र॒ वच॑सो बुबोधः ।  
> अस्तं॑ ता॒त्या धि॒या र॒यिं सु॒वीरं॑ पृ॒क्षो नो॒ अर्वा॒ न्यु॑हीत वा॒जी ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वासयसिऽइव । वेधसः । त्वं । नः । कदा । नः । इंद्र । वचसः । बुबोधः ।  
> अस्तं । तात्या । धिया । रयिं । सुवीरं । पृक्षः । नः । अर्वा । नि । उहीत । वाजी ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र कदा कस्मिन्काले नोऽस्मदीयं वचसो वचोरूपं स्तोत्रं बुबोधः । अवगच्छेः । कथा स त्वं वेधसः स्तोतॄनस्मान्वासयसीव । इवेदानीमर्थे । इदानीं स्वकीये स्थानेऽवस्थापयसि । किंच वाजी बलवानर्वा वेगवांस्त्वदीयोऽश्वस्तात्या  ॥ तनोतेरिदं रूपं ॥ संततया धियास्मत्प्रेरितया स्तुत्या हेतुभूत

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, when will you understand our speech, our praise in the form of words? How? Like one who now settles us the praisers in his own place, you establish us. Further, your swift, strong horse (is invoked) with a continuous praise (तात्या (*tātyā*) is a form of the root तनोते (*tanote*)), praise sent by us as cause

### Printed p. 203

> यो सुवीरं शोभनपुत्रोपेतं रयिं त्वदीयं धनं पृक्षोऽन्नं च नोऽस्मदीयमस्तं गृहं न्युहीत । निवहेत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

... may it bring to our house your wealth accompanied by good sons, and food.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- कदा (*kadā*)—when
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- वचसः (*vacasaḥ*)—praise
- बुबोधः (*bubodhaḥ*)—will you understand
- वेधसः (*vedhasaḥ*)—us the praisers
- वासयॆसीव (*vāsayèsīva*)—when will you make us dwell firmly in our place
- वाजी (*vājī*)—powerful
- अर्वा (*arvā*)—your horse
- तात्या (*tātyā*)—always
- धिया (*dhiyā*)—as reward for our praise
- सुवीरं (*suvīraṃ*)—together with excellent sons and others
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—your wealth
- पृक्षः (*pṛkṣaḥ*)—and food
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- अस्तं (*astaṃ*)—to the house
- नि उहीतॆ (*ni uhītè*)—let it bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, when will you understand our praise? When will you make us the praisers dwell firmly in our place? May your powerful horse always bring to our house, as reward for our praise, your wealth together with excellent sons and others, and food.

##### English translation
When, Indra, will you appropriate our praise : at present you establish us (your) adorers in our dwelling : let your swift horse, (influenced) by our protracted solemnity, convey to our abode riches, male offspring, and food

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि यं दे॒वी निर्ऋ॑तिश्चि॒दीशे॒ नक्षं॑त॒ इंद्रं॑ श॒रदः॑ सु॒पृक्षः॑ ।  
> उप॑ त्रिबं॒धुर्ज॒रद॑ष्टिमे॒त्यस्व॑वेशं॒ यं कृ॒णवं॑त॒ मर्ताः॑ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । यं । देवी । निःऽऋतिः । चित् । ईशे । नक्षंते । इंद्रं । शरदः । सुपृक्षः ।  
> उप । त्रिबंधुः । जरत्अष्टिं । एति । अस्ववेशं । यं । कृणवंत । मर्ताः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवी द्योतमाना निर्ऋतिश्चिद्भूमिरपीशे ॥ कृत्यार्थे केन्प्रत्ययः ॥ ईशितव्यास्वभूता सती यमिंद्रमभि नक्षते व्याप्नोति । सुपृक्षः शोभनान्नैरुपेताः शरदः संवत्सराश्च यमिंद्रं नक्षंते व्याप्नुवंति । मर्ता मरणधर्माणः स्तोतारो

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The shining goddess निर्ऋति (*nirṛti*), the Earth, though a ruler (the ending केन् (*ken*) in the sense of what is to be done), being herself subject to rule, pervades that Indra; and the years (शरदः (*śaradaḥ*)), rich in good food, pervade the Indra whom mortals, the mortal praisers

### Printed p. 204

> वयं यमिंद्रमस्ववेशं स्वकीये स्थानेऽनुपविशंतं कृणवंते कुर्वंति । त्रिबंधुस्त्रयाणां लोकानां बंधको विधारकः स इंद्रो जरदष्टिं जरज्जीर्णमष्टिरशनं यस्य बलस्य हेतुभूतं तद्बलमुपैति । उपगच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

... we make not abiding in his own place but settling in our own. The Indra who is त्रिबंधुः (*tribaṃdhuḥ*), binder and upholder of the three worlds, approaches that strength of which the जरदष्टि (*jaradaṣṭi*) (worn-out food) is the cause.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवी (*devī*)—divine
- निर्ऋतिश्चित् (*nirṛtiścit*)—even the goddess Earth
- ईशे (*īśe*)—though independent
- यं इंद्रं (*yaṃ iṃdraṃ*)—which Indra
- अभि (नक्षते) (*abhi (nakṣate)*)—she resorts to
- सुपृक्षः (*supṛkṣaḥ*)—rich in excellent food
- शरदः (*śaradaḥ*)—the years
- नक्षंते (*nakṣaṃte*)—resort to which Indra
- मर्ताः (*martāḥ*)—also men
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Indra
- अस्ववेशं (*asvaveśaṃ*)—so that he comes to their sacrifice, not remaining in his heaven
- कृणवंते (*kṛṇavaṃte*)—they make
- त्रिबंधुः (*tribaṃdhuḥ*)—Indra, kinsman of the three worlds
- जरदष्टिं (*jaradaṣṭiṃ*)—very abundant strength
- उपेति (*upeti*)—attains

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Even the divine Earth, though independent, resorts to Indra; the years, rich in excellent food, resort to Indra; men too make Indra come to the sacrifice without staying in his heaven. Such Indra, kinsman of the three worlds, attains very abundant strength (he is endowed with strength).

##### English translation
Indra, the upholder of the three regions, whom the divine Nirriti acknowledges as ruler,. whom abundant years pass over, whom mortals detain from his own abode, approaches to (recruit) his decaying strength.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॒ राधां॑सि सवितः स्त॒वध्यै॒ आ रायो॑ यंतु॒ पर्व॑तस्य रा॒तौ ।  
> सदा॑ नो दि॒व्यः पा॒युः सि॑षक्तु यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । राधांसि । सवितरिति । स्तवध्यै । आ । रायः । यंतु । पर्वतस्य । रातौ ।  
> सदा । नः । दिव्यः । पायुः । सिसक्तु । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 205

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सवितः सर्वस्य प्रेरक देव त्वत्सकाशात् स्तवध्यै स्तोतुं योग्यानि राधांसि धनानि नोऽस्माना यंतु । आगच्छंतु । पर्वतस्य । पर्वत इति कश्चिदिंद्रस्य सखा । एत-संज्ञकस्य देवस्य रातौ दाने सति रायो धनान्यस्माना यंतु । पायुः सर्वस्य पालको दिव्यो दिवि भवः स इंद्रः सदा सर्वदा नोऽस्मान् सिषक्तु । रक्षकत्वेन सेवतां । अस्मिन्सूक्ते ये प्रतिपादिता देवास्ते सर्वे यूयं नोऽस्मान् स्वस्तिभिः कल्याणैः सदा पात । पालयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Savitṛ, god who impels all, may riches (राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*), wealth) worthy to be praised come to us from you. Parvata: a certain friend of Indra. When the god of that name makes a gift, may riches come to us. Pāyu is the protector of all; Divya is the one born in heaven, namely Indra. May he always serve us as protector. All the gods who are celebrated in this sūkta, you, with blessings (welfare), always protect us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सवितः (*savitaḥ*)—O Savitṛ
- स्तवध्यै (*stavadhyai*)—worthy of praise
- राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*)—your riches
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ यंतु (*ā yaṃtu*)—may they come
- पर्वतस्य (*parvatasya*)—of the god Parvata
- रातौ (*rātau*)—by reason of generosity **NOTE**
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—his riches
- आ (*ā*)—may they come and join us
- पायुः (*pāyuḥ*)—the protector of the world
- दिव्यः (*divyaḥ*)—Indra, who dwells in heaven
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सिषक्तु (*siṣaktu*)—may he serve with protections (O gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Savitṛ, may your riches, worthy of praise, come to us. May the riches of the generous god Parvata also come and join us. May Indra, protector of the world and dweller in heaven, always come and serve us with his protections. O gods, always protect us with blessings (कल्याण दासगळिंद (*kalyāṇa dāsagaḷiṃda*) **NOTE**).

##### English translation
May riches worthy of laudation come, Savitri to us : riches that are in the bestowal of Parvata : may the heavenly protector (of all) ever preserve us : and do you, (universal gods), ever cherish us with blessings.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk there are praises of Savitṛ, of Indra, and of a deity called Parvata, said to be Indra's friend.

## Sūkta 38 (Maṇḍala 2)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उदु ष्य देव इत्यष्टर्चं पंचमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं सवितृदेवताकं । सप्तम्यष्टम्यौ वाजिदेवताके । भगमुग्रोऽवस इत्यर्धर्चो भगदेवत्ये सावित्रो वा । तथा चानुक्रमणिका । उदु ष्य सावित्रमंत्ये वाजिनौ भगमिति भागे वार्धर्चौ इति ॥ गतः सूक्तविनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning उदु ष्य देव (*udu ṣya deva*) has eight Ṛks; it is the fifth sūkta (of the anuvāka). Its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Savitṛ. The seventh and eighth Ṛks have Vāji (the Vājins) as deity. The two half-verses beginning भगमुग्रोऽवस (*bhagamugro 'vasa*) have Bhaga as deity, or else Savitṛ. So the Anukramaṇikā: उदु ष्य सावित्रमंत्ये वाजिनौ भगमिति भागे वार्धर्चौ (*udu ṣya sāvitramaṃtye vājinau bhagamiti bhāge vārdharcau*). The use of the whole sūkta (sūktaviniyoga) has been stated already.

##### English translation
Anuvāda: This sūkta, उदु ष्य देवः (*udu ṣya devaḥ*), is the fifth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has eight Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta. Savitṛ is the deity, the metre is Triṣṭubh. But the seventh and eighth Ṛks of this sūkta have the Vājins as deity.

### Printed p. 206
The latter half of the sixth Ṛk, भगमुग्रोऽवसे (*bhagamugro 'vase*), has Bhaga or Savitṛ as deity. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said उदु ष्य सावित्रमंत्ये वाजिनौ भगमिति भागोऽर्धर्चः (*udu ṣya sāvitramaṃtye vājinau bhagamiti bhāgo 'rdharcaḥ*) **NOTE**. The use has been stated before.

##### Particulars

Sūkta—38
Maṇḍala—2. Anuvāka—3. Sūkta—38.
Aṣṭaka—2. Adhyāya—4. Varga—5.
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta—8.
Seer—Vasiṣṭha.
Deity—1–5, and the first half of 6: Savitṛ; the second half of 6: Savitṛ or Bhaga; 7, 8: the Vājins.
Metre—Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदु॒ ष्य दे॒वः स॑वि॒ता य॑याम हिर॒ण्ययी॑म॒मतिं॒ यामशि॑श्रेत् ।  
> नू॒नं भगो॒ हव्यो॒ मानु॑षेभि॒र्वि यो रत्ना॑ पुरू॒वसु॒र्दधा॑ति ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । स्यः । देवः । सविता । ययाम । हिरण्ययीं । अमतिं । यां । अशिश्रेत् ।  
> नूनं । भगः । हव्यः । मानुषेभिः । वि । यः । रत्ना । पुरुवसुः । दधाति ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सविता सर्वस्य प्रेरकः स्य स देवो हिरण्ययीं सुवर्णमयीं याममतिं । रूपेणाव्यैतेत् । रूपं । प्रभामित्यर्थः । आशिश्रेत् आश्रयति ताममतिमुद्ययाम । उद्यच्छति । उद्गमयति । उ इति पादपूरणः । नूनमद्य भगो भजनीयो यः सविता मानुषेभिर्मनुष्यैः स्तोतृभिर्हव्यो हवनीयः स्तोतव्यो भवति । पुरूवसुर्बहुधनो यो देवः स्तोतृभ्यो रत्ना रत्नानि रमणीयानि धनानि वि दधाति करोति । स सविता देवस्ताममतिमुद्ययामेति पूर्वेण संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Savitṛ, the impeller of all, that god raises up (उद्ययाम (*udyayāma*)) the golden (हिरण्ययीं (*hiraṇyayīṃ*)) amati which he resorts to (आशिश्रेत् (*āśiśret*)). Amati is glossed as form, that is, radiance; the quoted words रूपेणाव्यैतेत् (*rūpeṇāvyaitet*) **NOTE** are followed by रूपं (*rūpaṃ*). He raises it up, makes it rise. उ (*u*) is a mere verse-filler. Now (today) Bhaga, the one to be shared in (bhajanīya), who is Savitṛ, is to be invoked, that is, praised, by men, the praisers. Purūvasu is one with much wealth: the god who bestows (वि दधाति (*vi dadhāti*)) on the praisers ratna, delightful riches. That god Savitṛ raised that amati: this is the connection with the preceding.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- भगः (*bhagaḥ*)—the venerable one
- मानुषेभिः (*mānuṣebhiḥ*)—by men
- हव्यः (*havyaḥ*)—the one who is praised
- पुरुवसुः (*puruvasuḥ*)—the one possessed of very abundant wealth
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Savitṛ-god
### Printed p. 207
- रत्ना (*ratnā*)—delightful riches
- वि दधाति (*vi dadhāti*)—gives to the praisers
- स्यः (*syaḥ*)—such a famous one
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the divine one
- सविता (*savitā*)—Savitṛ-god
- हिरण्ययीं (*hiraṇyayīṃ*)—golden
- यां अमतिं (*yāṃ amatiṃ*)—which radiant form
- आश्रेत् (*āśret*)—he has taken up, that
- उद्ययाम (*udyayāma*)—he spreads, making it shine

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The venerable one, praised by men, possessed of abundant wealth, the Savitṛ-god who gives delightful riches to the praisers: such a famous, divine Savitṛ makes his own golden, radiant form shine.

##### English translation
The divine Savitri has diffused the golden radiance on high, of which he is the asylum : verily Bhaga is to be adored by men who, abounding in wealth, distributes treasures (amoungst them)

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदु॑ तिष्ठ सवितः श्रु॒ध्य१॒॑स्य हिर॑ण्यपाणे॒ प्रभृ॑तावृ॒तस्य॑ ।  
> व्यु१॒॑र्वीं पृ॒थ्वीम॒मतिं॑ सृजा॒न आ नृभ्यो॑ मर्त॒भोज॑नं सुवा॒नः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । तिष्ठ । सवितरिति । श्रुधि । अस्य । हिरण्यपाणे । प्रभृतौ । ऋतस्य ।  
> वि । उर्वीं । पृथ्वीं । अमतिं । सृजानः । आ । नृभ्यः । मर्तभोजनं । सुवानः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सवितः सर्वस्य प्रेरयितर्देव त्वमुत्तिष्ठ । ऊर्ध्वं गच्छ । ततो हे हिरण्यपाणे हे सुवर्णहस्त त्वमस्मदभीप्सितप्रदानायर्तस्य यज्ञस्य प्रभृतौ प्रणयनेऽस्यास्मदीयमिदं स्तोत्रं श्रुधि । शृणु । उ इति पूरणः । कीदृशस्त्वं । उर्वीं विस्तीर्णां पृथ्वीं प्रथिताममतिं रूपं प्रभां वि सृजानो विसृजन्नृभ्यो नेतृभ्यः स्तोतृभ्यो मर्तभोजनं मनुष्याणां भोगयोग्यं धनमा सुवानः प्रेरयन् । एवंभूतस्त्वमिदं स्तोत्रं शृण्विति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Savitṛ, impeller of all, rise up, go upward. Then, O golden-handed one, in order to grant what we desire, at the opening (prabhṛti, the leading forward) of the ṛta, the sacrifice, hear this praise of ours; उ (*u*) is a filler. What are you like? You send forth the broad, spread-out amati, that is form or radiance, and you impel to the leaders (nṛ), the praisers, wealth fit for the enjoyment of mortals. Being such, hear this praise: such is the connection.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सवितः (*savitaḥ*)—O Savitṛ, impeller of all
- उत् तिष्ठ (*ut tiṣṭha*)—rise
- हिरण्यपाणे (*hiraṇyapāṇe*)—O Savitṛ with golden hands
- उर्वीं (*urvīṃ*)—which is extensive
- पृथ्वीं (*pṛthvīṃ*)—and spreads out abundantly, pervading
### Printed p. 208
- अमतिं (*amatiṃ*)—the form of radiance
- वि सृजानः (*vi sṛjānaḥ*)—spreading
- नृभ्यः (*nṛbhyaḥ*)—to the praisers, who are leaders
- मर्तभोजनं (*martabhojanaṃ*)—wealth fit for the enjoyment of men
- आ सुवानः (*ā suvānaḥ*)—supplying
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice
- प्रभृतौ (*prabhṛtau*)—at the time of beginning
- अस्य (*asya*)—this praise of ours
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Savitṛ, impeller of all, rise. O Savitṛ with golden hands, spreading the form of radiance that is extensive and pervades abundantly, and supplying to the praisers, who are leaders, wealth fit for the enjoyment of men, hear this praise of ours at the beginning of the sacrifice.

##### English translation
Rise up, Savitri : hear (our solicitations) upon the celebration of this ceremony, (you who are) diffusing light over the spacious earth, and bestowing human enjoyments upon men.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अपि॑ ष्टु॒तः स॑वि॒ता दे॒वो अ॑स्तु॒ यमा चि॒द्विश्वे॒ वस॑वो गृ॒णंति॑ ।  
> स नः॒ स्तोमा॑न्नम॒स्य१॒॑श्चनो॑ धा॒द्विश्वेः॑भि पातु पा॒युभि॒र्नि सू॒रीन् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अपि । स्तुतः । सविता । देवः । अस्तु । यं । आ । चित् । विश्वे । वसवः । गृणंति ।  
> सः । नः । स्तोमान् । नमस्यः । चनः । धात् । विश्वेभिः । पातु । पायुभिः । नि ।  
> सूरीन् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अपि च सविता देवोऽस्माभिः स्तुतोऽस्तु । अस्मदीयाः स्तुतीः शृणोत्वित्यर्थः । विश्वे वसवश्चित् सर्वे देवा अपि यं सवितारमा गृणंति अभिष्टुवंति नमस्यः सर्वैर्नमस्करणीयः स देवः स्तोमान्नोऽस्मदीयानि स्तोत्राणि चनोऽन्नं धात् । दधातु । अन्नफलानि करोतु । विश्वेभिर्विश्वैः सर्वैः पायुभिः पालनैः सूरीन् स्तोतॄनस्मान्नि पातु । नितरां पालयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And may the god Savitṛ be praised by us; that is, may he hear our praises. Whom all the Vasus, all the gods, praise (आ गृणंति (*ā gṛṇaṃti*), address with praise), that Savitṛ, to be revered (namasya) by all, may he set (धात् (*dhāt*), place) our praises (stomas) and food (चनः (*canaḥ*) is food): may he make them yield the fruits of food. With all (viśvebhiḥ) protections (pāyubhiḥ) may he protect well the sūris, the praisers, us; नि पातु (*ni pātu*) means may he protect completely.

### Printed p. 209

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अपि (*api*)—and
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the divine
- सविता (*savitā*)—Savitṛ
- स्तुतः (*stutaḥ*)—as one praised by us
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let him be
- विश्वे (*viśve*)—all
- वसवः चित् (*vasavaḥ cit*)—even the gods called Vasus
- यं (*yaṃ*)—whom, Savitṛ
- आ गृणंति (*ā gṛṇaṃti*)—they praise
- नमस्यः (*namasyaḥ*)—the venerable
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Savitṛ
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- स्तोमान् (*stomān*)—praises
- चनः (*canaḥ*)—and food
- धात् (*dhāt*)—may he hold
- विश्वेभिः (*viśvebhiḥ*)—all
- पायुभिः (*pāyubhiḥ*)—with protections
- सूरीन् (*sūrīn*)—us, the praisers
- नि पातु (*ni pātu*)—may he protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the divine Savitṛ be praised by us. Whom all the gods called Vasus praise, that same venerable Savitṛ, may he hold our praises and our foods. With all protections may he protect us, the praisers.

##### English translation
Glorified be the divine Savitri, whom all the gods praise : may that adorable (divinity) requite our praises (with) food : may he always protect the devout with all his protections.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि यं दे॒व्यदि॑तिर्गृ॒णाति॑ स॒वं दे॒वस्य॑ सवि॒तुर्जु॑षा॒णा ।  
> अ॒भि स॒म्राजो॒ वरु॑णो गृणंत्य॒भि मि॒त्रासो॑ अर्य॒मा स॒जोषाः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । यं । देवी । अदितिः । गृणाति । सवं । देवस्य । सवितुः । जुषाणा ।  
> अभि । संराजः । वरुणः । गृणंति । अभि । मित्रासः । अर्यमा । सजोषाः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवी द्योतमानादितिरदीना देवमाता यं सवितारमभि गृणाति अभिष्टौति । कीदृशी । सवितुर्देवस्यैव सवं प्रसवमनुज्ञां जुषाणा सेवमाना । सम्राजः सम्यग्राजमाना वरुणः । उपलक्षणमेतत् । वरुणादयो देवा यं सवितारमभि गृणंति अभिष्टुवंति । मित्रासो मित्रादयश्च सजोषाः समानप्रीतिरर्यमैतत्संज्ञको देवश्चयमभि गृणंति । स नः स्तोमांश्चनो धादिति पूर्वयर्चा संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The goddess Aditi, the shining one, the undiminished one, mother of the gods, praises (अभि गृणाति (*abhi gṛṇāti*)) that Savitṛ. What is she like? She delights in (serves) the sava of the god Savitṛ, that is, his impulsion, his permission. Samrāj, those shining well, Varuṇa: this is an indication (upalakṣaṇa), so Varuṇa and the other gods praise that Savitṛ. Mitrāsaḥ, Mitra and the others, and Aryaman, the god of that name, of the same delight (sajoṣāḥ), praise him. The connection is with the previous Ṛk: स नः स्तोमांश्चनो धादिति (*sa naḥ stomāṃścano dhāditi*).

### Printed p. 210

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवस्य (*devasya*)—of the radiant one
- सवितुः (*savituḥ*)—of the Savitṛ, who impels
- सवं (*savaṃ*)—the permission
- जुषाणा (*juṣāṇā*)—she who serves
- देवी (*devī*)—the divine
- अदितिः (*aditiḥ*)—the goddess Aditi
- यं (*yaṃ*)—whom, Savitṛ
- अभि गृणाति (*abhi gṛṇāti*)—she praises
- सम्राजः (*samrājaḥ*)—those who have complete sovereignty
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa and the other gods
- अभि (गृणंति) (*abhi (gṛṇaṃti)*)—whom, Savitṛ, they praise
- सजोषाः (*sajoṣāḥ*)—possessed of equal affection
- मित्रासः (*mitrāsaḥ*)—Mitra and the others
- अर्यमा (*aryamā*)—the god called Aryaman
- अभि गृणंति (*abhi gṛṇaṃti*)—whomever they praise, that Savitṛ may he hold our food and the rest

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The goddess Aditi, who serves the permission of the radiant, impelling Savitṛ, praises Savitṛ; the gods beginning with Varuṇa, who have complete sovereignty, praise Savitṛ; Mitra and the others of equal affection, and the god Aryaman, praise him: may that Savitṛ hold our food and the rest.

##### English translation
Whom the divine Aditi, delighting at the birth of the divine Savitri, glorifies, whom the supreme sovereigns, Varuna, Mitra, Aryaman, (and other gods), consentaneously adore.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि ये मि॒थो व॒नुषः॒ सपं॑ते रा॒तिं दि॒वो रा॑ति॒षाचः॑ पृथि॒व्याः ।  
> अहि॑र्बु॒ध्न्य॑ उ॒त नः॑ शृणोतु॒ वरू॒त्र्येक॑धेनुभि॒र्नि पा॑तु ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । ये । मिथः । वनुषः । सपंते । रातिं । दिवः । रातिऽसाचः । पृथिव्याः ।  
> अहिः । बुध्न्यः । उत । नः । शृणोतु । वरूत्री । एकधेनुभिः । नि । पातु ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> रातिषाचो दानसेविनो वनुषः संभक्तारो ये यजमाना मिथः परस्परं संहता भूत्वा सवितारमभिलक्ष्य सपंते परिचरंति । कीदृशं । दिवो द्युलोकस्य पृथिव्या भूमेश्च रातिं मित्रभूतं । उतापि चाहिर्बुध्न्यः । बुध्नेऽंतरिक्षे भवो बुध्न्यः । एतीत्यहिः । एतत्पदद्वयाभिधो मध्यमस्थानोऽग्निरहिर्बुध्न्य इत्युच्यते ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificers who are rātiṣāc, devoted to giving, vanuṣaḥ, sharers (worshippers), gathered together (mithaḥ, with one another), direct themselves to Savitṛ and serve him (सपंते (*sapaṃte*)). What is he like? A friend to the gift (rāti) of heaven and of earth. And also Ahirbudhnya: budhna is the atmosphere, one who is in the budhna is budhnya; एतीत्यहिः (*etītyahiḥ*). The deity of the middle region, Agni, denoted by these two words, is called Ahirbudhnya.

### Printed p. 211

> सवितुर्मित्रभूतः सोऽपि तेषां नोऽस्माकं सवितृविषयं स्तोत्रं शृणोतु । तथा वरूत्री वाग्देवी च सवितृसहिता सत्येकधेनुभिर्मुख्याभिर्गोभिर्नि पातु । नितरामस्मान्पालयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The one who is a friend of Savitṛ (Ahirbudhnya) also hears our praise addressed to Savitṛ. Likewise Varūtrī, the goddess of speech, together with Savitṛ, protects us with the principal cows; let her guard us completely.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the heaven-world
- पृथिव्याः (*pṛthivyāḥ*)—of the earth
- रातिं (*rātiṃ*)—Savitṛ, the friend
- रातिषाचः (*rātiṣācaḥ*)—those who attend to giving
- वनुषः (*vanuṣaḥ*)—those who enjoy what he has given
- ये (*ye*)—which sacrificers
- मिथः (*mithaḥ*)—gathering together
- अभि सपंते (*abhi sapaṃte*)—serve
- नः (*naḥ*)—such praise of ours
- शृणोतु (*śṛṇotu*)—may Savitṛ hear
- उत (*uta*)—and
- अहिर्बुध्न्यः (*ahirbudhnyaḥ*)—may the god Ahirbudhnya also hear
- वरूत्री (*varūtrī*)—the protecting goddess of speech
- एकधेनुभिः (*ekadhenubhiḥ*)—together with the principal cows
- नि पातु (*ni pātu*)—may she protect us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Savitṛ hear the praise of us sacrificers who, enjoying the wealth given by Savitṛ, gather together and serve Savitṛ, the friend of heaven and earth. May the god Ahirbudhnya also hear it. And may the protecting goddess of speech, with the principal cows, protect us.

##### English translation
Whom those solicitous for wealth, those enjoying (it), mutually worship, the benefactor of heaven and earth : may Ahirbhudhnya hear us : may the protectress, (the goddess of speech), cherish us with excellent cattle.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अनु॒ तन्नो॒ जास्पति॑र्मंसीष्ट॒ रत्नं॑ दे॒वस्य॑ सवि॒तुरि॑या॒नः ।  
> भग॑मु॒ग्रोऽव॑से॒ जोह॑वीति॒ भग॒मनु॑ग्रो॒ अध॑ याति॒ रत्नं॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनु । तत् । नः । जाःपतिः । मंसीष्ट । रत्नं । देवस्य । सवितुः । इयानः ।  
> भगं । उग्रः । अवसे । जोहवीति । भगं । अनुग्रः । अध । याति । रत्नं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इयानोऽस्माभिर्याच्यमानो जास्पतिः प्रजानां पालकः सविता देवः सवितुर्देवस्य स्वस्य संबंधि रत्नं रमणीयं तत्प्रसिद्धं धनं नोऽस्माकमनु मंसीष्ट । अनुमन्यतां । उग्र ओजस्वी स्तोता भगं भजनीयं सवितारं भगसंज्ञकं देवं वा-

### Printed p. 212

> वसे नोऽस्माकं रक्षणाय जोहवीति । भृशं ह्वयति । अथापि चानुग्रोऽसमर्थः स्तोता भगमेतत्संज्ञकं सवितारं वा रत्नं रमणीयं तत्प्रसिद्धं धनं याति । याचते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Savitṛ, the protector of progeny, being besought by us, is to grant us the desirable wealth that belongs to himself, the god Savitṛ; अनु मंसीष्ट (*anu maṃsīṣṭa*) means "let him consent". The mighty (energetic) praiser repeatedly and intensely calls on Bhaga, the adorable Savitṛ, or the god named Bhaga, for our protection. And the incapable praiser too begs for that desirable wealth from Bhaga or Savitṛ.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इयानः (*iyānaḥ*)—the one besought by us
- जास्पतिः (*jāspatiḥ*)—Savitṛ, protector of progeny
- देवस्य सवितुः (*devasya savituḥ*)—of himself, the divine one
- रत्नं (*ratnaṃ*)—attractive
- तत् (*tat*)—well-known wealth
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अनु मंसीष्ट (*anu maṃsīṣṭa*)—may he provide
- उग्रः (*ugraḥ*)—the brilliant praiser
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—the venerable god Bhaga
- अवसे (*avase*)—for our protection
- जोहवीति (*johavīti*)—calls many times
- अथ (*atha*)—and
- अनुग्रः (*anugraḥ*)—the incapable praiser
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—Bhaga
- रत्नं (*ratnaṃ*)—wealth
- याति (*yāti*)—begs

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Savitṛ, besought by us and the protector of progeny, provide us with his own attractive wealth, he being of divine nature. The brilliant praiser calls the venerable god Bhaga many times for our protection. The incapable praiser begs wealth from the god Bhaga.

##### English translation
May the protector of progeny, when solicited, consent to bestow upon us the precious (wealth) of the divine Savitri : the ardent (adorer) invokes repeatedly Bhaga for protection ; the less ardent solicits Bhaga for wealth.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शं नो॑ भवंतु वा॒जिनो॒ हवे॑षु दे॒वता॑ता मि॒तद्र॑वः स्व॒र्काः ।  
> जं॒भयं॒तोऽहिं॒ वृकं॒ रक्षां॑सि॒ सने॑म्य॒स्मद्यु॑यव॒न्नमी॑वाः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शं । नः । भवंतु । वाजिनः । हवेषु । देवताता । मितद्रवः । सुअर्काः ।  
> जंभयंतः । अहिं । वृकं । रक्षांसि । सनेमि । अस्मत् । युयवन् । अमीवाः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वैश्वदेवे पर्वणि वाजिनस्य हविषः शं नो भवंत्वित्यादिके द्वे याज्यानुवाक्ये । सूत्रितं च । शं नो भवंतु वाजिनो हवेषु वाजेवाजेऽवत वाजिनो न इत्यूर्ध्वज्ञुरनवासं याज्यां । आ. २-१७ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

At the Vaiśvadeva parvan, for the offering to Vājin, these two verses beginning शं नो भवंतु (*śaṃ no bhavaṃtu*) are the yājyā and anuvākyā. It is prescribed by the sūtra: शं नो भवंतु वाजिनो हवेषु वाजेवाजेऽवत वाजिनो न इत्यूर्ध्वज्ञुरनवासं याज्यां (*śaṃ no bhavaṃtu vājino haveṣu vājevāje 'vata vājino na ityūrdhvajñuranavāsaṃ yājyāṃ*) (Ā. 2-17).

### Printed p. 213

> देवताता देवतातौ यज्ञे हवेष्वस्मदीयेषु स्तोत्रेषु मितद्रवो मितद्रवणा मितमार्गाः स्वर्काः शोभनान्ना वाजिन एतदभिधायका देवा नोऽस्माकं शं सुखाय भवंतु । अपि चाहिमार्गस्य हंतारं वृकं वसूनामादातारं । चोरमिति शेषः । रक्षांसि च जंभयंतो हिंसंतो वाजिनो देवाः सनेमि । पुराणनाम्नैतत् । पुरातना अमीवा रोगानस्मदस्मत्तो युयवन् । पृथक्कुर्वंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the sacrifice, at our invocations (praises), the gods called Vājins, of restrained paths, bringing good food, are to be for our happiness. Destroying the serpent-like path-obstructor, the wolf (a thief, taking away riches) and the Rākṣasas, may the Vājins keep the ancient diseases away from us; सनेमि (*sanemi*) is an old word meaning "ancient".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवताता (*devatātā*)—in the sacrifice
- हवेषु (*haveṣu*)—while our praise is being recited
- मितद्रवः (*mitadravaḥ*)—those with regulated paths
- स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*)—those with excellent food
- वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*)—the powerful gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—of us
- शं (*śaṃ*)—for happiness
- भवंतु (*bhavaṃtu*)—may they be
- अहिं (*ahiṃ*)—the path-obstructor
- वृकं (*vṛkaṃ*)—the greedy thief
- रक्षांसि (*rakṣāṃsi*)—the Rākṣasas
- जंभयंतः (*jaṃbhayaṃtaḥ*)—the gods who destroy
- सनेमि (*sanemi*)—ancient
- अमीवाः (*amīvāḥ*)—diseases
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—from us
- युयवन् (*yuyavan*)—may they separate

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the gods, of regulated paths, possessing excellent food and powerful, be for our happiness while praise is being recited in the sacrifice. May the gods who destroy the path-obstructing, greedy thief and the Rākṣasas separate the ancient diseases from us.

##### English translation
May the Vajins, with slackened speed, bringing excellent food, be (disposed) for our happiness, upon our invocations at the worship of the gods: destroying the murderer, the robber, the Rakshas and keeping from us ancient maladies.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Vaiśvadeva parvan, in the offering of oblation made to the deity called Vāji, this Ṛk शं नो भवंतु (*śaṃ no bhavaṃtu*) and the Ṛk following it are to be recited as yājyā and anuvākyā respectively, as elaborated by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra शं नो भवंतु वाजिनो हवेषु वाजेवाजेऽवत वाजिनो न इत्यूर्ध्वज्ञुरनवासं याज्यां (*śaṃ no bhavaṃtu vājino haveṣu vājevāje 'vata vājino na ityūrdhvajñuranavāsaṃ yājyāṃ*) (Ā. 2-17).
वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*)—For the word वाजिन् (*vājin*) Yāska gives the meaning वाजी वेजनवान् (*vājī vejanavān*) (Ni. 2-28), a horse with swift motion. In the present Ṛk the word वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*) is nominative plural and indicates gods named Vājis. This word has another meaning also, "rays". To explain the word वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*) Yāska takes this very Ṛk as an example and gives its Nirukta:

### Printed p. 214
शं नो भवंतु ऎंब ई ऋक्कन्नू इदर मुंदिन ऋक्कन्नू क्रमवागि याज्या मत्तु अनुवाक्यामंत्रगळन्नागि पठिसबेकॆंदु आश्वलायनश्रौतसूत्रद शं नो भवंतु वाजिनो हवेषु वाजेवाजेऽवत वाजिनो न इत्यूर्ध्वज्ञुरनवासं याज्यां ऎंब सूत्रदिंद (आ. २-१७) विस्तृतवागिरुवुदु. वाजिनः—वाजिन् शब्दक्कॆ यास्करु वाजी वेजनवान् (नि. २-२८) वेगमनवुळ्ळ अश्ववु ऎंदु अर्थविवरणॆमाडिद्दारॆ. प्रकृत ऋक्किनल्लि वाजिनः ऎंब शब्दवु प्रथमाबहुवचनांतवागिद्दु वाजिगळॆंब हॆसरिन देवतॆगळन्नु सूचिसुवुदु. ई शब्दक्कॆ रश्मिगळु ऎंब इन्नॊंदु अर्थवू इरुवुदु. वाजिनः ऎंब शब्दद अर्थवन्नु विवरिसुवुदक्कागि यास्करु प्रकृत ऋक्कन्ने उदाहरणॆयागि तॆगॆदुकॊंडु अदक्कॆ निरुक्तवन्नु हेळिद्दारॆ.— शं नो भवंतु वाजिनो ह्वानेषु देवतातौ यज्ञे मितद्रवः सुमितद्रवः । स्वर्काः स्वंचना इति वा स्वर्चना इति वा स्वर्चिष इति वा । जंभयंतोऽहिं च वृकं च रक्षांसि च क्षिप्रमस्मद्यावयंत्वमीवा देवाश्वा इति वा ॥ (*śaṃ no bhavaṃtu èṃba ī ṛkkannū idara muṃdina ṛkkannū kramavāgi yājyā mattu anuvākyāmaṃtragaḷannāgi paṭhisabekèṃdu āśvalāyanaśrautasūtrada śaṃ no bhavaṃtu vājino haveṣu vājevāje 'vata vājino na ityūrdhvajñuranavāsaṃ yājyāṃ èṃba sūtradiṃda (ā. 2-17) vistṛtavāgiruvudu. vājinaḥ—vājin śabdakkè yāskaru vājī vejanavān (ni. 2-28) vegamanavuḷḷa aśvavu èṃdu arthavivaraṇèmāḍiddārè. prakṛta ṛkkinalli vājinaḥ èṃba śabdavu prathamābahuvacanāṃtavāgiddu vājigaḷèṃba hèsarina devatègaḷannu sūcisuvudu. ī śabdakkè raśmigaḷu èṃba innòṃdu arthavū iruvudu. vājinaḥ èṃba śabdada arthavannu vivarisuvudakkāgi yāskaru prakṛta ṛkkanne udāharaṇèyāgi tègèdukòṃḍu adakkè niruktavannu heḷiddārè.— śaṃ no bhavaṃtu vājino hvāneṣu devatātau yajñe mitadravaḥ sumitadravaḥ | svarkāḥ svaṃcanā iti vā svarcanā iti vā svarciṣa iti vā | jaṃbhayaṃto 'hiṃ ca vṛkaṃ ca rakṣāṃsi ca kṣipramasmadyāvayaṃtvamīvā devāśvā iti vā ‖*) (Ni. 12-44)
The word वाजिन् (*vājin*) means a horse of swift motion or a divine horse. Since the word वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*) is plural here, it means extremely swift divine horses or gods. देवतातौ (*devatātau*) means in the sacrifice.
मितद्रवः (*mitadravaḥ*), सुमितद्रवः (*sumitadravaḥ*), मितद्रवणा (*mitadravaṇā*), मितमार्गाः (*mitamārgāḥ*): ordinarily a horse is under the charge of the charioteer, so its speed has a limit according to the charioteer's wish; it must go slowly or quickly as he likes. So its speed is limited, that is, subject to regulation. Or, as the Bhāṣyakāra says, मितमार्गाः (*mitamārgāḥ*): it must go only on a fixed path, that is, the path the charioteer likes. So the word मितमार्ग (*mitamārga*) or मितद्रवः (*mitadravaḥ*) indicates this meaning too.
स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*)—For the word स्वर्क (*svarka*) Yāska says स्वंचना इति वा । शोभनं अंचंति गच्छंति । (*svaṃcanā iti vā | śobhanaṃ aṃcaṃti gacchaṃti |*) They go with a beautiful or excellent step, hence the name स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*); or स्वर्चनाः शोभनं वा अर्चनं येषां (*svarcanāḥ śobhanaṃ vā arcanaṃ yeṣāṃ*): their praise is well extolled, that is, their qualities are lauded, hence स्वर्चनाः (*svarcanāḥ*) or स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*); or स्वर्चिषः वा येषां शोभना अर्चिषो दीप्तयः ते आगच्छंतु । (*svarciṣaḥ vā yeṣāṃ śobhanā arciṣo dīptayaḥ te āgacchaṃtu |*) Their bodily lustre is very radiant. Because they are well grown, fat and full of enthusiasm with a sturdy body, and have glittering bodily splendour, they are called स्वर्चिषः (*svarciṣaḥ*) or स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*). Thus three kinds of meaning are given for स्वर्काः (*svarkāḥ*). The two words मितद्रवः स्वर्काः (*mitadravaḥ svarkāḥ*) are adjectives of वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*).
अहिं च वृकं च रक्षांसि (*ahiṃ ca vṛkaṃ ca rakṣāṃsi*)—अहिं आगत्य हंतारं । (*ahiṃ āgatya haṃtāraṃ |*) The enemy who chases and kills is called अहि (*ahi*); वृकं वसूनामादातारं अथवा यश्च पृकस्तस्करो मुष्णाति । (*vṛkaṃ vasūnāmādātāraṃ athavā yaśca pṛkastaskaro muṣṇāti |*) A thief who steals riches is called वृक (*vṛka*); रक्षांसि येऽस्मान् रहसि क्षिण्वंति । (*rakṣāṃsi ye 'smān rahasi kṣiṇvaṃti |*) The host of Rākṣasas who, hidden or in a secret place, harm us without our knowing, are called रक्षांसि (*rakṣāṃsi*). The main purport of the nirvacana is that the prayer here is that these gods destroy these three kinds of enemies and protect us.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वाजे॑वाजेवत वाजिनो नो॒ धने॑षु विप्रा अमृता ऋतज्ञाः ।  
> अ॒स्य मध्वः॑ पिबत मा॒दय॑ध्वं तृ॒प्ता या॑त प॒थिभि॑र्देव॒यानैः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 215

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वाजेऽवाजे । अवत । वाजिनः । नः । धनेषु । विप्राः । अमृताः । ऋतज्ञाः ।  
> अस्य । मध्वः । पिबत । मादयध्वं । तृप्ताः । यात । पथिऽभिः । देवऽयानैः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वाजिन एतन्नामका देवा विप्रा मेधाविनोऽमृता अमरणधर्माण ऋतज्ञाः सत्यं जानंतः एवंभूताः संतो यूयं वाजे वाजे सर्वेषु युद्धेषु
> नोऽस्माननेषु धननिमित्तेष्ववत । पालयत । ततो यूयमस्य मध्वो मधुरोपेतमिमं सोमं पिबत । सोमपानानंतरं मादयध्वं । यूयं तृप्ता भवत । ततस्तृप्ता यूयं देवयानैर्देवगमनसाधनैः पथिभिर्मार्गैर्यात । गच्छत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods named Vājins, being wise, immortal and knowing the truth, protect us in every battle, in those fought for wealth. Then drink this sweet Soma, and after drinking be exhilarated and satisfied. Then, satisfied, go by the paths that are the means of going to the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*)—O gods named Vāji
- विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)—wise
- अमृताः (*amṛtāḥ*)—free of mortality
- ऋतज्ञाः (*ṛtajñāḥ*)—knowers of truth, you
- वाजेवाजे (*vājevāje*)—in every battle
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- धनेषु (*dhaneṣu*)—for the sake of wealth
- अवत (*avata*)—protect
- मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)—sweet
- अस्य (*asya*)—this Soma
- पिबत (*pibata*)—drink
- मादयध्वं (*mādayadhvaṃ*)—be satisfied
- तृप्ताः (*tṛptāḥ*)—satisfied you
- देवयानैः (*devayānaiḥ*)—on which gods travel
- पथिभिः (*pathibhiḥ*)—by paths
- यात (*yāta*)—depart

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods named Vājis, you who are wise, immortal and knowers of truth, protect us in every battle for the sake of wealth. Drink this sweet Soma, be satisfied. Satisfied, travel by the paths of the gods.

##### English translation
Wise, immortal Vajins, observers of truth defend us in every conflict, and for the sake of wealth ; drink of this sweet (Soma beverage), be exhilarated (thereby) and satisfied ; proceed by the paths traversed by the gods.

##### English translation
Thirty-ninth Sūkta

## Sūkta 39 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऊर्ध्वो अग्निरिति सप्तर्चं षष्ठं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं वैश्वदेवं ।
> ऊर्ध्वः सप्त वैश्वदेवं त्वित्यनुक्रमणिका । सूक्तविनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥ द्वितीये
> छंदोमे प्रउगशस्त्रे ऊर्ध्वो अग्निरिति वैश्वदेवस्य तृचः । सूत्रितं च । ऊर्ध्वो
> अग्निः सुमतिं वस्वो अश्रेदुषस्या नः सरस्वती जुषाणेति प्रउगं । आ. ८-१० । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sixth sūkta, ऊर्ध्वो अग्निः (*ūrdhvo agniḥ*), of seven Ṛks, has Vasiṣṭha as seer, the Viśvedevas as deity and Triṣṭubh as metre. The Anukramaṇikā says ऊर्ध्वः सप्त वैश्वदेवं तु (*ūrdhvaḥ sapta vaiśvadevaṃ tu*). The use of the sūkta is by indication (laiṅgika). In the second chandoma, in the prauga-śastra, the three Ṛks beginning ऊर्ध्वो अग्निः (*ūrdhvo agniḥ*) are the Vaiśvadeva tṛca. It is prescribed by the sūtra: ऊर्ध्वो अग्निः सुमतिं वस्वो अश्रेदुषस्या नः सरस्वती जुषाणेति प्रउगं (*ūrdhvo agniḥ sumatiṃ vasvo aśreduṣasyā naḥ sarasvatī juṣāṇeti praugaṃ*) (Ā. 8-10).

### Printed p. 216

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning ऊर्ध्वो अग्निः (*ūrdhvo agniḥ*) is the sixth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has seven Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is its seer; the Viśvedevas are the deity; Triṣṭubh is the metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: ऊर्ध्वः सप्त वैश्वदेवं तु (*ūrdhvaḥ sapta vaiśvadevaṃ tu*). The use of the sūkta is by indication. When the prauga-śastra mantras are recited in the second chandoma, the three Ṛks beginning ऊर्ध्वो अग्निः (*ūrdhvo agniḥ*) are to be recited as the Vaiśvadeva tṛca, as elaborated by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra ऊर्ध्वो अग्निः सुमतिं वस्वो अश्रेदुषस्या नः सरस्वती जुषाणेति प्रउगं (*ūrdhvo agniḥ sumatiṃ vasvo aśreduṣasyā naḥ sarasvatī juṣāṇeti praugaṃ*) (Ā. 8-10).

Sūkta 39
Maṇḍala 7 · Anuvāka 3 · Sūkta 39 · Aṣṭaka 5 · Adhyāya 4 · Varga 6
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 7
Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Viśvedevas. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऊ॒र्ध्वो अ॒ग्निः सु॑म॒तिं वस्वो॑ अश्रेत्प्रती॒ची जू॒र्णिर्दे॒वता॑तिमेति ।  
> भे॒जाते॒ अद्री॑ र॒थ्ये॑व॒ पंथा॑मृ॒तं होता॑ न इषि॒तो य॑जाति ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ऊर्ध्वः । अग्निः । सुऽमतिं । वस्वः । अश्रेत् । प्रतीची । जूर्णिः । देवऽतातिं । एति ।  
> भेजाते इति । अद्री इति । रथ्याऽइव । पंथां । ऋतं । होता । नः । इषितः । यजाति ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निरंगनादिगुणविशिष्ट ऊर्ध्व उद्गमनः सन् वस्वो वासकस्य स्तोतुः सुमतिमस्मदीयां शोभनां स्तुतिमश्रेत् । श्रयतु । सेवतां । प्रतीच्यभिमुखी जूर्णिः सर्वासां प्रजानां जरयित्र्योदेवतातिं देवतातिं यज्ञमेति । गच्छति । अद्री
> आद्रियंतौ श्रद्धावंतौ पत्नीयजमानौ पंथां पंथानं यज्ञमार्गं रथ्येव कन्येव भेजाते । सेवेते । तथेषितः संप्रेषितो नोऽस्मदीयो होता ऋतं
> यज्ञं यजाति । यजतु । करोत्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, endowed with qualities such as the leading (aṅgana), rising upward, resorts to the good praise of us, the praiser who is the dweller (vasu); let him attend to it. Facing west, the one who ages all creatures goes to the sacrifice (देवतातिं (*devatātiṃ*)). The two revering, faithful ones, the wife and the sacrificer, resort to the path of the sacrifice like a maiden (रथ्येव (*rathyeva*)). Likewise our Hotṛ, sent forth, performs the sacrifice; the meaning is "let him do it".

### Printed p. 217

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni, the god
- ऊर्ध्वः (*ūrdhvaḥ*)—having appeared with face upward
- वस्वः (*vasvaḥ*)—of the praiser
- सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)—the excellent praise
- अश्रेत् (*aśret*)—may he accept
- प्रतीची (*pratīcī*)—she who faces us
- जूर्णिः (*jūrṇiḥ*)—the goddess Uṣas, who causes the decay of creatures
- देवतातिं (*devatātiṃ*)—to the sacrifice
- एति (*eti*)—comes
- अद्री (*adrī*)—the devout sacrificer and his wife
- पंथां (*paṃthāṃ*)—the path of the sacrifice
- रथ्येव (*rathyeva*)—like charioteers
- भेजाते (*bhejāte*)—they follow
- इषितः (*iṣitaḥ*)—directed
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- होता (*hotā*)—the Hotṛ
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—the sacrifice
- यजाति (*yajāti*)—let him perform

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let the god Agni, having appeared with face upward, accept the excellent praise of the praiser. The goddess Uṣas, who faces us and causes the decay of creatures, comes to the sacrifice. The devout sacrificer and his wife follow the path of the sacrifice like charioteers. Let our Hotṛ, directed by us, perform the sacrifice.

##### English translation
Let Agni, risen on high, accept the praise of the worshipper : she who makes (all creatures) old, looking to the west, goes to the sacrifice : the pious pair, like two raiders in a chariot, follow the path (of the ceremony) : let the Hotri, as enjoined, celebrate the rite.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र वा॑वृजे सुप्र॒या ब॒र्हिरे॑षा॒मा वि॒श्पती॑व॒ बीरि॑ट इयाते ।  
> वि॒शाम॒क्तोरु॒षसः॑ पू॒र्वहू॑तौ वा॒युः पू॒षा स्व॒स्तये॑ नि॒युत्वा॑न् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । ववृजे । सुप्रयाः । बर्हिः । एषां । आ । विश्पती इवेति विश्पतीऽइव । बीरिटे ।  
> इयाते इति ।  
> विशां । अक्तोः । उषसः । पूर्वहूतौ । वायुः । पूषा । स्वस्तये । नियुत्वान् ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एषां यजमानानां संबंधि सुप्रयाः शोभनान्नेन युक्तं बर्हिः कुशसमूहं प्र ववृजे । प्र वृज्यते । आसाद्यत इत्यर्थः । विश्पतीव । इवेतीदानीमर्थे ।
> इदानीमस्मदीयानां प्रजानां पालकौ नियुत्वान् । नियुच्छब्देन अश्वा उच्यंते ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The grass (बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)), the heap of kuśa belonging to these sacrificers and provided with good food (सुप्रयाः (*suprayāḥ*)), has been spread out (प्र ववृजे (*pra vavṛje*): it is set down, laid out). In विश्पतीव (*viśpatīva*) the word इव (*iva*) is used in the sense "now". The two protectors of our people, Vāyu "possessed of niyut" and Pūṣan: by the word नियुत् (*niyut*) horses are meant.

### Printed p. 218

> तद्वान् वायुः पूषा च विशां प्रजानां स्वस्तये क्षेमायाक्तो रात्रेः संबंधिन्या
> उषसः सकाशात्पूर्वहूतौ पूर्वस्मिन्नाह्वाने सति बीरिटेऽंतरिक्ष इयाते । आगच्छतां ।
> यद्वा । विश्पतीवेत्युपमा । विशां मनुष्याणां बीरिटे गणे विश्पतीव राजानौ यथा
> गच्छतां तद्वत् । अस्मिन्पक्षे विशामित्युभयत्र संबध्यते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Vāyu, who has them, and Pūṣan, for the welfare (स्वस्तये (*svastaye*)) of the people (विशां (*viśāṃ*)), when the earlier invocation (पूर्वहूतौ (*pūrvahūtau*)) takes place in advance of Uṣas, who belongs to the night (अक्तोः (*aktoḥ*)), let them come (इयाते (*iyāte*)) to the firmament (बीरिटे (*bīriṭe*)). Alternatively, विश्पतीव (*viśpatīva*) is a comparison: as two kings go in a host (बीरिटे गणे (*bīriṭe gaṇe*)) of men, so let they come. In this view विशां (*viśāṃ*) is connected with both places.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—of the sacrificers
- सुप्रयाः (*suprayāḥ*)—provided with excellent food
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—the grass seat
- प्र ववृजे (*pra vavṛje*)—has been spread
- विश्पतीव (*viśpatīva*)—like kings, protectors of the people
- नियुत्वान् वायुः (*niyutvān vāyuḥ*)—Vāyu, who has horses named niyut
- पूषा (*pūṣā*)—and the god Pūṣan
- विशां (*viśāṃ*)—of the people
- स्वस्तये (*svastaye*)—for the welfare
- अक्तोः (*aktoḥ*)—belonging to the night
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—than Uṣas
- पूर्वहूतौ (*pūrvahūtau*)—by reason of an invocation that comes earlier
- बीरिटे (*bīriṭe*)—in the firmament
- आ इयाते (*ā iyāte*)—may they graciously come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The grass seat of the sacrificers, furnished with excellent food, has been spread. Like kings who protect their people, let Vāyu, who has the horses called niyut, and the god Pūṣan graciously come to the firmament for the welfare of the people, by reason of an invocation that comes earlier (at dawn) than Uṣas, who belongs to the night.

##### English translation
The food-bestowing sacred grass of these (the worshippers) is strewn : may the two lords of people, Vayu, with the Niyut steeds, and Pushan, invoked before the dawn upon the close of the night, appear now in the firmament for the welfare of mankind.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

बीरिटे (*bīriṭe*)—The commentator has explained this word in two ways. He gives one meaning, "in the firmament", for बीरिटे (*bīriṭe*); and with विशां मनुष्याणां बीरिटे गणे विश्पतीव राजानौ (*viśāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bīriṭe gaṇe viśpatīva rājānau*), "like kings, protectors of the people, in the assembly of men or of subjects", he gives another meaning, "in the assembly".
Yāska has explained the word by giving a derivation:
बीरिटे । बीरिटं तैटीकिरंतरिक्षमेवमाह पूर्वं वेतेरुत्तरमीरतेर्वयांसीरंत्यस्मिन् भांसि वा । तदेतदस्यामृच्युदाहरंत्यपि निगमो भवति ॥ (*bīriṭe | bīriṭaṃ taiṭīkiraṃtarikṣamevamāha pūrvaṃ veteruttaramīratervayāṃsīraṃtyasmin bhāṃsi vā | tadetadasyāmṛcyudāharaṃtyapi nigamo bhavati ‖*) (Ni. 5-28)
That बीरिट (*bīriṭa*) means the firmament is the view of the teacher Taiṭīki. How? The first half of the word, the syllable बी (*bī*) or वी (*vī*), is derived from the root वे (*ve*), and the latter half, the part ईरट् (*īraṭ*), from the root ईर् (*īr*), so that it becomes वीरिटं (*vīriṭaṃ*) or बीरिटं (*bīriṭaṃ*). व (*va*) means birds; ईर् (*īr*) means "to move about". इरतेर्वयांसीरंत्यस्मिन् (*iratervayāṃsīraṃtyasmin*): since birds move about in it (the firmament)

### Printed p. 219
the meaning "firmament" for the word वीरिट (*vīriṭa*) or बीरिट (*bīriṭa*) is proper. Or भांसि वा (*bhāṃsi vā*): since the lights, the stars and the rest, move about in this firmament, shining, one may say that बीरिटं (*bīriṭaṃ*) means the firmament. To make this meaning clear, Yāska takes the present Ṛk itself as the example and gives its derivation:
प्रवृज्यते सुप्रायणं बर्हिरेषामेयाते सर्वस्य पातारौ वा पालयितारौ वा । बीरिटमंतरिक्षं भियो वा भासो वा तति । अपि वोपमार्थे स्यात् विश्पती इव राजानौ बीरिटे गणे मनुष्याणां रात्र्या विवासे पूर्वस्यामभिहूतौ वायुश्च नियुत्वान् पूषा च स्वस्त्ययनाय ॥ (*pravṛjyate suprāyaṇaṃ barhireṣāmeyāte sarvasya pātārau vā pālayitārau vā | bīriṭamaṃtarikṣaṃ bhiyo vā bhāso vā tati | api vopamārthe syāt viśpatī iva rājānau bīriṭe gaṇe manuṣyāṇāṃ rātryā vivāse pūrvasyāmabhihūtau vāyuśca niyutvān pūṣā ca svastyayanāya ‖*)
नियुत्वान् नियुतोऽस्याश्वाः नियुतो नियमनाद्वा नियोजनाद्वा ॥ (*niyutvān niyuto 'syāśvāḥ niyuto niyamanādvā niyojanādvā ‖*) (Ni. 5-28)
In the present Ṛk the deities Vāyu and Pūṣan are praised. These deities are protectors of all, that is, they guard everyone. For the coming of such deities the soft kuśa grass called बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*) is spread on the sacrificial altar as a seat. बीरिटं (*bīriṭaṃ*) means the firmament; because it is filled with fear or with light, बीरिटं (*bīriṭaṃ*) means the firmament. Or the word may also be used to indicate a comparison. In this sense of comparison, बीरिटे (*bīriṭe*) is to be taken as meaning गणे (*gaṇe*) (in a host). As kings, who protect all, shine, held in honour, when they come into a throng of men, so: thus the word बीरिट (*bīriṭa*) can be given the meaning "host" or "crowd". उषसः पूर्वहूतौ (*uṣasaḥ pūrvahūtau*) means रात्र्या विवासे पूर्वस्यामभिहूतौ (*rātryā vivāse pūrvasyāmabhihūtau*): in the last part of the night, that is, at the time of the appearing of Uṣas, may this god Vāyu and Pūṣan graciously come in the firmament to bring auspiciousness to the world. Here the word नियुत्वान् (*niyutvān*) means the god Vāyu, who has the horses called नियुत् (*niyut*). The horses of Vāyu are named नियुतः (*niyutaḥ*) (Ni. 5-28). This epithet belongs only to Vāyu, not to Pūṣan. As to the word नियुत (*niyuta*): नियमनाद्वा (*niyamanādvā*), because the going of this horse is subject to restraint (the charioteer's control), or नियोजनाद्वा (*niyojanādvā*), because this horse is yoked to the chariot. Therefore the name नियुत् (*niyut*) has come to Vāyu's horse: such is the intention of the derivation.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ज्म॒या अत्र॒ वस॑वो रंत दे॒वा उ॒रावं॒तरि॑क्षे मर्जयंत  ॒
> शु॒भ्राः ।

### Printed p. 220

> अर्वाक्पथ उरुज्रयः कृणुध्वं श्रोता दूतस्य जग्मुषो  
> नो अस्य ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ज्मयाः । अत्र । वसवः । रंत । देवाः । उरौ । अंतरिक्षे । मर्जयंत । शुभ्राः ।  
> अर्वाक् । पथः । उरुऽज्रयः । कृणुध्वं । श्रोत । दूतस्य । जग्मुषः । नः । अस्य ।

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वसवो वसुसंज्ञा देवा अत्रास्मिन्यज्ञे ज्मयाः पृथिव्यां भवा रंत । रमयंतां । उरौ विस्तीर्णेऽंतरिक्षे स्थिताः शुभ्रा दीप्यमाना मरुतश्च मर्जयंत ।
> परिचर्यंते । हे उरुज्रयः प्रभूतगमना वसवो मरुतश्च यूयं पथो
> युष्मदीयान्मार्गानर्वागस्मदभिमुखं यथा भवति तथा कृणुध्वं । कुरुत । अपि च
> यूयं जग्मुषो युष्मान्प्रति गतवतो नोऽस्मदीयस्यास्य दूतस्याग्नेराह्वानं
> श्रोत । शृणुत । अग्निर्हि यजमानानां दूतः सन्देवानाह्वयतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The gods called Vasus (वसवः (*vasavaḥ*)) are born on earth (ज्मयाः (*jmayāḥ*): those who exist on the earth) and let them delight (रंत (*raṃta*): let them cause delight) in this sacrifice. The shining (शुभ्राः (*śubhrāḥ*)) Maruts, who stand in the vast firmament, are served (मर्जयंत (*marjayaṃta*)). "O Vasus and Maruts, of wide-ranging speed (उरुज्रयः (*urujrayaḥ*)), make your paths turn towards us (अर्वाक् (*arvāk*)), and hear (श्रोत (*śrota*)) the call of this our messenger Agni, who has gone to you (जग्मुषः (*jagmuṣaḥ*))." The sense is that Agni, being the messenger of the sacrificers, summons the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वसवः (*vasavaḥ*)—those known as Vasus
- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—the gods
- अत्र (*atra*)—in this sacrifice
- ज्मयाः (*jmayāḥ*)—being born from the earth
- रंत (*raṃta*)—let them sport
- उरौ (*urau*)—in the vast
- अंतरिक्षे (*aṃtarikṣe*)—firmament
- शुभ्राः (*śubhrāḥ*)—the Maruts, who dwell in it and shine
- मर्जयंत (*marjayaṃta*)—are served
- उरुज्रयः (*urujrayaḥ*)—O swift-going Maruts, and Vasus
- पथः (*pathaḥ*)—to our path of sacrifice
- अर्वाक् (*arvāk*)—so as to face it
- कृणुध्वं (*kṛṇudhvaṃ*)—direct your course
- जग्मुषः (*jagmuṣaḥ*)—who is coming towards you
- नः (*naḥ*)—on our behalf
- अस्य दूतस्य (*asya dūtasya*)—this Agni, the messenger of the gods, his call
- श्रोत (*śrota*)—hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let the gods called Vasus, being on earth, sport in this sacrifice. The Maruts, who dwell in the vast firmament and shine, are served. O swift-going Maruts, and O Vasus, let your course be turned towards our sacrifice. Hear the call of this Agni, who is coming to you and is our messenger of the gods.

##### English translation
May the divine Vasus sport in this occasion upon the earth : the brilliant (Maruts) in the expansive firmament are being worshipped : swift-moving deities, direct your paths towards us : hear (the words) of this our messenger, (Agni), approaching to you.

### Printed p. 221

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk the Vasus and the Maruts are praised. On the question who the Vasus are, Yāska has given a derivation as follows:
वसवो यद्विवसते सर्वमग्निर्वसुभिर्वासव इति समाख्या तस्मात् पृथिवीस्थानाः । इंद्रो वसुभिर्वासव इति समाख्या तस्मान्मध्यस्थानाः । वसव आदित्यरश्मयो विवासनात्तस्माद्द्युस्थानाः ॥ (*vasavo yadvivasate sarvamagnirvasubhirvāsava iti samākhyā tasmāt pṛthivīsthānāḥ | iṃdro vasubhirvāsava iti samākhyā tasmānmadhyasthānāḥ | vasava ādityaraśmayo vivāsanāttasmāddyusthānāḥ ‖*) (Ni. 12-41)
The word वसवः (*vasavaḥ*) means those who cover and pervade everything. How? Since Agni is included in the group of deities called वसवः (*vasavaḥ*) (the eight Vasus), and Agni is a deity of the earth region, the Vasus have pervaded the earth. Further, since Indra is included in their group, and Indra is a deity of the middle region, the Vasus have pervaded the middle region (the firmament). Again, since the word वसवः (*vasavaḥ*) also means the rays of the sun, and the sun's rays pervade and illumine the whole of heaven, the Vasus have pervaded heaven, the region of the sky. Therefore, because these Vasus pervade all three regions, that is, the worlds earth, firmament and heaven, they have received the name वसवः (*vasavaḥ*). Yāska has given here, as examples, two mantras in which the word वसवः (*vasavaḥ*) is used: one is a mantra of the Vājasaneya Saṃhitā (the Śukla Yajurveda), the other is the present Ṛk. For this Ṛk too he has given a derivation:
ज्मया अत्र वसवोऽरमंत देवा ज्मा पृथिवी तस्यां भवा उरौ चांतरिक्षे मर्जयंत गमयंतः शुभ्राः शोभमाना अर्वाच एनान् पथो बहुजनाः कुरुध्वं शृणुत दूतस्य जग्मुषो नो अस्याग्नेः । (*jmayā atra vasavo 'ramaṃta devā jmā pṛthivī tasyāṃ bhavā urau cāṃtarikṣe marjayaṃta gamayaṃtaḥ śubhrāḥ śobhamānā arvāca enān patho bahujanāḥ kurudhvaṃ śṛṇuta dūtasya jagmuṣo no asyāgneḥ |*) (Ni. 12-43)
Let the Vasus who are on earth, joined with the earth, sport with joyful minds. Here ज्मा (*jmā*) means the earth (Ni. 2-5); those who are on that earth are the Vasus, and these Vasus also dwell in the vast firmament. They are also very bright. O swift-going Maruts and Vasus, come by this path that faces us. And hear the words, in the form of a call, of this Agni, who has taken on the role of messenger and is coming to you: such is the main intention of the derivation.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते हि य॒ज्ञेषु॑ य॒ज्ञिया॑स॒ ऊमाः॑ स॒धस्थं॒ विश्वे॑ अ॒भि संति॑  ॒
> देवाः ।  
> ता अ॑ध्व॒र उ॑श॒तो य॑क्ष्यग्ने श्रु॒ष्टी भगं॒ नास॑त्या॒ पुरं॑धिं ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 222

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । हि । यज्ञेषु । यज्ञियासः । ऊमाः । सधऽस्थं । विश्वे । अभि । संति । देवाः ।  
> तान् । अध्वरे । उशतः । यक्षि । अग्ने । श्रुष्टी । भगं । नासत्या । पुरंऽधिं ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यज्ञेषु यागेषु ते हि ते खलु प्रसिद्धा यज्ञियासो यज्ञार्हा ऊमा
> रक्षका विश्वे सर्वे देवाः सधस्थं सहस्थानमभि संति । अभिभवंति । आक्रामंति ॥ हे
> अग्ने अध्वरेऽस्मदीये यज्ञ उशतः कामयमानांस्तान् देवान्यक्षि । यज । तथा
> श्रुष्टी । क्षिप्रनामैतत् । क्षिप्रं भगमेतन्नामकं देवं नासत्या नासत्यावश्विनौ च
> पुरंधिं पुरूणां ध्यातारमिंद्रं च यज ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

At the sacrifices (यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)) those well-known (ते हि (*te hi*)) gods, all of them (विश्वे (*viśve*)), worthy of sacrifice (यज्ञियासः (*yajñiyāsaḥ*)) and protectors (ऊमाः (*ūmāḥ*)), come upon the common place (सधस्थं (*sadhasthaṃ*)) and take possession of it (अभि संति (*abhi saṃti*): they overcome, they occupy). "O Agni, at our sacrifice (अध्वरे (*adhvare*)) worship (यक्षि (*yakṣi*): offer to) those gods who desire it (उशतः (*uśataḥ*)). Likewise श्रुष्टी (*śruṣṭī*), a name meaning "quickly": quickly worship the god named Bhaga, the Nāsatyas, that is, the two Aśvins, and Purandhi, that is, Indra, the one who thinks of (provides for) many."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—at the times of our sacrifices
- ते हि (*te hi*)—those well-known
- यज्ञियासः (*yajñiyāsaḥ*)—worthy of worship
- ऊमाः (*ūmāḥ*)—and protecting
- विश्वे देवाः (*viśve devāḥ*)—all the gods
- सधस्थं (*sadhasthaṃ*)—the place of sacrifice
- अभिसंति (*abhisaṃti*)—occupy on all sides
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- अध्वरे (*adhvare*)—in our sacrifice
- उशतः (*uśataḥ*)—who desire the sacrifice
- तान् (*tān*)—those gods
- यक्षि (*yakṣi*)—worship
- श्रुष्टी (*śruṣṭī*)—quickly
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—the god Bhaga
- नासत्या (*nāsatyā*)—the Aśvin gods
- पुरंधिं (*puraṃdhiṃ*)—Indra: propitiate them

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

At the times of our sacrifices all the gods, well known, worthy of worship and protectors, occupy the place of sacrifice. O Agni, in our sacrifice worship those gods who desire the sacrifice. Quickly propitiate Bhaga, the Aśvin gods and Indra.

##### English translation
These universal adorable guardian deities occupy a common station at sacrifices : worship, Agni, those divinities, deservers (of oblations) at the ceremony, the swift Bhaga, the Nasatyas and Purandhi.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ गिरो॑ दि॒व आ पृ॑थि॒व्या मि॒त्रं व॑ह॒ वरु॑ण॒मिंद्र॑म॒ग्निं ।  
> आर्य॒मण॒मदि॑तिं॒ विष्णु॑मेषां॒ सर॑स्वती म॒रुतो॑ मादयंतां ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 223

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । अग्ने । गिरः । दिवः । आ । पृथिव्याः । मित्रं । वह । वरुणं । इंद्रं । अग्निं ।  
> आ । आर्यमणं । अदितिं । विष्णुं । एषां । सरस्वती । मरुतः । मादयंतां ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं दिवो द्युलोकात्सकाशाद्गिरो गरणीयान् स्तुत्यान्देवानस्मदीयं यज्ञं प्रत्या वह । आह्वानं कुरु । पृथिव्या अंतरिक्षाच्चा वह । कान्देवानिति
> तदुच्यते । मित्रमेतन्नामकं वरुणं चेंद्रं च देवेषु देवतारूपेणावस्थितं
> चाग्निमर्यमणमेतन्नामकं अदितिमदीनां पृथिवीं च विष्णुं च । एवंभूतान्देवानेषामस्माकं यजमानानामर्थाया वह । सरस्वती वाग्देवता च मरुतश्च मादयंतां । अस्मदीयैः स्तोत्रैर्हविर्भिश्च माद्यंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, from the world of heaven bring to our sacrifice the praiseworthy gods; bring them also from the earth and the atmosphere. Which gods? Mitra and Varuna, Indra, and Agni who abides among the gods in the form of a deity, Aryaman, Aditi (the earth, according to the gloss) and Viṣṇu. Bring such gods for us, the sacrificers. Let Sarasvatī, the goddess of speech, and the Maruts be delighted with our praises and oblations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—from the world of heaven
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—the gods worthy of praise
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring to our sacrifice
- पृथिव्याः (*pṛthivyāḥ*)—from the earth also
- आ (*ā*)—bring
- मित्रं (*mitraṃ*)—Mitra
- वरुणं (*varuṇaṃ*)—Varuṇa
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—the god Agni
- आर्यमणं (*āryamaṇaṃ*)—Aryaman
- अदितिं (*aditiṃ*)—the earth
- विष्णुं (*viṣṇuṃ*)—Viṣṇu
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—for us the sacrificers
- आ (*ā*)—bring
- सरस्वती (*sarasvatī*)—the goddess Sarasvatī
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Marut deities
- मादयंतां (*mādayaṃtāṃ*)—let them be satisfied with our sacrificial oblations

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, bring to our sacrifice the gods worthy of praise from the world of heaven. Bring them from the earth too. Bring Mitra, Varuṇa, Indra, the god Agni, Aryaman, the earth and Viṣṇu for us the sacrificers. May the goddess Sarasvatī and the Marut deities be satisfied with our oblations.

##### English translation
Bring, Agni, whether from heaven or earth, the adorable deities, Mitra, Varuna, Indra and Agni, Aryaman, Aditi and Vishnu, (for the good) of these (worshippers) : and may Saraswati and Maruts be delighted (by our offerings).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> र॒रे ह॒व्यं म॒तिभि॑र्य॒ज्ञिया॑नां॒ नक्ष॒त्कामं॒ मर्त्या॑ना॒मसि॑न्वन् ।  
> धाता॑ र॒यिम॑विद॒स्यं स॑दा॒सां स॑क्षी॒महि॒ युज्ये॑भि॒र्नु दे॒वैः ॥

### Printed p. 224

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ररे । हव्यं । मतिऽभिः । यज्ञियानां । नक्षत् । कामं । मर्त्यानां । असिन्वन् ।  
> धात । रयिं । अविदस्यं । सदासां । सक्षीमहि । युज्येभिः । नु । देवैः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यज्ञियानां । चतुर्थ्यर्थे षष्ठी । यज्ञार्हेभ्यो देवेभ्यो मतिभिरस्मदीयाभिः स्तुतिभिः सह हव्यं हवी ररे । अस्माभिर्दीयते । मर्त्यानां मनुष्याणामस्माकं काममभिलाषमसिन्वन्नप्रतिबद्धन्नग्निर्नक्षत् । अस्मदीयं यज्ञं व्याप्नोतु ॥ हे देवाः यूयमविदस्यं अनुपक्षयणीयं सदासां सर्वदा संभजनीयं
> रयिं धनं धात । अस्मभ्यं दत्त । न्वद्य वयं युज्येभिः सहायभूतैरिह यज्ञे
> समागतैर्देवैः सक्षीमहि । यद्वा । न्वित्युपमार्थे । युज्येभिर्नु बंधुभिरिव देवैः
> सक्षीमहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

यज्ञियानां (*yajñiyānāṃ*) stands in the genitive in the sense of the dative: to the gods worthy of sacrifice the oblation is given by us together with our praises. Agni, not obstructing the desire of us mortals, is to reach (pervade) our sacrifice. O gods, give us wealth that does not waste away and is always to be shared; today may we be united with the gods who have assembled here at the sacrifice as helpers. Or else: नु (*nu*) is in the sense of comparison, so "may we be united with the gods as with kinsmen."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यज्ञियानां (*yajñiyānāṃ*)—to the gods worthy of worship
- मतिभिः (*matibhiḥ*)—accompanied by our praises
- हव्यं (*havyaṃ*)—the oblation
- ररे (*rare*)—is offered
- मर्त्यानां (*martyānāṃ*)—of us the sacrificers
- कामं (*kāmaṃ*)—the desired object
- असिन्वन् (*asinvan*)—Agni, who does not obstruct
- नक्षत् (*nakṣat*)—let him be united with our sacrifice
- (O gods,) अविदस्यं (*avidasyaṃ*)—imperishable
- सदासां (*sadāsāṃ*)—always worshipful
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- धात (*dhāta*)—give us
- नु (*nu*)—now
- युज्येभिः (*yujyebhiḥ*)—assembled at the sacrifice
- देवैः (*devaiḥ*)—with the gods
- सक्षीमहि (*sakṣīmahi*)—we shall be united

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

To the gods worthy of worship an oblation accompanied by our praises is offered. May Agni, who does not obstruct the desire of us the sacrificers, be united with the sacrifice. O gods, give us wealth that is imperishable and always worshipful. We shall now be united with the gods assembled at the sacrifice.

##### English translation
The oblation is offered together with praises to the adorable deities : may (Agni), unaverse to the desire of mortals, be present : bestow (upon us, gods), unwasting, all-benefiting riches ; and may we to-day be associated with the assembled deities.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू रोद॑सी अ॒भिष्टु॑ते॒ वसि॑ष्ठैरृ॒तावा॑नो॒ वरु॑णो मि॒त्रो  ॒
> अ॒ग्निः ।

### Printed p. 225

> यच्छंतु चंद्रा उपमं नो अर्कं यूयं पात स्वस्तिभिः  
> सदा नः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । रोदसी इति । अभिष्टुते इत्यभिस्तुते । वसिष्ठैः । ऋतावानः । वरुणः । मित्रः ।  
> अग्निः ।  
> यच्छंतु । चंद्राः । उपमं । नः । अर्कं । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> न्वद्य रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ वसिष्ठैरस्माभिः । पूजार्थं बहुवचनं । अभिष्टुते अभितः सर्वतः स्तुते अभूतां । तथा ऋतावानोऽस्माभिः क्रियमाणैर्यज्ञैरुपेता वरुणो मित्रोऽग्निश्चैवंभूता देवा अस्माभिरभिष्टुता आसन् । चंद्रा आह्लादका देवा नोऽस्मभ्यमर्कमर्चनीयमन्नमुपमं सर्वोत्कृष्टं यच्छंतु ।
> ददतु । सूक्ते प्रतिपादिता ये देवास्ते सर्वे यूयं नोऽस्मान् स्वस्तिभिरविनाशैः
> सदा सर्वदा पात । पालयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Now heaven and earth are praised on all sides by us the Vasiṣṭhas (the plural is for honour). Likewise Varuṇa, Mitra and Agni, endowed with the sacrifices performed by us, have been praised by us. May the gladdening gods give us excellent, most exalted food worthy of worship. All you gods celebrated in the sūkta, protect us always with blessings that do not perish.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नु (*nu*)—now
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth
- वसिष्ठैः (*vasiṣṭhaiḥ*)—by us who bear the name Vasiṣṭha
- अभिष्टुते (*abhiṣṭute*)—are praised
- ऋतावानः (*ṛtāvānaḥ*)—endowed with sacrifice
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—and Agni are praised by us
- चंद्राः (*caṃdrāḥ*)—all the gladdening gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- उपमं (*upamaṃ*)—most excellent
- अर्कं (*arkaṃ*)—food
- यच्छंतु (*yacchaṃtu*)—let them give
- (All gods,) यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Heaven and earth are praised by us who bear the name Vasiṣṭha. Varuṇa, Mitra and Agni, endowed with sacrifice, are praised by us. May all the gladdening gods give us most excellent food. O all gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
Heaven and earth are now glorified by the Vashithas, as are Varuna, the object of worship, and Mitra and Agni : may they, the conferrers of joy, bestow upon us excellent food : and do you (all) ever cherish us with blessings.

### Printed p. 226
## Sūkta 40 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ओ श्रुष्टिरिति सप्तर्चं सप्तमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं वैश्वदेवं । ओ
> श्रुष्टिरित्यनुक्रांतं । विनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seventh sūkta, ओ श्रुष्टिः (*o śruṣṭiḥ*), of seven Ṛks, has Vasiṣṭha as seer, the Viśvedevas as deity, and Triṣṭubh as metre. It is so listed in the Anukramaṇī as ओ श्रुष्टिः (*o śruṣṭiḥ*). The application (viniyoga) is by the indication of the text (लैंगिकः (*laiṃgikaḥ*)).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta ओ श्रुष्टिः (*o śruṣṭiḥ*) is the seventh sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has seven Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is its seer; the Viśvedevas are the deity; Triṣṭubh is the metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is given as ओ श्रुष्टिः (*o śruṣṭiḥ*). The application is by indication.

Sūkta 40. Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 3; Sūkta 40. Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 4; Varga 6. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 7. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Viśvedevas. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ओ श्रु॒ष्टिर्वि॑द॒थ्या॒३॒॑ ४ समे॑तु॒ प्रति॒ स्तोमं॑ दधीमहि तु॒राणां॑ ।  
> यद॒द्य दे॒वः स॑वि॒ता सु॒वाति॒ स्यामा॑स्य र॒त्निनो॑ विभा॒गे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ओ इति । श्रुष्टिः । विदथ्या । सं । एतु । प्रति । स्तोमं । दधीमहि । तुराणां ।  
> यत् । अद्य । देवः । सविता । सुवाति । स्याम । अस्य । रत्निनः । विभागे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवाः विदथ्या विदथेन त्वदीयेन चित्रेण संपाद्या श्रुष्टिः सुखमस्मानो
> आ समेतु । आगच्छतु । अथवा श्रुष्टिर्वेगवती विदथ्या विदथे यज्ञे क्रियमाणास्मदीया स्तुतिर्युष्मानागच्छतु । वयं तुराणां वेगवतां देवानां स्तोमं स्तोत्रं
> प्रति दधीमहि । कुर्वीमहि । अद्येदानीं सविता देवो यद्धनं सुवाति अस्मभ्यं प्रेरयेत् रत्निनो रमणीयधनवतोऽस्य सवितुस्तस्य धनस्य विभागे दाने स्याम
> वयं भवेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, may the satisfaction (श्रुष्टिः (*śruṣṭiḥ*)) brought about by your wondrous rite come to us easily. Or else: may our swift praise, made at the sacrifice, come to you. We offer praise to the swift gods. May we have a share in the distribution of the delightful wealth that the god Savitṛ today sends to us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O gods) विदथ्या (*vidathyā*)—our praise composed at the time of sacrifice
- श्रुष्टिः (*śruṣṭiḥ*)—swiftly
- ओ समेतु (*o sametu*)—let it reach you (or: let the pleasures given by you come to us)
- तुराणां (*turāṇāṃ*)—of the mighty gods, praising them
- स्तोमं (*stomaṃ*)—the hymn
- प्रति दधीमहि (*prati dadhīmahi*)—we perform
- अद्य (*adya*)—now
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the divine
- सविता (*savitā*)—Savitṛ
- यत् (*yat*)—whatever wealth
- सुवाति (*suvāti*)—he provides for us
- रत्निनः (*ratninaḥ*)—attractive
- अस्य (*asya*)—of that same wealth
- विभागे (*vibhāge*)—in the giving
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be worthy

### Printed p. 227

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, may our praise composed at the time of sacrifice swiftly reach you. We compose a hymn praising the mighty gods. Whatever wealth the divine Savitṛ provides for us, may we be worthy of the giving of that same attractive wealth.

##### English translation
May the satisfaction derived from pious rites come to us as we contemplate the glorification of the swift-moving (divinities): may we be included in the apportionment by that wealth-bestowing deity (of the riches) which the divine Savitri to-day distributes.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मि॒त्रस्तन्नो॒ वरु॑णो॒ रोद॑सी च॒ द्युभ॑क्त॒मिंद्रो॑ अर्य॒मा  ॑
> ददातु ।  
> दिदे॑ष्टु दे॒व्यदि॑ती॒ रेक्णो॑ वा॒युश्च॒ यन्नि॑यु॒वैते॒ भग॑श्च ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मित्रः । तत् । नः । वरुणः । रोदसी इति । च । द्युभक्तं । इंद्रः । अर्यमा ।  
> ददातु ।  
> दिदेष्टु । देवी । अदितिः । रेक्णः । वायुः । च । यत् । नियुवैते इति नियुवैते ।  
> भगः । च ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मित्रो देवो नोऽस्मभ्यं तत्प्रसिद्धं धनं ददातु । प्रयच्छतु । तथा वरुणो ददातु । रोदसी च द्यावापृथिव्यौ च दत्तां । तथेंद्रो द्युभक्तं दैवैर्मोदमानैः स्तोतृभिः सेवितं तद्धनं ददातु । अर्यमा च ददातु । तथादितिर्देवी
> रेक्णो धनं दिदेष्टु । तत् अस्मभ्यं दिशतु । वायुश्च भगश्चेत्युभौ देवौ यद्धनं
> नियुवैते अस्मान्नितरां योजयेतां तद्धनमिति पूर्वेण संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May the god Mitra give us that well-known wealth; so may Varuṇa; so may heaven and earth. Likewise may Indra give that wealth which is द्युभक्तं (*dyubhaktaṃ*), enjoyed by praisers who rejoice with the gods; and may Aryaman give it. So may the goddess Aditi bestow that wealth, दिदेष्टु (*dideṣṭu*), direct it to us. The wealth which both gods, Vāyu and Bhaga, would join to us firmly: that wealth is connected with what precedes.

### Printed p. 228

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—the god Mitra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- तत् (*tat*)—the well-known wealth
- ददातु (*dadātu*)—let him give
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—let Varuṇa also give
- रोदसी च (*rodasī ca*)—let heaven and earth also give
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- द्युभक्तं (*dyubhaktaṃ*)—let him give wealth enjoyed by brilliant praisers
- अर्यमा (*aryamā*)—let Aryaman also provide
- वायुश्च (*vāyuśca*)—Vāyu
- भगश्च (*bhagaśca*)—and the god Bhaga
- यत् (*yat*)—whatever wealth
- नियुवैते (*niyuvaite*)—they provide to us
- रेक्णः (*rekṇaḥ*)—that same wealth
- देवी (*devī*)—the divine
- अदितिः (*aditiḥ*)—Aditi
- दिदेष्टु (*dideṣṭu*)—let her provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the god Mitra give us the well-known wealth. May Varuṇa, heaven and earth, Indra and Aryaman also give us wealth fit to be enjoyed by the praisers. The wealth which Vāyu and the god Bhaga provide for us, that same wealth may the divine Aditi provide.

##### English translation
May Mitra and Varuna, heaven and earth, Indra and Aryaman, give us that (wealth) which is merited by brilliant (laudations) : may the divine Aditi be disposed to give us riches, which Vayu and Bhaga may preserve ever in our keeping.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सेदु॒ग्रो अ॑स्तु मरुतः॒ स शु॒ष्मी यं मर्त्यं॑ पृषदश्वा॒ अवा॑थ ।  
> उ॒तेम॒ग्निः सर॑स्वती जु॒नंति॒ न तस्य॑ रा॒यः प॑र्ये॒तास्ति॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । इत् । उग्रः । अस्तु । मरुतः । सः । शुष्मी । यं । मर्त्यं । पृषत्अश्वाः ।  
> अवाथ ।  
> उत् । ईं । अग्निः । सरस्वती । जुनंति । न । तस्य । रायः । परिएता । अस्ति ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे पृषदश्वाः । पृषच्छब्देन केचिन्मृगविशेषा उच्यंते । त एवाश्वा वाहा
> येषां ते । एवंभूता हे मरुतो रुद्रपुत्रा देवा यूयं मर्त्यं मरणधर्माणं यं यजमानमवाथ पालयथ स एव यजमान उग्रोऽस्तु । ओजस्वी
> भवतु । तथा स शुष्मी बलवान् भवतु । तथोतापि चाग्निरंगनादिगुणयुक्तो देवो

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maruts, sons of Rudra, whose horses (पृषदश्वाः (*pṛṣadaśvāḥ*)) are drawn from certain spotted deer (the word पृषत् (*pṛṣat*) denotes certain kinds of deer, and these are their draught animals): whatever mortal sacrificer you protect, may that very sacrificer be mighty (उग्रः (*ugraḥ*), vigorous), and may he be powerful. Likewise Agni, endowed with such qualities as his body, a god, … **NOTE**

### Printed p. 229
#### Ṛk 3 (continued)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सरस्वती वाग्देवता चेत्याद्याः सर्वे देवा ईमेनं यजमानं जुनंति । प्रवर्तयंति । तस्य यजमानस्य संबंधिनो रायो धनस्य कश्चिदपि पर्येता परिगंता नास्ति
> नाशको न भवतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sarasvatī, the goddess of speech, and all the other deities impel (set in motion) this sacrificer. No one at all is a "circumvent­er" of that sacrificer's wealth, that is, there is no one who destroys it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पृषदश्वाः (*pṛṣadaśvāḥ*)—those having deer-formed horses
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- मर्त्यं (*martyaṃ*)—the human
- यं (*yaṃ*)—whichever sacrificer
- अवाथ (*avātha*)—you protect
- सः इत् (*saḥ it*)—that very one
- उग्रः (*ugraḥ*)—being exceedingly valorous
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let him be
- सः (*saḥ*)—he
- शुष्मी (*śuṣmī*)—let him be endowed with strength
- उत (*uta*)—and
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—the god Agni
- सरस्वती (*sarasvatī*)—the goddess Sarasvatī
- ईं (*īṃ*)—this sacrificer
- जुनंति (*junaṃti*)—they stimulate
- तस्य (*tasya*)—of this sacrificer
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—to the wealth
- पर्येता (*paryetā*)—a plunderer
- न अस्ति (*na asti*)—there is none

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, whose horses are of deer form, whichever human sacrificer you protect, let him be exceedingly valorous and let him be endowed with strength. Agni and Sarasvatī stimulate him. No one will be a plunderer of this sacrificer's wealth.

##### English translation
Maruts, whose steeds are the spotted deer, may the mortal whom you protect be resolute, be strong, for him Agni and Saraswati also defend, and there be no despoiler of his riches.
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं हि ने॒ता वरु॑ण ऋ॒तस्य॑ मि॒त्रो राजा॑नो अर्य॒मापो॒ धुः ।  
> सु॒हवा॑ दे॒व्यदि॑तिरन॒र्वा ते नो॒ अंहो॒ अति॑ पर्ष॒न्नरि॑ष्टान् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । हि । नेता । वरुणः । ऋतस्य । मित्रः । राजानः । अर्यमा । अप । धुरिति धुः ।  
> सुहवा । देवी । अदितिः । अनर्वा । ते । नः । अंहः । अति । पर्षन् । अरिष्टान् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋतस्य यज्ञस्य सत्यस्य वा नेता प्रापयितायं ह्ययं खलु वरुणश्च मित्रश्चार्यमा चैते राजानः समर्था देवा अपोऽस्मदीयं यज्ञादिलक्षणं कर्म धुः ।
> अधुः । धूधूति । अनर्वा केनाप्यप्रतिगता देवी द्योतमानादितिरदीना देवमाता सु-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Varuṇa is the leader, the conveyer, of the rite or of truth. Varuṇa, Mitra and Aryaman, these sovereign, capable gods, carry on our work consisting of sacrifice and the like (धुः (*dhuḥ*) is read as अधुः (*adhuḥ*), from the root धू (*dhū*)). The goddess Aditi, shining, unopposed by anyone, not poor, mother of the gods, is of good invocation (सुहवा (*suhavā*))

### Printed p. 230

> हवा शोभनाह्वाना भवति । ते वरुणादयो देवा अरिष्टानबाधितान् सतो नोऽस्मानंहो दुरितादति पर्षन् । अतिपारयंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

She is of fair calling. May those gods, Varuṇa and the rest, carry us, unharmed, beyond sin (अंहः (*aṃhaḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice
- नेता (*netā*)—the director
- अयं हि वरुणः (*ayaṃ hi varuṇaḥ*)—this Varuṇa
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- अर्यमा (*aryamā*)—Aryaman
- राजानः (*rājānaḥ*)—all these lordly deities
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—this our rite
- धुः (*dhuḥ*)—they accomplish
- अनर्वा (*anarvā*)—the unharmed
- देवी (*devī*)—the divine
- अदितिः (*aditiḥ*)—Aditi
- सुहवा (*suhavā*)—is worthy of excellent invocation
- ते (*te*)—all these gods, Varuṇa and the rest
- अरिष्टान् (*ariṣṭān*)—unharmed
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- अंहः (*aṃhaḥ*)—from sin
- अति पर्षन् (*ati parṣan*)—may they protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Varuṇa, the director of the sacrifice, and Mitra and Aryaman, all these lordly deities, carry out this our sacrificial rite. Aditi, the unharmed and divine, is worthy of excellent invocation. May all these gods, Varuṇa and the rest, protect us, unharmed, from sin.

##### English translation
This Varuna, the leader of the rite, and the royal Mitra and Aryaman, uphold my acts, and the divine unopposed Aditi, earnestly invoked : may they convey us safe beyond evil.
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्य दे॒वस्य॑ मी॒ळ्दुषो॑ व॒या विष्णो॑रे॒षस्य॑ प्रभृ॒थे ह॒विर्भिः॑ ।  
> वि॒दे हि रु॒द्रो रु॒द्रियं॑ महि॒त्वं या॑सि॒ष्टं व॒र्तिर॑श्विना॒ विरा॑वत् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । देवस्य । मीळ्दुषः । वयाः । विष्णोः । एषस्य । प्रभृथे । हविर्भिः ।  
> विदे । हि । रुद्रः । रुद्रियं । महित्वं । यासिष्टं । वर्तिः । अश्विना । इरावत् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रभृथे हविर्भिर्हवीरूपैरन्नैरेषस्य प्रापणीयस्य मीळ्दुषः कामानां
> सेक्तुर्विष्णोः सर्वदेवात्मकस्यास्य देवस्य । विष्णुः सर्वा देवताः । ऐ. ब्रा. १-१ ।
> इति श्रुतेः । अस्य देवा वयाः शाखा इव भवंति । रुद्रो देवो रुद्रियं रुद्रसंबंधि सुखं महित्वं महत्त्वं च विदे हि । अस्मान् प्रापयति खलु । अपि च हे अश्विनौ

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"At the offering (प्रभृथे (*prabhṛthe*)) with oblations", that is, with food in the form of oblations, of this god Viṣṇu, the one to be approached, the showerer of desires, who is the soul of all gods. By the śruti विष्णुः सर्वा देवताः (*viṣṇuḥ sarvā devatāḥ*) (Ai. Brā. 1-1), the gods are like his branches. The god Rudra procures (विदे (*vide*)) for us Rudra's happiness and greatness. And, O Aśvins, you two gods

### Printed p. 231

> देवौ युवामिरावद्वर्ल्क्षणान्नयुक्तं वर्तिरस्मदीयं गृहं यूसिष्टं । आयूसिष्टं आगच्छतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

come to our house furnished with food (इरावत् (*irāvat*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रभृथे (*prabhṛthe*)—offered
- हविर्भिः (*havirbhiḥ*)—by means of oblations
- एषस्य (*eṣasya*)—of the one approached
- मीळ्हुषः (*mīḷhuṣaḥ*)—the showerer of desires
- देवस्य (*devasya*)—of the god
- अस्य विष्णोः (*asya viṣṇoḥ*)—to this Viṣṇu
- वयोः (*vayoḥ*)—all the other gods are indeed in the form of branches **NOTE**
- रुद्रः (*rudraḥ*)—the god Rudra
- रुद्रियं (*rudriyaṃ*)—the happiness pertaining to Rudra
- महित्वं (*mahitvaṃ*)—and the greatness
- विदे हि (*vide hi*)—let him procure for us
- अश्विनौ (*aśvinau*)—O Aśvins
- इरावत् (*irāvat*)—furnished with oblation
- वर्तिः (*vartiḥ*)—to our house
- यासिष्टं (*yāsiṣṭaṃ*)—be gracious to come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

To this Viṣṇu, who is served by the offering of oblations, who showers desires and is divine in nature, all the other gods are as branches. May the god Rudra procure for us the happiness and the greatness that pertain to Rudra. O Aśvins, be pleased to come to our house furnished with oblations.

##### English translation
I propitiate with oblations the ramifications of that divine attainable Vishuu, the showerer of benefits : Rudra. bestow upon us the maguificence of his nature : the Aswins have come to our dwelling abounding with (sacrificial) food.
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मात्र॑ पूषन्नाघृण इरस्यो॒ वरू॑त्री॒ यद्रा॑ति॒षाच॑श्च॒ रास॑न् ।  
> म॒यो॒भुवो॑ नो॒ अर्वं॑तो॒ नि पां॑तु वृ॒ष्टिं परि॑ज्मा॒ वातो॑ ददातु ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । अत्र । पूषन् । आघृणे । इरस्यः । वरूत्री । यत् । रातिसाचः । च । रासन् ।  
> मयःभुवः । नः । अर्वंतः । नि । पांतु । वृष्टिं । परिज्मा । वातः । ददातु ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे आघृणे प्राप्तदीप्ते एवंभूत हे पूषन् देव अत्रस्मिन्दाने इरस्यः । विघातं मा कृथाः । वरूत्री सर्वैर्वरणीया सरस्वती रातिषाचश्च । रातिदानं । तस्य

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Pūṣan, radiant one (आघृणे (*āghṛṇe*)), do not cause obstruction (इरस्यः (*irasyaḥ*)) in this giving. Sarasvatī, who is to be chosen by all (वरूत्री (*varūtrī*)), and the gods' wives, givers of bounty (रातिसाचः (*rātisācaḥ*)), the gift

### Printed p. 232

> संभक्त्र्यै देवपत्न्यैश्च यद्धनं रासन् अस्मभ्यं प्रयच्छेयुः । अत्र मा कृथा इति पूर्वेण संबंधः । किंच मयोभुवः सुखस्य भावका अर्वंतो गच्छंतो देवा नोऽस्मान्नि पांतु । नितरां पालयंतु । परिज्मा परितो गंता वातो वायुर्वृष्टिं वृष्टिलक्षणमुदकं ददातु । प्रयच्छत्वस्मभ्यं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

of that wealth which the goddesses' consort-companions would give us: "do not cause (obstruction) here" connects with what precedes. Moreover, may the gods, bringers of happiness (मयोभुवः (*mayobhuvaḥ*)), who go (अर्वंतः (*arvaṃtaḥ*)), protect us completely. May Vāta, the wind that moves all around (परिज्मा (*parijmā*)), give us rain, water in the form of rain.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आघृणे (*āghṛṇe*)—O radiant one
- पूषन् (*pūṣan*)—O god Pūṣan
- वरूत्री (*varūtrī*)—Sarasvatī, worthy of the regard of all
- रातिषाचश्च (*rātiṣācaśca*)—and the generous-giving wives of the gods
- यत् (*yat*)—whatever wealth
- रासन् (*rāsan*)—they give us
- अत्र (*atra*)—to this act of giving
- मा इरस्यः (*mā irasyaḥ*)—do not cause obstacles
- मयोभुवः (*mayobhuvaḥ*)—the givers of happiness
- अर्वंतः (*arvaṃtaḥ*)—who come to us, the gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- नि पांतु (*ni pāṃtu*)—may they protect fully
- परिज्मा (*parijmā*)—moving everywhere
