- वातः (*vātaḥ*)—the wind
- वृष्टिं (*vṛṣṭiṃ*)—water in the form of rain
- ददातु (*dadātu*)—may he supply

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O radiant Pūṣan, do not obstruct the act of giving by which Sarasvatī, worthy of the regard of all, and the generous wives of the gods give us wealth. May the gods, givers of happiness, who come to us, protect us fully. May the wind, which moves everywhere, supply water in the form of rain.

##### English translation
Resplendent Pushan, oppose not (hindrance)on this occasion : may the protectress, (Saraswati), and the liberal (wives of the gods), grant us wealth: may the over-moving deities, the sources of happiness, protect us : may the circumambient Vata send us rain.
#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू रोद॑सी अ॒भिष्टु॑ते॒ वसि॑ष्ठैर्ऋ॒तावा॑नो॒ वरु॑णो मि॒त्रो अ॒ग्निः ।  
> यच्छं॑तु चं॒द्रा उ॑प॒मं नो॑ अ॒र्कं यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । रोदसी इति । अभिस्तुते इत्यभिस्तुते । वसिष्ठैः । ऋतऽवानः । वरुणः । मित्रः । अग्निः ।  
> यच्छंतु । चंद्राः । उपमं । नः । अर्कं । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥

### Printed p. 233

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वं व्याख्यातेयं । अक्षरार्थस्तु । द्यावापृथिव्यौ वरुणादयो देवाश्च वसिष्ठैरस्माभिरभिष्टुता भवंति । एवंभूता आह्लादका देवाः सर्वोत्कृष्टमन्नमस्मभ्यं ददतु । अस्मिन् सूक्ते प्रतिपादिताः सर्वे देवा यूयं कल्याणैरस्मान्सर्वदा पालयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This (Ṛk) was explained before. The literal sense: heaven and earth and the gods Varuṇa and the rest are praised by us, the Vasiṣṭhas. May such gods, givers of joy, give us the most excellent food. May all the gods celebrated in this sūkta, you, always protect us with blessings.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नु (*nu*)—now
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth
- वसिष्ठैः (*vasiṣṭhaiḥ*)—by us called Vasiṣṭhas
- अभिष्टुते (*abhiṣṭute*)—are praised
- ऋतावानः (*ṛtāvānaḥ*)—those possessed of sacrifice
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—and Agni are praised by us
- चंद्राः (*caṃdrāḥ*)—all the gods, givers of delight
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- उपमं (*upamaṃ*)—most excellent
- अर्कं (*arkaṃ*)—food
- यच्छंतु (*yacchaṃtu*)—let them give
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you (O gods)
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Heaven and earth are now praised by us, called Vasiṣṭhas. Varuṇa, Mitra and Agni, who possess the sacrifice, are praised by us. May all the gods, givers of delight, give us the most excellent food. O gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
Heaven and earth are now glorified by the Vasishthas, as are Varuna the object of worship, and Mitra and Agni : may they, the conferrers of joy, bestow upon us excellent food : an o you (all) ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 41 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रातरग्निमिति सप्तर्चमष्टमं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । प्रातर्भागं जगत्याद्या लिंगोक्तदेवतांत्योपस्येति । वसिष्ठ ऋषिः । आद्या जगती शिष्टास्त्रिष्टुभः । आद्याग्नींद्रादिदेवत्या द्वितीयाद्याः पंच भगदेवत्याः सप्तम्युषोदेवत्या । अत्र
> केचिदाहुः ।   भगसूक्तं  ।  क्षिप्रं
> स्तूयते । ऋग्वि. ७-४१ । इति ॥
> 30

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta प्रातरग्निं (*prātaragniṃ*) has seven Ṛks and is the eighth sūkta. The Anukramaṇikā says: प्रातर्भागं जगत्याद्या लिंगोक्तदेवतांत्योपस्येति (*prātarbhāgaṃ jagatyādyā liṃgoktadevatāṃtyopasyeti*). The seer is Vasiṣṭha. The first Ṛk is Jagatī, the rest Triṣṭubh. The first has Agni, Indra and others as deity; the five from the second have Bhaga; the seventh has Uṣas. Some say here: *bhagasūktaṃ ... kṣipraṃ stūyate* (Ṛgvi. 7-41).

### Printed p. 234
The Anuvāda: This sūkta प्रातरग्निं (*prātaragniṃ*) is the eighth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has seven Ṛks. The Anukramaṇikā says प्रातर्भागं जगत्याद्या लिंगोक्तदेवतास्त्योषस्या (*prātarbhāgaṃ jagatyādyā liṃgoktadevatāstyoṣasyā*). Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; its first Ṛk is in the Jagatī metre; the remaining Ṛks are in Triṣṭubh. The first Ṛk has Agni, Indra and others as deity; the five Ṛks following the second have Bhaga; the seventh Ṛk is addressed to Uṣas. As to the application (viniyoga) of this sūkta, some say:
निवेष्टुकामो रोगार्तो भगसूक्तं जपेत्सदा । निवेशं विशति क्षिप्रं रोगैश्च परिमुच्यते ॥ (*niveṣṭukāmo rogārto bhagasūktaṃ japetsadā | niveśaṃ viśati kṣipraṃ rogaiśca parimucyate ‖*) (Ṛgvidhāna 2-25)
One who wishes to enter a new region, or one afflicted by disease, who constantly recites this Bhaga-sūkta, will, as he wishes, be freed from disease and quickly enter the new region: thus the fruit-statement (phalaśruti) of this sūkta is given.
Sūkta—41
Maṇḍala—7 ‖ Anuvāka—3 ‖ Sūkta—41 ‖
Aṣṭaka—5 ‖ Adhyāya—4 ‖ Varga—8 ‖
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta—7 ‖
Ṛṣi: Vasiṣṭha ‖
Deity: 1. the deities named by gender. 2-6. Bhaga. 7. Uṣas ‖
Metre: 1. Jagatī. 2-7. Triṣṭubh ‖
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रा॒तर॒ग्निं प्रा॒तरिंद्रं॑ हवामहे प्रा॒तर्मि॒त्रावरु॑णा प्रा॒तर॒श्विना॑ ।  
> प्रा॒तर्भगं॑ पू॒षणं॒ ब्रह्म॑ण॒स्पतिं॑ प्रा॒तः सोम॑मु॒त रु॒द्रं हु॑वेम ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रातः । अग्निं । प्रातः । इंद्रं । हवामहे । प्रतः । मित्रावरुणा । प्रातः । अश्विना ।  
> प्रतः । भगं । पूषणं । ब्रह्मणः । पतिं । प्रतः । सोमं । उत । रुद्रं । हुवेम ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रातरुषःकालेऽग्निं देवं हवामहे । वयं स्तोतार आह्वयामः । तथा प्रातःकाल इंद्रं हवामहे । तथा मित्रावरुणा मित्रावरुणावहोरात्राभिमानिनौ

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

At dawn, the time of Uṣas, we, the praisers, invoke the god Agni. Likewise at dawn we invoke Indra. Likewise Mitra and Varuṇa, Mitra and Varuṇa being the presiding deities of day and night

### Printed p. 235

> देवौ प्रातर्वयं हवामहे । तथाश्विनौ देवानां भिषजौ प्रातर्वयं हवामहे । तथा प्रातर्भगं देवं पूषणं देवं ब्रह्मणस्पतिं मंत्राभिमानिनमेतत्संज्ञकं चाह्वयामः । तथा प्रातः सोममेतत्संज्ञकं देवमुतापि च रुद्रं देवं च हुवेम आह्वयामः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sentence continues from the previous page. The gods are Mitra and Varuṇa, who preside over day and night, and the two Aśvins, the physicians of the gods. We invoke them at dawn. We also invoke at dawn Bhaga, Pūṣan, and Brahmaṇaspati, the deity of the sacred formula (mantra), who is known by that name. Likewise we invoke Soma, so named, and Rudra, the god.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—at this dawn time
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we call
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—we call Indra also
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—at dawn
- मित्रावरुणा (*mitrāvaruṇā*)—we call Mitra and Varuṇa
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin deities
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—we call at the morning time
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—Bhaga also
- पूषणं (*pūṣaṇaṃ*)—the god Pūṣan also
- ब्रह्मणस्पतिं (*brahmaṇaspatiṃ*)—we call Brahmaṇaspati also
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—at the morning time
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—the god Soma also
- उत (*uta*)—and
- रुद्रं (*rudraṃ*)—Rudra also
- हुवेम (*huvema*)—we call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

At this dawn time we call Agni, Indra, Mitra and Varuṇa, and the Aśvin deities. In this very morning we also pray to and call Bhaga, the god Pūṣan, Brahmaṇaspati, the god Soma, and Rudra.

##### English translation
We invoke at dawn Agni : at dawn Indra : at dawn Mitra and Varuna : at dawn the Ashwins : at dawn Bhaga, Pushan, Brahmanaspathi : at dawn Soma and Rudra.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Although the word Bhaga and Ṛks praising the deity Bhaga occur here and there, the present sūkta (Ṛ. Saṃ. 7-41) is the only whole sūkta devoted to the deity Bhaga; other deities too are praised in this sūkta. The name of this Bhaga is recited about 60 times. The word Bhaga here means "giver, one who distributes". In this sense the word is used twenty times, generally as an epithet of the deity Savitṛ. In all these sūktas the word is conceived as "one who distributes wealth". When the generosity of Indra, Agni and other deities is praised, it is customary to compare them to Bhaga. The word Bhaga also means fortune, prosperity, wealth; in this sense too the word is used in about 20 places. In the second Ṛk of this sūkta the deity Bhaga is called the distributor, and men express the wish that they too may share in it. Elsewhere (Ṛ. Saṃ. 5-46-6) there is a prayer that he distribute immense wealth to his devotees.
It is said that the goddess Uṣas is the sister of Bhaga (Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-123-5) and that the eye of Bhaga is adorned with rays (Ṛ. Saṃ. 3-34-14). Yāska (Ni. 12-13) has said that Bhaga is the lord of the morning time.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रा॒त॒र्जितं॒ भग॑मु॒ग्रं हु॑वेम व॒यं पु॒त्रमदि॑ते॒र्यो वि॑ध॒र्ता ।  
> आ॒ध्रश्चि॒द्यं मन्य॑मानस्तु॒रश्चि॒द्राजा॑ चि॒द्यं भगं॑ भ॒क्षीत्याह॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रातःजितं । भगं । उग्रं । हुवेम । वयं । पुत्रं । अदितेः । यः । विधर्ता ।  
> आध्रः । चित् । यं । मन्यमानः । तुरः । चित् । राजा । चित् । यं । भगं । भक्षि । इति । आह ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो भगो देवो विधर्ता विश्वस्य जगतो धारको जितं जयशीलमुग्रमुद्गूर्णमदितेः पुत्रं भगं देवं प्रातःकाल एव वयं हुवेम । आह्वयामः । आध्रश्चिद्दरिद्रोऽपि स्तोता यं भगं देवं मन्यमानः स्तुवन् भगं भजनीयं धनं भक्षि भज विभज मह्यं देहीत्याह ब्रवीति । तुरश्चित् । तुरतिर्गतिकर्मा । प्राप्तधनोऽपि राजा चित् समर्थोऽपि जनो यं भगं देवं भजनीयं धनं मह्यं भक्षि देहीत्याह । तं भगं प्रातरेव वयं हुवेमेति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Bhaga is the sustainer (विधर्ता (*vidhartā*)) of the whole world, victorious (जितं (*jitaṃ*)) and fierce (उग्रं (*ugraṃ*), "uplifted"), the son of Aditi. We invoke this god Bhaga at dawn itself. आध्रश्चित् (*ādhraścit*) means even a poor worshipper who, praising (मन्यमानः (*manyamānaḥ*)) a god, says "भक्षि (*bhakṣi*)", that is, "share out, give me the wealth to be shared (भगं (*bhagaṃ*))". तुरश्चित् (*turaścit*): तुरतिर्गतिकर्मा (*turatirgatikarmā*), the root tur denotes motion; here it means one who has obtained wealth. Even a king (राजा चित् (*rājā cit*)), a man of power, says to that god Bhaga, "give me the wealth to be shared". The construction is: that Bhaga we invoke at dawn.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आध्रश्चित् (*ādhraścit*)—the worshipper who is poor
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Bhaga
- मन्यमानः (*manyamānaḥ*)—praising
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—the venerable wealth
- भक्षि (*bhakṣi*)—give to me
- इति (*iti*)—thus
- आह (*āha*)—he begs
- तुरश्चित् (*turaścit*)—the wealthy man also
- राजा चित् (*rājā cit*)—even the lord
- यं (*yaṃ*)—such a one as would beg of that very Bhaga
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- विधर्ता (*vidhartā*)—who is the sustainer of the whole world
- प्रातर्जितं (*prātarjitaṃ*)—victorious at dawn
- उग्रं (*ugraṃ*)—possessed of great strength
- अदितेः (*aditeḥ*)—of Aditi
- पुत्रं (*putraṃ*)—the son
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—the god Bhaga
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—at this dawn
- हुवेम (*huvema*)—we call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Whether the worshipper is poor or a lord, in whatever condition, he prays to the venerable Bhaga alone, "Give me venerable wealth". We, at this dawn, pray to and call that god Bhaga to whom they so pray: who is the sustainer of the whole world, victorious, possessed of great strength, and the son of Aditi.

##### English translation
We invoke at dawn the victorious fierce Bhaga, the son of Aditi; who is the sustainer (of the world), to whom the poor man praising him applies

### Printed p. 237

saying, give (me wealth), to whom the opulent prince (addresses the same prayer).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Yāska has given the Nirukta for this Ṛk—
प्रातर्जितं भगमुग्रं ह्वयेम वयं पुत्रमदितेर्यो विधारयिता सर्वस्याधृक्षिद्यं मन्यमान आध्राश्चिद्दरिद्रस्तुरश्चित् । तुर इति यमनाम तरतेर्वा त्वरतेर्वा त्वरया तूर्णगतिर्यमो राजा चिद्यं भगं भक्षीत्याह ॥ (*prātarjitaṃ bhagamugraṃ hvayema vayaṃ putramaditeryo vidhārayitā sarvasyādhṛkṣidyaṃ manyamāna ādhrāściddaridrasturaścit | tura iti yamanāma taratervā tvaratervā tvarayā tūrṇagatiryamo rājā cidyaṃ bhagaṃ bhakṣītyāha ‖*)
अंधो भग इत्याहुरनुो न दृश्यते । प्राशित्रमस्याक्षिणी निर्जघानेति च ब्राह्मणं । जनं भगो गच्छतीति वा विज्ञायते जनं गच्छत्यादित्य उदयेन ॥ (*aṃdho bhaga ityāhuranuo na dṛśyate | prāśitramasyākṣiṇī nirjaghāneti ca brāhmaṇaṃ | janaṃ bhago gacchatīti vā vijñāyate janaṃ gacchatyāditya udayena ‖*) (Ni. 12-14)
We invoke at this dawn the deity named Bhaga, who is victorious in the morning, mighty, the son of Aditi, and who upholds and protects everything. Whether poor or rich, one should pray to this Bhaga, meditating on him, for the obtaining of wealth, saying "Give wealth". The word तुरः (*turaḥ*) and the word यम (*yama*) are words of the same meaning. This word तुरः (*turaḥ*) may be derived either from the root तरति (*tarati*), meaning "cross", or from the root त्वरति (*tvarati*), meaning "hasten". Yama is swift of movement. A king too begs this god Bhaga, "Give me venerable wealth".
Some say that this Bhaga is blind (*aṃdhaḥ*): that is, this god is not seen by anyone (Bhaga in the sense of the Sun). Before the sun has risen this god is visible to no one. In this matter there is the Brāhmaṇa sentence प्राशित्रमस्याक्षिणी निर्जघान (*prāśitramasyākṣiṇī nirjaghāna*): one named Prāśitra destroyed the two eyes of this god; this too serves as an example. It is to be understood that this Bhaga (fortune, prosperity) goes to men; that is, Bhaga or Āditya, after rising, goes to the people by means of his rays. This is the intention of the etymology.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> भग॒ प्रणे॑त॒र्भग॒ सत्य॑राधो॒ भगे॒मां धिय॒मुद॑वा॒ दद॑न्नः ।  
> भग॒ प्रणो॑ जनय॒ गोभि॒रश्वै॒र्भग॒ प्र नृभि॑र्नृ॒वंतः॑ स्याम ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> भग । प्रनेतरिति प्रनेतः । भग । सत्यराधः । भग । इमां । धियं । उत् । अव ।  
> ददत् । नः ।  
> भग । प्र । नः । जनय । गोभिः । अश्वैः । भग । प्र । नृभिः । नृवंतः । स्याम ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 238

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे भग देव त्वं प्रणेता प्रकर्षेण नेतासि । तादृश प्रणेतर्हे भग त्वं सत्यराधः सत्यधनोऽसि । तादृश सत्यराधो हे भग त्वं नोऽस्मभ्यं ददत् कामान्प्रयच्छन्निमामस्मदीयां धियं स्तुतिमुदव । उद्रक्ष । सफलयुक्तां कुरु । हे भग त्वं गोभिरश्वैश्च नोऽस्मान् प्र जनय । प्रोद्भूतान् कुरु । हे भग त्वत्प्रसादाद्वयं नृभिर्नेतृभिः पुत्रादिभिर्नृवंतो मनुष्यवंतः प्र स्याम । प्रभवेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O god Bhaga, you are the leader (प्रणेता (*praṇetā*)), one who leads forth. As such a leader, O Bhaga, you are the possessor of true wealth (सत्यराधः (*satyarādhaḥ*)). O Bhaga of true wealth, granting (ददत् (*dadat*)) us our desires, protect (उदव (*udava*), "guard up") this our praise (धियं (*dhiyaṃ*)) and make it fruitful. O Bhaga, cause us to be born forth (प्र जनय (*pra janaya*)) with cattle and horses, that is, make us prosperous. O Bhaga, by your grace may we be rich in men (नृवंतः (*nṛvaṃtaḥ*)), that is, rich in leaders (नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)), such as sons, and may we prevail.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रनेतः (*pranetaḥ*)—O leader, the best guide
- भग (*bhaga*)—O god Bhaga
- (त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you)
- सत्यराधः (*satyarādhaḥ*)—you are possessed of wealth that is true in nature
- भग (*bhaga*)—O god Bhaga
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- ददत् (*dadat*)—granting our desires
- इमां धियं (*imāṃ dhiyaṃ*)—this praise of ours
- उदव (*udava*)—make fruitful
- भग (*bhaga*)—O god Bhaga
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with cattle
- अश्वैः (*aśvaiḥ*)—and with horses
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- प्र जनय (*pra janaya*)—make us born forth
- भग (*bhaga*)—O Bhaga
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—together with sons and others
- नृवंतः (*nṛvaṃtaḥ*)—possessed of a retinue
- प्र स्याम (*pra syāma*)—may we be of excellent standing

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O god Bhaga, best of guides, you are possessed of wealth that is true in nature. O Bhaga, granting us our desires, make this our praise fruitful. Make us abound in cattle and horses. Favour us so that we are filled with a retinue of sons and others.

##### English translation
Bhaga chief leader of rites, Bhaga faithful promiser of wealth, Bhaga granting (our wishes), fructify this ceremony, enrich us with cattle and horses : may we, Bhaga, be eminent with male decendants and followers.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒तेदानीं॒ भग॑वंतः स्यामो॒त प्र॑पि॒त्व उ॒त मध्ये॒ अह्नां॑ ।  
> उ॒तोदि॑ता मघव॒न्सूर्य॑स्य व॒यं दे॒वानां॑ सुम॒तौ स्या॑म ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । इदानीं । भगवंतः । स्याम । उत । प्रपित्वे । उत । मध्ये । अह्नां ।  
> उत । उत्इता । मघवन् । सूर्यस्य । वयं । देवानां । सुमतौ । स्याम ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 239

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उतापि चेदानीं वयं भगवंतः स्याम । हे भग भगेन त्वया स्वामिना युक्ता भवेम । यद्वा । भगवंतो धनवंतः स्याम । उतापि च प्रपित्वेऽह्नां प्राप्ते पूर्वाह्णे भगवंतः स्याम । उतापि चाह्नां दिवसानां मध्ये मध्याह्ने भगवंतः स्याम । उतापि च हे मघवन्धनवन्भग देव सूर्यस्य सर्वस्य प्रेरकस्य देवस्योदितोदितावुदये सति वयं त्वदनुग्रहाद्देवानामिंद्रादीनां सुमतावनुग्रहबुद्धौ स्याम । भवेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Now may we be blessed with Bhaga (भगवंतः (*bhagavaṃtaḥ*)): O Bhaga, may we be united with you as lord. Or else: may we be possessed of wealth. Also in the forenoon (प्रपित्वे (*prapitve*)), when the day has set in, may we be fortunate. Also in the middle of the days (*ahnāṃ madhye*), at midday, may we be fortunate. And, O Maghavan, O wealthy god Bhaga, at the rising (उदिता (*uditā*)) of the Sun, the impeller of all, may we by your favour be in the good will (सुमतौ (*sumatau*)), the gracious disposition, of the gods, Indra and the others.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उत (*uta*)—and
- इदानीं (*idānīṃ*)—now
- (वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we)
- भगवंतः (*bhagavaṃtaḥ*)—may we become worthy of the favour of the venerable god Bhaga.) Let us be possessed of wealth. **NOTE**
- उत (*uta*)—and
- प्रपित्वे (*prapitve*)—let us be fortunate in the forenoon
- उत (*uta*)—and
- अह्नां (*ahnāṃ*)—of the day
- मध्ये (*madhye*)—may we be wealthy in the middle time
- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Bhaga, the wealthy
- सूर्यस्य (*sūryasya*)—of the Sun
- उदिता (*uditā*)—at the time of rising
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—of the gods, Indra and others
- सुमतौ (*sumatau*)—in the gracious disposition
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Throughout the day let us gain the favour of the god Bhaga. O god Bhaga, grant that we become wealthy both in the forenoon and at midday. At the time of sunrise, cause us to gain the favour of Indra and the other deities.

##### English translation
May we now have Bhaga (for our lord), whether in the forenoon or at mid-day, or at sun-rise : may we, Maghavan, enjoy the favour of the gods.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> भग॑ ए॒व भग॑वा अस्तु देवा॒स्तेन॑ व॒यं भग॑वंतः स्याम ।  
> तं त्वा॑ भग॒ सर्व॒ इज्जो॑हवीति॒ स नो॑ भग पुरए॒ता भ॑वे॒ह ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> भगः । एव । भगवान् । अस्तु । देवाः । तेन । वयं । भगवंतः । स्याम ।  
> तं । त्वा । भग । सर्वः । इत् । जोहवीति । सः । नः । भग । पुरएता । भव । इह ॥

### Printed p. 240

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवाः भगो देव एव भगवान् धनवानस्तु । तेन भगेन देवेन धनेन वा वयं भगवंतः स्याम । धनवंतो भवेम । हे भग तं प्रसिद्धं त्वा त्वां सर्व इत्सर्व एव जनो जोहवीति । भृशं पुनःपुनराह्वयति । हे भग देव स त्वमिहास्मिन्यज्ञे नोऽस्माकं पुरएता पुरोगंता भव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, let the god Bhaga alone be possessed of wealth (भगवान् (*bhagavān*)). By that Bhaga, the god, or by wealth, may we be possessed of wealth. O Bhaga, you, that famous one, every single man calls (जोहवीति (*johavīti*)) again and again, intensely. O god Bhaga, may you in this sacrifice be our leader who goes before (पुरएता (*puraetā*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—O gods
- भग एव (*bhaga eva*)—the god Bhaga alone
- भगवान् (*bhagavān*)—as possessor of wealth
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let him be
- तेन (*tena*)—by the favour of that Bhaga
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- भगवंतः (*bhagavaṃtaḥ*)—possessed of wealth
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be
- भग (*bhaga*)—O god Bhaga
- तं (*taṃ*)—the famous
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- सर्व इत् (*sarva it*)—all people
- जोहवीति (*johavīti*)—call again and again
- भग (*bhaga*)—O god Bhaga
- सः (*saḥ*)—such you
- इह (*iha*)—in this sacrifice of ours
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- पुरएता (*puraetā*)—as guide
- भव (*bhava*)—be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, let the god Bhaga alone be wealthy. By the favour of Bhaga may we too become wealthy. O god Bhaga, all people call you, the famous one, again and again. O god Bhaga, be our guide in this sacrifice of ours.

##### English translation
May Bhaga, gods, be the possessor of opulence, and through him, may we be possessed of wealth, every one verily repeatedly invokes you, Bhaga : Bhaga, be our proceder at this solemnity.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सम॑ध्व॒रायो॒षसो॑ नमंत दधि॒क्रावे॑व॒ शुच॑ये प॒दाय॑ ।  
> अ॒र्वा॒ची॒नं व॑सु॒विदं॒ भगं॑ नो॒ रथ॑मि॒वाश्वा॑ वा॒जिन॒ आ व॑हंतु ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सं । अध्वराय । उषसः । नमंत । दधिक्रावाऽइव । शुचये । पदाय ।  
> अर्वाचीनं । वसुविदं । भगं । नः । रथम्ऽइव । अश्वाः । वाजिनः । आ । वहंतु ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शुचये शुद्धाय गमनयोग्याय पदाय स्थानाय । दधिक्रावेवाश्वो यथा । तथोषस उषोदेवता अध्वरायास्मदीयाय यागाय सं नमंत । संगच्छंतु ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

शुचये (*śucaye*) means for the pure place (पदाय (*padāya*)) fit to be gone to. As the horse Dadhikrā does, so may the Uṣas deities bow (नमंत (*namaṃta*)) together to our sacrifice (अध्वराय (*adhvarāya*)); that is, may they come together.

### Printed p. 241

> वाजिनो वेगवंतोऽश्वा रथमिव रथं यथा तथोषसोऽर्वाचीनमस्मदभिमुखं
> वसुविदं धनस्य प्रापकं भगं देवं नोऽस्मान्प्रत्या वहंतु । आनयंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Just as swift horses (वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*)) draw a chariot, so may the Dawns bring to us the god Bhaga, who is turned towards us (अर्वाचीनं (*arvācīnaṃ*)) and is the finder and bestower of wealth (वसुविदं (*vasuvidaṃ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शुचये (*śucaye*)—pure
- पदायॆ (*padāyè*)—for the obtaining of a place
- दधिक्रावेव (*dadhikrāveva*)—like a horse
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the Dawn-goddesses
- अध्वरायॆ (*adhvarāyè*)—to our sacrifice
- सं नमंतॆ (*saṃ namaṃtè*)—let them come and join
- वाजिनः (*vājinaḥ*)—swift-going
- अश्वाः (*aśvāḥ*)—horses
- रथमिव (*rathamiva*)—as they draw a chariot
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the Dawns
- अर्वाचीनं (*arvācīnaṃ*)—him who is turned towards us
- वसुविदं (*vasuvidaṃ*)—the bestower of wealth
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—the god Bhaga
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ वहंतु (*ā vahaṃtu*)—let them bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

For the obtaining of a pure place, may the Dawn-goddesses, who are like a horse, come and join our sacrifice. As swift horses draw a chariot, may the Dawns bring to us the god Bhaga, who is turned towards us and is the bestower of wealth.

##### English translation
May the Dawns come to our sacrifice as a horse to a suitable station :
as rapid steeds convey a chariot, so may the Dawns bring to us Bhaga, down-descending, charged with riches.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अश्वा॑वती॒र्गोम॑तीर्न उ॒षासो॑ वी॒रव॑तीः॒सद॑मुच्छंतु भ॒द्राः  
> घृ॒तं दुहा॑ना वि॒श्वतः॒ प्रपी॑ता यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अश्वऽवतीः । गोमतीः । नः । उषसः । वीरवतीः । सदं । उच्छंतु । भद्राः ।  
> घृतं । दुहानाः । विश्वतः । प्रपीताः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> भद्रा भजनीया उषस उषोदेवता अश्वावतीरश्ववत्योऽश्वसहिताः सश्च्यो गोमतीर्गोमत्यश्च वीरवतीर्वीरवत्यः पुत्रादिजनोपेताश्च भवंत्यो नोऽस्मभ्यं सदं सर्वदोच्छंतु । व्युच्छंतु । नैशं तमो विवासयंतु । कीदृश्यः । घृतमुदकं दुहानाः सिंचंत्यो विश्वतः सर्वगुणैः प्रसीताः प्रवृद्धाः । एवंभूता उषसस्तमु उच्छंतु । अस्मिन् सूक्ते प्रतिपादिता हे सर्वे देवाः यूयं नोऽस्मान् सदा सर्वदा स्वस्तिभिः कल्याणैः पाते । पालयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The auspicious, adorable Dawns, possessing horses (अश्वऽवतीः (*aśva 'vatīḥ*)), cattle (गोमतीः (*gomatīḥ*)) and heroes, that is, sons and other people, are asked to dawn for us always, driving away the night's darkness. They are further described as pouring out water (घृतं (*ghṛtaṃ*) is water), and as grown full with all qualities. Then, addressing all the gods praised in this sūkta, he says: "You, protect us always with blessings."

### Printed p. 242

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- घृतं (*ghṛtaṃ*)—water in the form of rain
- दुहानाः (*duhānāḥ*)—those who milk (it) forth
- विश्वतः (*viśvataḥ*)—with all qualities
- प्रपीताः (*prapītāḥ*)—those who are nourished
- भद्राः (*bhadrāḥ*)—those who bring auspiciousness
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the Dawn-goddesses
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अश्ववतीः (*aśvavatīḥ*)—as givers of horses
- गोमतीः (*gomatīḥ*)—as givers of cattle
- वीरवतीः (*vīravatīḥ*)—as givers of heroic sons and others; being so,
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- सदं (*sadaṃ*)—always
- उच्छंतु (*ucchaṃtu*)—let them destroy the darkness
- (O gods) यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पातॆ (*pātè*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the Dawn-goddesses, who milk forth water in the form of rain, who are nourished with all qualities and who bring auspiciousness, give us horses, cattle and heroic sons and others, and always destroy the darkness for us. O gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
May the auspicious Dawn ever break, bestowing horses and cattle and male descendants, shedding water, and endowed with all good things : and do you ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 42 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्र ब्रह्माण इति षडृचं नवमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं वैश्वदेवं । अत्रानुक्रांतं । प्र ब्रह्माणः षड्वैश्वदेवं त्विति ॥ सूक्तविनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥ तृतीये छंदोमे प्रउगशस्त्रे प्र ब्रह्माण इति वैश्वदेवस्तृचः । सूत्रितं च । प्र ब्रह्माणो अंगिरसो नक्षंत सरस्वतीं देवयंतो हवंते । आ. ८-११ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning प्र ब्रह्माणः (*pra brahmāṇaḥ*) has six Ṛks and is the ninth sūkta. Its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its deity the Viśvedevas, its metre Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī says: प्र ब्रह्माणः षड्वैश्वदेवं तु (*pra brahmāṇaḥ ṣaḍvaiśvadevaṃ tu*). The use of the sūkta is by indication (लैंगिकः (*laiṃgikaḥ*)). In the Prauga-śastra of the third Chandoma day, the three Ṛks beginning प्र ब्रह्माणः (*pra brahmāṇaḥ*) form the Vaiśvadeva tṛca. It is also given in the sūtra: प्र ब्रह्माणो अंगिरसो नक्षंत सरस्वतीं देवयंतो हवंते (*pra brahmāṇo aṃgiraso nakṣaṃta sarasvatīṃ devayaṃto havaṃte*) (Ā. 8-11).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning "प्र ब्रह्माणः (*pra brahmāṇaḥ*)" is the ninth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. Its seer is Vasiṣṭha; its deity the Viśvedevas; its metre Triṣṭup. In the Anukramaṇī it is said: प्र ब्रह्माणः षड्वैश्वदेवं तु (*pra brahmāṇaḥ ṣaḍvaiśvadevaṃ tu*). The use of the sūkta is by indication. While reciting the mantras of the Prauga-śastra in the third Chandoma, the three Ṛks beginning "प्र ब्रह्माणः (*pra brahmāṇaḥ*)" are to be recited as the Vaiśvadeva tṛca; this is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, in the sūtra प्र ब्रह्माणो अंगिरसो नक्षंत सरस्वतीं देवयंतो हवंते (*pra brahmāṇo aṃgiraso nakṣaṃta sarasvatīṃ devayaṃto havaṃte*) (Ā. 8-11).

### Printed p. 243

- Sūkta—42
- Maṇḍala—7; Anuvāka—3; Sūkta—42
- Aṣṭaka—5; Adhyāya—4; Varga—9
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta—6
- Ṛṣi—Vasiṣṭha
- Deity—Viśvedevas
- Metre—Triṣṭup

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र ब्र॒ह्माणो॒ अंगि॑रसो नक्षंत॒ प्र क्रं॑द॒नुर्॑भ॒न्य॑स्य वेतु ।  
> प्र धे॒नव॑ उद॒प्रुतो॑ नवंत यु॒ज्याता॒मद्री॑ अध्व॒रस्य॒ पेशः॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । ब्रह्माणः । अंगिरसः । नक्षंत । प्र । क्रंदनुः । नभन्यस्य । वेतु ।  
> प्र । धेनवः । उदप्रुतः । नवंत । युज्यातां । अद्री इति । अध्वरस्य । पेशः ॥ १

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ब्रह्माणोंगिरस एतन्नामका ऋषयः प्र नक्षंते । सर्वत्र व्याप्नुवंतु
> क्रंदनुः पर्जन्यो नभन्यस्य स्तोत्रस्यास्मदीयं स्तोत्रं प्र वेतु । प्रकर्षेणेच्छतु ।
> धेनवः प्रीणयित्र्यो नद्य उदप्रुत उदकानि सिंचंत्यः प्र नवंते । सर्पंतु । अद्री
> आद्रियंतौ पत्नीयजमानावध्वरस्य यज्ञस्य पेशो रूपं युज्यातां । योजयेतां ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seers called Brahmāṇas and Aṅgirases are to pervade everywhere. Krandanu, that is Parjanya, is to desire our praise (स्तोत्रस्य (*stotrasya*)) eagerly. The Dhenus, the nourishing rivers, pouring out waters (उदप्रुतः (*udaprutaḥ*)), are to flow forth. The two who honour the sacrifice (अद्री (*adrī*)), the wife and the sacrificer, are to be joined with the form (पेशः (*peśaḥ*)) of the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ब्रह्माणः (*brahmāṇaḥ*)—the Brahma-speakers
- अंगिरसः (*aṃgirasaḥ*)—Aṅgirases
- प्र नक्षंते (*pra nakṣaṃte*)—let them pervade everywhere
- क्रंदनुः (*kraṃdanuḥ*)—Parjanya
- नभन्यस्य (*nabhanyasya*)—our praise
- प्र वेतु (*pra vetu*)—let him desire fully
- धेनवः (*dhenavaḥ*)—the satisfying rivers
- उदप्रुतः (*udaprutaḥ*)—pouring out full streams
- प्र नवंते (*pra navaṃte*)—let them flow forth
- अद्री (*adrī*)—the wife and the sacrificer, who honour the sacrifice
- अध्वरस्य (*adhvarasya*)—of the sacrifice
- पेशः (*peśaḥ*)—the form
- युज्यातां (*yujyātāṃ*)—(having attained it) let them serve

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the Aṅgirases, the Brahma-speakers, pervade everywhere. May Parjanya desire our praise fully. May the satisfying rivers flow forth, pouring out full streams. May the wife and the sacrificer, who honour the sacrifice, become one with the form of the sacrifice and serve it.

### Printed p. 244

##### English translation
May the Brahmanas, the Angirasas, be every-where present : may Krandanu be conscious of (our) adoration : may the rivers glide along, distributing water : may the pious couple, (the Yajamana and his wife), conjointly appreciate the beauty of the sacrifice.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सु॒गस्ते॑ अग्ने॒ सन॑वित्तो॒ अध्वा॑ यु॒क्ष्वा सु॒ते ह॒रितो॑ रो॒हित॑श्च ।  
> ये वा॒ सद्म॑न्नरु॒षा वी॑र॒वाहो॑ हु॒वे दे॒वानां॒ जनि॑मानि स॒त्तः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सुगः । ते । अग्ने । सनवित्तः । अध्वा । युंक्ष्व । सुते । हरितः । रोहितः । च ।  
> ये । वा । सद्मन् । अरुषाः । वीरवाहः । हुवे । देवानां । जनिमानि । सत्तः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सनवित्तः सनाच्चिरकालादारभ्य लब्धस्ते त्वदीयोऽध्वा मार्गः सुगः
> सुष्ठु गंतव्यो भवतु । किंच हरितः श्यामवर्णा रोहितश्च लोहितवर्णाश्चेत्युभयुविधा ये वा ये च ते त्वदीया अश्वाः सद्मन् सद्मनि यज्ञगृहे वीरवाहो वीरं
> शूरं त्वां वहंतोऽरुषा आरोचमाना भवंति तांश्च त्वं सु युक्ष्व । त्वदीये
> रथे सुष्ठु संयोजय । निसत्तो यज्ञगृहे निषण्णोऽहं होता सन् देवानामिंद्रादीनां जनिमा जनान् संघान्हुवे । आह्वयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, your path, obtained (सनवित्तः (*sanavittaḥ*)) from long ago, is to be easy to travel. Further, the two kinds of your horses, the dark (हरितः (*haritaḥ*)) and the red (रोहितः (*rohitaḥ*)), which shine as they carry you, the hero, in the sacrificial house: yoke them well to your chariot. Seated (निसत्तः (*nisattaḥ*)) in the sacrificial house as the Hotṛ, I call the hosts of the gods, Indra and the rest.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सनवित्तः (*sanavittaḥ*)—obtained from long ago
- ते (*te*)—your
- अध्वा (*adhvā*)—path
- सुगः (*sugaḥ*)—is fit for excellent travelling
- सुते (*sute*)—at the time of the pressing of Soma
- हरितः (*haritaḥ*)—dark-coloured
- रोहितश्च (*rohitaśca*)—and golden-coloured
- ये वा (*ye vā*)—those horses which
- सद्मन् (*sadman*)—in the sacrificial house
- वीरवाहः (*vīravāhaḥ*)—are carriers of heroes
- अरुषाः (*aruṣāḥ*)—and attractive; those
- युक्ष्व (*yukṣva*)—yoke to the chariot
- सत्तः (*sattaḥ*)—I, seated in the sacrificial house
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—of the gods, Indra and others
- जनिमानि (*janimāni*)—the host
- हुवे (*huve*)—call to the sacrifice

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, your path, obtained from long ago, is fit for excellent travelling. At the time of the pressing of Soma, yoke to the chariot those dark-coloured and golden-coloured horses which in the sacrificial house are carriers of heroes and attractive. I, seated in the sacrificial house, call the host of the gods, Indra and the rest, to the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 245

##### English translation
Pleasant, Agni, be your long-familiar path : yoke for the libation the day, the ruddy horses, who brilliant-shining, are the conveyers of (you), the hero, to the hall of sacrifice, where seated, I invoke the companion of the gods.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> समु॑ वो य॒ज्ञं म॑हय॒न्नमो॑भिः॒ प्र होता॑ मं॒द्रो रि॑रिच उपा॒के ।  
> यज॑स्व॒ सु पु॑र्वणीक दे॒वाना य॒ज्ञिया॑म॒रम॑तिं ववृत्याः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सं । ऊं इति । वः । यज्ञं । महयन् । नमःभिः । प्र । होता । मंद्रः । रिरिचे । उपाके ।  
> यजस्व । सु । पुरुअनीक । देवान् । आ । यज्ञियां । अरमतिं । ववृत्याः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवाः वो युष्माकं यज्ञं नमोभिर्नमस्कारैर्युक्ता इमे स्तोतारो वा यजमाना वा सं महयन् । सम्यक् पूजयंति । उ इति पूरकः । मंद्रः
> स्तुतिशील उपाकेऽस्माकं समीपे स्थितोऽस्मदीयो होता प्र रिरिचे । अन्येभ्यो
> होतृभ्योऽतिरिच्यते । हे यजमान त्वं देवान् सु सुष्ठु यजस्व । हे पुर्वणीक
> बहुतेजस्विन्नग्ने त्वं यज्ञियां यज्ञार्हामरमतिं भूमिमा ववृत्याः । आवर्तय ।
> तथा च निगमांतरे । आ नो महीमरमतिं सजोषाः । ऋ. ५-४३-६ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, these worshippers or sacrificers, bringing homage (नमोभिः (*namobhiḥ*)), honour your sacrifice well. The praise-loving Hotṛ who stands near us has surpassed (प्र रिरिचे (*pra ririce*)) the other Hotṛs. O sacrificer, worship the gods well. O Agni, of many splendours (पुर्वणीक (*purvaṇīka*)), turn hither (आ ववृत्याः (*ā vavṛtyāḥ*)) the earth (अरमतिं (*aramatiṃ*)) fit for sacrifice. So also in another text: आ नो महीमरमतिं सजोषाः (*ā no mahīmaramatiṃ sajoṣāḥ*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 5-43-6).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O gods,) वः (*vaḥ*)—your
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—sacrifice
- नमोभिः (*namobhiḥ*)—the praisers accompanied by homage
- सं महयन् (*saṃ mahayan*)—worship
- उपाके (*upāke*)—who is near us
- मंद्रः (*maṃdraḥ*)—and is praise-loving
- होता (*hotā*)—the Hotṛ
- प्र रिरिचे (*pra ririce*)—is surpassing others in excellence. (O sacrificer,) देवान् (*devān*)—the gods
- सु (*su*)—exceedingly

### Printed p. 246

- यजस्व (*yajasva*)—worship
- पुर्वणीक (*purvaṇīka*)—O Agni, who has exceeding splendour
- यज्ञियां (*yajñiyāṃ*)—fit for sacrifice
- अरमतिं (*aramatiṃ*)—the earth
- आ ववृत्याः (*ā vavṛtyāḥ*)—encompass

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, praisers accompanied by homage worship your sacrifice. The Hotṛ, who is near us and is praise-loving, has surpassed the others in power. O sacrificers, worship the gods exceedingly. O Agni, of exceeding splendour, encompass the earth that is fit for sacrifice.

##### English translation
The (worshippers) offer you, (gods), this sacrifice, with prostrations : the ministrant priest, who is near us repeating pious praise, excels (all others) worship well the gods : resplendent (Agni), make the venerable earth revolve

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य॒दा वी॒रस्य॑ रे॒वतो॑ दुरो॒णे स्यो॑न॒शीरति॑थिरा॒चिके॑तत् ।  
> सुप्री॑तो अ॒ग्निः सुधि॑तो॒ दम॒ आ स वि॒शे दा॑ति॒ वार्य॒मिय॑त्यै ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यदा । वीरस्य । रेवतः । दुरोणे । स्योनशीः । अतिथिः । आचिकेतत् ।  
> सुप्रीतः । अग्निः । सुधितः । दमे । आ । सः । विशे । दाति । वार्यं । इयत्यै ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अतिथिः सर्वेषामतिथिभूतोऽग्निर्यदा वीरस्य वीरकस्य स्तोत्राणां प्रेरयिता रेवतो हविष्मतो यजमानस्य दुरोणे गृहे स्योनशीः सुखेन शयनीये
> आचिकेतत् प्रज्ञायते अग्निर्दमे यज्ञगृहे सुधितः । आकारश्चार्थे । सुनिहितश्च सुष्ठु
> निहितः सन् यदा सुप्रीतो भवति तदा सोऽग्निरियत्या उपगच्छंत्यै विशे प्रजायै
> वार्यं वरणीयं धनं दाति । ददाति । प्रयच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, the guest (अतिथिः (*atithiḥ*)) of all, becomes known (आचिकेतत् (*āciketat*)) in the house (दुरोणे (*duroṇe*)) of the sacrificer who is a hero, that is a sender-forth of praises, and who has oblations, lying comfortably in a pleasant resting place. Agni is well placed (सुधितः (*sudhitaḥ*)) in the sacrificial house; the ending (आ (*ā*)) has the sense of "and". When, well placed, he is well pleased, then Agni gives desirable wealth (वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*)) to the people who approach him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अतिथिः (*atithiḥ*)—the venerable
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- यदा (*yadā*)—when
- वीरस्य (*vīrasya*)—of him who is a sender-forth of praise
- रेवतः (*revataḥ*)—and who has oblations, the sacrificer's
- दुरोणे (*duroṇe*)—in the house
- स्योनशीः (*syonaśīḥ*)—lying comfortably
- आचिकेतत् (*āciketat*)—he is known; then
- सुधितः (*sudhitaḥ*)—well established
- सुप्रीतः (*suprītaḥ*)—and satisfied
- सः (*saḥ*)—Agni
- इयत्यै (*iyatyai*)—to the one who approaches him
- विशे (*viśe*)—to the sacrificers
- दमे (*dame*)—in the sacrificial house
- वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*)—the desired wealth
- दाति (*dāti*)—gives

### Printed p. 247

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

When the venerable Agni is known to be lying at ease in the house of the worshipper who sends forth praise and has oblations, then Agni, well established and well pleased, gives the desired wealth in the sacrificial house to the worshippers who approach him.

##### English translation
When Agni, reposing at his ease in the dwelling of the liberal worshipper, is welcomed as a guest, thus suitably placed in the hall of sacrifice, he gives well-pleased, desirable (wealth) to the people who approach him.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒मं नो॑ अग्ने अध्व॒रं जु॑षस्व म॒रुत्स्विंद्रे॑ य॒शसं॑ कृधी नः ।  
> आ नक्ता॑ ब॒र्हिः स॑दतामु॒षासो॒शंता॑ मि॒त्रावरु॑णा यजे॒ह ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इमं । नः । अग्ने । अध्वरं । जुषस्व । मरुत्सु । इंद्रे । यशसं । कृधि । नः ।  
> आ । नक्ता । बर्हिः । सदतां । उषसा । उशंता । मित्रावरुणा । यज । इह ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं नोऽस्मदीयमिममध्वरं यज्ञं जुषस्व । सेवस्व । किंच
> मरुत्स्विंद्रस्य सखिभूतेषु देवेष्विंद्रे च यशसं यशोयुक्तं हविर्लक्षणान्नवंतं नोऽस्मदीयं यज्ञं हे अग्ने त्वं कृधि । कुरु । स्थापयेत्यर्थः । तथा
> नक्ता रात्रिश्चोषसा दिवसश्च । अहर्निशे इत्यर्थः । बर्हिर्बर्हिषि कुशमये आ सदतां ।
> उपविशतां । अपि च हे अग्ने उशंतोशंतौ यज्ञमिच्छंतौ मित्रावरुणा मित्रावरुणौ देवाविहास्मिन्यज्ञे यज पूजय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Agni, accept this sacrifice of ours." Further, he asks Agni to make our sacrifice, which has oblation and food, renowned among the Maruts, the friends of Indra, and in Indra: कृधि (*kṛdhi*) means "make, establish". Likewise let Night and Dawn, that is, night and day, sit on the sacred grass made of kuśa. And, O Agni, worship in this sacrifice Mitra and Varuṇa, the two gods who desire the sacrifice (उशंता (*uśaṃtā*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- इमं अध्वरं (*imaṃ adhvaraṃ*)—this sacrifice
- जुषस्व (*juṣasva*)—accept
- मरुत्सु (*marutsu*)—among the Maruts
- इंद्रे (*iṃdre*)—and in Indra
- यशसं (*yaśasaṃ*)—endowed with oblation
- नः (*naḥ*)—our sacrifice
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—establish
- नक्ता (*naktā*)—the goddess of night
- उषसा (*uṣasā*)—the goddess of day
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—upon our grass seat
- आ सदतां (*ā sadatāṃ*)—let them sit
- उशंता (*uśaṃtā*)—desiring a share of the sacrifice
- मित्रावरुणा (*mitrāvaruṇā*)—Mitra and Varuṇa
- इह (*iha*)—in this sacrifice
- यज (*yaja*)—worship

### Printed p. 248

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, accept this sacrifice of ours. Establish our sacrifice, with its oblation, among the Maruts and in Indra. Let the goddess of night and the goddess of day sit on our grass seat. O Agni, worship in this sacrifice Mitra and Varuṇa, who desire a share of the sacrifice.

##### English translation
Be gratified, Agni, by this our sacrifice : render our (worship) renowned among Indra and the Maruts : let the days and nights sit down on the sacred grass : worship (Agni), at this rite, Mitra and Varuna, desiring (the oblation).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वाग्निं स॑ह॒स्यं१॒॑ वसि॑ष्ठो रा॒यस्का॑मो वि॒श्वप्स्न्य॑स्य स्तौत् ।  
> इषं॑ र॒यिं प॑प्रथ॒द्वाज॑म॒स्मे यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । अग्निं । सहस्यं । वसिष्ठः । रायःकामः । विश्वप्स्न्यस्य । स्तौत् ।  
> इषं । रयिं । पप्रथत् । वाजं । अस्मे इति । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वसिष्ठ एतदाख्य ऋषी रायस्कामः पश्वादिधनानीच्छन्नेवमुक्तप्रकारेण
> सहस्यं । सहो बलं । तस्य पुत्रं । तद्वंतमिति वा । अग्निं देवं विश्वप्स्न्यस्य पुरुरूपस्य धनस्य लाभाय स्तौत् । एवंभूतोऽग्निरस्मे अस्मभ्यमिषमन्नं रयिं धनं
> वाजं बलं च पप्रथत् । प्रथयतु । विस्तारयतु । ददात्वित्यर्थः । अस्मिन्सूक्ते प्रतिपादिताः सर्वे देवाः यूयमस्मान्कल्याणैः सर्वदा पालयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seer Vasiṣṭha, desiring wealth (रायस्कामः (*rāyaskāmaḥ*)), that is, cattle and other riches, praised in the manner stated the god Agni, सहस्यं (*sahasyaṃ*) ("son of strength", *sahaḥ* being strength; or "possessed of strength"), for the gaining of manifold wealth (विश्वप्स्न्यस्य (*viśvapsnyasya*)). May Agni, thus praised, spread out and give us food, wealth and strength (पप्रथत् (*paprathat*) = let him extend, let him give). And all the gods who are set forth in this sūkta: "Protect us always with blessings."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वसिष्ठः (*vasiṣṭhaḥ*)—the seer Vasiṣṭha
- रायस्कामः (*rāyaskāmaḥ*)—desiring wealth
- एव (*eva*)—in this manner
- सहस्यं (*sahasyaṃ*)—the son of strength
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- विश्वप्स्न्यस्य (*viśvapsnyasya*)—for the gaining of manifold wealth
- स्तौत् (*staut*)—praised
- (such Agni) अस्मे (*asme*)—to us
- इषं (*iṣaṃ*)—food
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- वाजं (*vājaṃ*)—strength
- पप्रथत् (*paprathat*)—let him give abundantly
- (O gods) यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

### Printed p. 249

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The seer Vasiṣṭha, desiring wealth, praised as described above Agni, the son of strength, for the gaining of wealth of many forms. May Agni, thus praised, extend to us and give abundant wealth, food and strength. O gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
Thus has Vasishtha, wishing for riches, glorified the vigorous Agni for the sake of every sort of wealth : may he bestow upon us food, riches, strength : and do you, (gods), ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 43 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्र वो यज्ञेष्विति पंचर्चं दशमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं वैश्वदेवं । प्र वः पंचेत्यनुक्रांतं ॥ सूक्तविनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥ प्रथमे छंदोमे प्रउगशस्त्रे प्र वो यज्ञेष्विति वैश्वदेवस्यृचः । सूत्रितं च । प्र वो यज्ञेषु देवयंतो अर्चन् प्र क्षोदसा धायसा सस्र एषेति प्रउगं । आ. ८-९ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This tenth sūkta, "प्र वो यज्ञेषु (*pra vo yajñeṣu*)", of five verses, has Vasiṣṭha as seer, the Viśvedevas as deity and Triṣṭubh as metre; the Anukramaṇī says "pra vaḥ pañca". Its use in the ritual (viniyoga) is Laiṅgika (by indication). In the first chandoma, in the Prauga-śastra, the verse beginning "प्र वो यज्ञेषु (*pra vo yajñeṣu*)" is a Vaiśvadeva verse. This is stated in the sūtra beginning प्र वो यज्ञेषु देवयंतो (*pra vo yajñeṣu devayaṃto*) (Ā. 8-9).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning "प्र वो यज्ञेषु (*pra vo yajñeṣu*)" is the tenth sūkta in the third anuvāka. It has five ṛks. Its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its deity the Viśvedevas, its metre Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is said "pra vaḥ pañca". The use of the sūkta is Laiṅgika. In the first chandoma, when the mantras of the Prauga-śastra are recited, the three verses beginning "प्र वो यज्ञेषु (*pra vo yajñeṣu*)" are to be recited as the Vaiśvadeva ṛc; this is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra (Ā. 8-9) beginning "pra vo yajñeṣu devayanto arcan pra kṣodasā dhāyasā sasra eṣā iti prauga".

##### Particulars

- Sūkta—43
- Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 3; Sūkta 43
- Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 4; Varga 10
- Number of ṛks in the sūkta—5
- Ṛṣi—Vasiṣṭha
- Devatā—Viśve Devāḥ
- Chandas—Triṣṭubh

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र वो॑ य॒ज्ञेषु॑ देव॒यंतो॑ अर्च॒न्द्यावा॒ नमो॑भिः पृथि॒वी इ॒षध्यै॑ ।

### Printed p. 250

> येषां ब्रह्माण्यसमानि विप्रा विष्वग्वियंति वनिनो न शाखाः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । वः । यज्ञेषु । देवयंतः । अर्चन् । द्यावा । नमःभिः । पृथिवी इति । इषध्यै ।  
> येषां । ब्रह्माणि । असमानि । विप्राः । विष्वक् । वियंति । वनिनः । न । शाखाः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवयंतो देवान्कामयमाना विप्रा यज्ञेषु नमोभिः स्तुतिभिर्हविर्भिर्वा वो युष्मानिषध्यै अभिप्राप्तुं प्रार्चन् । प्रार्चयंति । प्रकर्षेण स्तुवंति । द्यावा दिवं च पृथिवी भूमिं च प्रस्तुवंति । येषां विप्राणां मेधाविनां ब्रह्माणि
> स्तोत्राणि वनिनो न शाखा वृक्षस्य शाखा इव विष्वग्श्वतो वियंति । गच्छंति । ते
> विप्राः प्रार्चन्निति पूर्वेण संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sages (विप्रा (*viprā*)), desiring the gods (देवयंतः (*devayaṃtaḥ*)), at sacrifices praise you intensely with नमोभिः (*namobhiḥ*), that is, with praises or with oblations, in order to reach you (इषध्यै (*iṣadhyai*)). They also praise heaven and earth. These are the wise sages whose ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*), that is, hymns of praise, go forth in every direction like the branches of a tree. The connection is with the earlier part: "those sages praised".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O gods) येषां (*yeṣāṃ*)—of whichever praiser
- असमानि (*asamāni*)—extraordinary
- ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)—hymns
- वनिनः (*vaninaḥ*)—of a tree
- शाखाः न (*śākhāḥ na*)—like branches
- विष्वक् (*viṣvak*)—everywhere
- वियंति (*viyaṃti*)—spread
- देवयंतः (*devayaṃtaḥ*)—those who seek the favour of the gods
- विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)—the wise worshippers (praisers)
- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—at sacrifices
- नमोभिः (*namobhiḥ*)—together with oblations
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- इषध्यै (*iṣadhyai*)—in order to approach
- प्रार्चन् (*prārcan*)—praise exceedingly
- द्यावा (*dyāvā*)—the heavenly world
- पृथिवीं (*pṛthivīṃ*)—the earth, they praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, those praisers whose extraordinary hymns spread everywhere like the branches of a tree, who seek the favour of the gods and are wise, praise you exceedingly at sacrifices, with oblations, in order to approach you. They praise heaven and earth also.

##### English translation
Devout worshippers seek to attain you, gods, by praises at sacrifices : they (worship) the heaven and earth, they of whom the diversified adorations spread everywhere like the branches of trees.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र य॒ज्ञ ए॑तु॒ हेत्वो॒ न सप्ति॒रुद्य॑च्छध्वं॒ सम॑नसो घृ॒ताचीः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 251

> स्तृणीत बर्हिरध्वराय साधूर्ध्वा शोचींषि देवयून्यस्थुः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । यज्ञः । एतु । हेत्वः । न । सप्तिः । उत् । यच्छध्वं । समनसः । घृताचीः ।  
> स्तृणीत । बर्हिः । अध्वराय । साधु । ऊर्ध्वा । शोचींषि । देवयूनि । अस्थुः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमस्मदीयो यज्ञः प्रैतु । देवान्प्रति गच्छतु । तत्र दृष्टांतः । हेत्वो न सप्तिः शीघ्रगाम्यश्वो यथा तद्वत् । हे ऋत्विजः सर्वे यूयं समनसः
> सदृशमनस्काः संतो घृताचीः स्रुच उद्यच्छध्वं । हस्त उद्यम्य धारयत । तथाध्वराय योगं कर्तुं साधु साधकं बर्हिः कुशमयं स्तृणीत । वेद्यां छादयत । हे अग्ने देवयूनि देवान्कामयमानानि त्वदीयानि शोचींष्यर्चींष्यूर्ध्वोर्ध्वमुखान्यस्थुः । तिष्ठंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Let this sacrifice of ours go forth, go towards the gods. An illustration: like a swift-going horse (हेत्वो न सप्तिः (*hetvo na saptiḥ*)). O priests, all of you, being of one mind, lift up the ladles (घृताचीः (*ghṛtācīḥ*)) and hold them raised in the hand. Likewise, to perform the sacrifice (अध्वराय (*adhvarāya*)), spread the effective grass made of kuśa, cover the altar. O Agni, let your flames (शोचींषि (*śocīṃṣi*)), which desire the gods (देवयूनि (*devayūni*)), stand upward-facing.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यज्ञः (*yajñaḥ*)—our sacrifice
- हेत्वः (*hetvaḥ*)—swift-going
- सप्तिः न (*saptiḥ na*)—like a horse
- प्र एतु (*pra etu*)—let it quickly go and reach the gods
- (O priests) समनसः (*samanasaḥ*)—all of you, being of one mind
- घृताचीः (*ghṛtācīḥ*)—the ladle-vessel
- उद्यच्छध्वं (*udyacchadhvaṃ*)—lift up
- अध्वराय (*adhvarāya*)—for the performance of the sacrifice
- साधु (*sādhu*)—effective for it
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—the grass seat
- स्तृणीत (*stṛṇīta*)—spread on the altar
- (O Agni) देवयूनि (*devayūni*)—desiring the gods
- शोचींषि (*śocīṃṣi*)—your flames
- ऊर्ध्वा (*ūrdhvā*)—upward-facing
- अस्थुः (*asthuḥ*)—let them rise and stand

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let our sacrifice go quickly and reach the gods, like a swift-going horse. O priests, all of you, being of one mind, lift up and hold the ladle-vessel. For the performance of the sacrifice, spread the effective grass seat on the altar. O Agni, let your flames, which desire the gods, rise and stand upward-facing.

##### English translation
Let the sacrifice proceed like a swift courser (to the gods) : elevate, (priests), with one accord, your ladles, charged with butter : spread for the solemnity the sacred grass : let the flames (of the burnt-offering) to the gods ascend on high.

### Printed p. 252

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ पु॒त्रासो॒ न मा॒तरं॒ विभृ॑त्राः॒ सानौ॑ दे॒वासो॑ ब॒र्हिषः॑ सदंतु ।  
> आ वि॒श्वाची॑ विद॒थ्या॑मन॒क्त्वग्ने॒ मा नो॑ दे॒वता॑ता॒ मृध॑स्कः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । पुत्रासः । न । मातरं । विभृत्राः । सानौ । देवासः । बर्हिषः । सदंतु ।  
> आ । विश्वाची । विदथ्यां । अनक्तु । अग्ने । मा । नः । देवताता । मृधः । करिति कः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मातरं जननीं विभृत्रा विशेषेण भर्तव्याः पुत्रासो न पुत्रा इव तद्वदस्माकं भरणीया देवासो देवा बर्हिषः कुशमयस्य वेद्यामास्तीर्णस्य सानावुन्नते
> देश आ सदंतु । उपविशंतु । हे अग्ने विदथ्यां यज्ञयोग्यां त्वदीयां ज्वालां
> विश्वाची । विश्वं सर्वं हविरंचति गच्छतीति विश्वाची जुहूः । अनक्तु । आ समंतात्सिंचतु । देवताता देवतातौ युद्धे नोऽस्माकं मृधो हिंसकान् हे अग्ने त्वं
> मा कः । मा कार्षीः । यज्ञवाचको देवतातिशब्दोऽत्र संग्रामे वर्तते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Like sons who are specially borne (विभृत्रा (*vibhṛtrā*)) by the mother, so let the gods, who are to be borne by us, sit down on the elevated place (सानौ (*sānau*)) of the altar spread with grass made of kuśa. O Agni, let the विश्वाची (*viśvācī*), which goes (*aṃcati*) to all (विश्वं (*viśvaṃ*)) oblation, that is, the Juhū ladle, pour on every side your flame (विदथ्यां (*vidathyāṃ*)), which is fit for the sacrifice. O Agni, in the देवताता (*devatātā*), in battle, do not bring (मा कः (*mā kaḥ*)) upon us मृधः (*mṛdhaḥ*), harmers. The word देवताति (*devatāti*), which denotes sacrifice, here stands for battle.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मातरं (*mātaraṃ*)—on the lap of the mother who bore them
- विभृत्राः (*vibhṛtrāḥ*)—exceedingly borne
- पुत्रासो न (*putrāso na*)—like sons
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—the gods who are borne by us
- बर्हिषः (*barhiṣaḥ*)—of the grass seat
- सानौ (*sānau*)—on the elevated place
- आ सदंतु (*ā sadaṃtu*)—let them sit
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- विदथ्यां (*vidathyāṃ*)—in your flame, fit for worship
- विश्वाची (*viśvācī*)—the Juhū vessel that pours all oblations
- आ अनक्तु (*ā anaktu*)—let it pour the oblation
- (O Agni) देवताता (*devatātā*)—in battle
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- मृधः (*mṛdhaḥ*)—harmers
- मा कः (*mā kaḥ*)—do not produce

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Like sons exceedingly borne on the lap of the mother who bore them, let the gods, who are borne by us, sit on the elevated part of the grass seat. O Agni, let the Juhū vessel, which pours all oblations, pour the oblation into your flame, which is fit for worship. O Agni, do not produce harmers for us in battle.

##### English translation
Let the gods sit down on the summit of the sacred grass, like the children nursed on the (lap of the) mother : let the full ladle, Agni, pour

### Printed p. 253
(the oblation) on the sacrificial flame: give us not up to our adversaries in battle.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते सी॑षपंत॒ जोष॒मा यज॑त्रा ऋ॒तस्य॒ धाराः॑ सु॒दुघा॒ दुहा॑नाः ।  
> ज्येष्ठं॑ वो अ॒द्य मह॒ आ वसू॑ना॒मा गं॑तन॒ सम॑नसो॒ यति॒ ष्ठ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । सीषपंत । जोषं । आ । यजत्राः । ऋतस्य । धाराः । सुदुघाः । दुहानाः ।  
> ज्येष्ठं । वः । अद्य । महः । आ । वसूनां । आ । गंतन । समनसः । यति । स्थ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यजत्रा यजनीयास्ते इंद्रादयो देवा ऋतस्योदकस्य सुदुघाः सुखेन
> दोग्धुं शक्या धारा दुहाना वर्षंतो जोषं पर्याप्तं यथा भवति तथा आ सीषपंत । सपतिः परिचरणार्थः । स्तुतिभिरा समंतात्पर्यचीचरन् । अस्मान्परिचरणं कुर्वंतु । स्वीकुर्वंत्विति यावत् । अद्यास्मिन्दिने हे देवाः वसूनां धनानां मध्ये
> ज्येष्ठं श्रेष्ठं वो युष्मदीयं महो मंहनीयं धनमा गच्छतु । यूयमपि
> समनसस्तुल्यमतयः संत आ गंतन । आगच्छत । हे देवाः यूयं यति स्थ आगंतनेति संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Those adorable gods, Indra and the rest, milking the easily milked streams of water, that is, raining, are to serve us abundantly, to full satisfaction. The root सपतिः (*sapatiḥ*) here has the sense of serving; they are to serve with praises on every side, that is, to accept us. Sāyaṇa addresses the gods: today, among riches, the best and most honourable of your wealth is to come to us. And the gods themselves, being of like mind, are to come. The words यति स्थ (*yati stha*) are connected with आगंतन (*āgaṃtana*): "you who are so many, come."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यजत्राः (*yajatrāḥ*)—the adorable
- ते (*te*)—those gods, Indra and the others
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of water
- सुदुघाः (*sudughāḥ*)—easy to milk
- धाराः (*dhārāḥ*)—streams of rain
- दुहानाः (*duhānāḥ*)—pouring out
- जोषं (*joṣaṃ*)—so as to attain satisfaction
- आ सीषपंत (*ā sīṣapaṃta*)—let them be served by us (or: let them serve us so as to give us satisfaction)
- अद्य (*adya*)—today, on this day of the sacrifice
- वसूनां (*vasūnāṃ*)—among riches
- ज्येष्ठं (*jyeṣṭhaṃ*)—the excellent
- वः (*vaḥ*)—your
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—the honourable wealth
- आ गंतन (*ā gaṃtana*)—let it come and join us
- समनसः (*samanasaḥ*)—of one mind
- आ गंतन (*ā gaṃtana*)—come to us and join us
- यति स्थ (*yati stha*)—gathered together

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the gods Indra and the others, who are able to milk the water, who shed streams of rain, and who are adorable, serve us so as to bring us satisfaction. O gods, today, on this day of the sacrifice, may the most excellent and honourable of your riches come and join us. And you also, of one mind, come together to us.

### Printed p. 254

##### English translation
May the adorable deities, who are the bestowers of water, the shedders of showers, be fully propitiated (by our praises): may the most precious and commendable of your treasures (be ours) to-day: and do you with one accord come hither.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा नो॑ अग्ने वि॒क्ष्व द॑शस्य॒ त्वया॑ व॒यं स॑हसाव॒न्नास्क्राः॑  
> रा॒या यु॒जा स॑ध॒मादो॒ अरि॑ष्टा यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑  
> नः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । नः । अग्ने । विक्षु । आ । दशस्य । त्वया । वयं । सहसावन् । आस्क्राः ।  
> राया । युजा । सधमादः । अरिष्टाः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने एव्येवं स्तुतस्त्वं विक्षु प्रजासु मध्ये नोऽस्मभ्यमा दशस्य । धनमभिप्रयच्छ । हे सहसावन्बलवन्नग्ने त्वयास्क्रा अस्कन्ना वयं युजा नित्ययुक्तेन राया धनेन सधमादः सह माद्यंतोऽरिष्टा अहिंसिता भवेम । अस्मिन्सूक्ते प्रतिपादिताः सर्वे देवाः यूयं नोऽस्मान्कल्याणैः सर्वदा पालयतॆ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, thus praised, give us wealth among the peoples. O mighty Agni, may we, not abandoned by you, rejoicing together with wealth that is constantly joined to us, be unharmed. All the gods celebrated in this sūkta: you protect us always with blessings.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- एव (*eva*)—thus praised, you
- विक्षु (*vikṣu*)—among the people
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ दशस्य (*ā daśasya*)—give wealth
- सहसावन् (*sahasāvan*)—O mighty Agni
- त्वया (*tvayā*)—by you
- आस्क्राः (*āskrāḥ*)—not abandoned
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- युजा (*yujā*)—that is permanently joined
- राया (*rāyā*)—with wealth
- सधमादः (*sadhamādaḥ*)—rejoicing together
- अरिष्टाः (*ariṣṭāḥ*)—unharmed; may we be so
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you (O gods)
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पातॆ (*pātè*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, thus praised, give us wealth among the people. O mighty Agni, may we, not abandoned by you, with the help of wealth ever joined with us, be rejoicing together and unharmed. O gods, protect us always with blessings.

### Printed p. 255

##### English translation
Be glorified, Agni: grant us (wealth) among the people: may we, vigorous Agni, ever be undeserted by you, but always be rejoicing and unmolested in the possession of riches: and do you, (gods), ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 44 (Maṇḍala 7)
Sāyaṇa's introduction: the sūkta beginning दधिक्रां वः (*dadhikrāṃ vaḥ*) has five Ṛks, is the eleventh sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its deity is the one called Dadhikrā. The first verse is in Jagatī metre and has the deities stated in its own indications (Dadhikrā and the others); the remaining four are in Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī says: दधिक्रां दाधिक्रं जगत्याद्या लिंगोक्तदेवतेति (*dadhikrāṃ dādhikraṃ jagatyādyā liṃgoktadevateti*). The viniyoga has been stated already.

**Anuvāda:** The sūkta दधिक्रां वः (*dadhikrāṃ vaḥ*) is the eleventh sūkta in the third anuvāka and has five Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is its seer; Dadhikrāvan is the deity. The first Ṛk is in Jagatī metre and has the deities indicated by its own terms, Dadhikrā and others. The remaining four Ṛks are in Triṣṭubh metre. The Anukramaṇī says: Dadhikrā … The viniyoga was stated earlier.

Sūkta 44; Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 3; Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 4; Varga 11. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 5. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: 1. deities stated by indication; 2–5. Dadhikrā. Metre: 1. Jagatī; 2–5. Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द॒धि॒क्रां वः॑ प्रथ॒मम॒श्विनो॒षस॑म॒ग्निं समि॑द्धं॒ भग॑मू॒तये॑  
> हुवे ।  
> इंद्रं॒ विष्णुं॑ पू॒षणं॒ ब्रह्म॑ण॒स्पति॑मादि॒त्यान्द्यावा॑पृथि॒वी अ॒पः  ॑
> स्वः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दधिक्रां । वः । प्रथमं । अश्विना । उषसं । अग्निं । सम्ऽइद्धं । भगं । ऊतये । हुवे ।  
> इंद्रं । विष्णुं । पूषणं । ब्रह्मणः । पतिं । आदित्यान् । द्यावापृथिवी इति । अपः ।  
> स्वः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 256

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे स्तोतारः वो युष्माकमूतये रक्षणाय प्रथमं दधिक्रामश्वाभिमानिनीं देवतां हुवे । आह्वयामि । ततोऽश्विनावश्विदेवौ उषसमुषोदेवतां च समिद्धं सम्यग्दीप्तमग्निं च भगमेतदाख्यं देवं चाह्वयामि । इंद्रं विष्णुं पूषणं च ब्रह्मणस्पतिमादित्यान् द्यावापृथिवी आप उदकदेवताः स्वः सूर्यमित्येतान्देवानाह्वयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O praisers, for your protection I invoke first Dadhikrā, the deity presiding over the horse. Then I invoke the two Aśvins, the Dawn, the well-kindled Agni, and the god named Bhaga; Indra, Viṣṇu, Pūṣan, Brahmaṇaspati, the Ādityas, heaven and earth, the Waters (the deities of water), and Svar, the sun: I invoke these gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O praisers) वः (*vaḥ*)—your
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—for protection
- प्रथमं (*prathamaṃ*)—first
- दधिक्रां (*dadhikrāṃ*)—the deity presiding over the horse
- हुवे (*huve*)—I call
- (then) अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin deities
- उषसं (*uṣasaṃ*)—the goddess Dawn
- समिद्धं (*samiddhaṃ*)—the blazing
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- भगं (*bhagaṃ*)—the god Bhaga; I call
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- विष्णुं (*viṣṇuṃ*)—Viṣṇu
- पूषणं (*pūṣaṇaṃ*)—the god Pūṣan
- ब्रह्मणस्पतिं (*brahmaṇaspatiṃ*)—Brahmaṇaspati
- आदित्यान् (*ādityān*)—the Ādityas
- द्यावापृथिवी (*dyāvāpṛthivī*)—heaven and earth
- आपः (*āpaḥ*)—the deities of the waters
- स्वः (*svaḥ*)—the sun; I call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praisers, for your protection I first call the deity presiding over the horse. Then I call the Aśvin deities, the goddess Dawn, the blazing Agni, Bhaga, Indra, Pūṣan, Viṣṇu, Brahmaṇaspati, the Ādityas, heaven and earth, the water deities, and the sun: all these deities.

##### English translation
For your preservation (worshippers), I invoke, first, Dadhikra, then the Ashwins, the Dawn, the kindled Agni, Bhaga, Indra, Vishnu, Pushan, Brahmanaspati, the Adityas, heaven and earth, the waters, the sun.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the present Ṛk, together with the deity called Dadhikrā, the goddess Dawn, Agni, Bhaga, Indra, Viṣṇu, Pūṣan, Brahmaṇaspati, the Ādityas, heaven and earth, and the deities presiding over water are all praised. In the following Ṛks of this sūkta only the deity Dadhikrā is praised.

Besides the horses that draw the chariots of the gods, certain other horses are individually mentioned in the Ṛgveda. Of these, Dadhikrā, Tārkṣya, Paidva and Etaśa are the chief. Since the present sūkta praises the horse-deity Dadhikrā, only this deity is discussed here. Among the horse-deities Dadhikrā is the chief one. This horse is mentioned in four sūktas of the Ṛgveda: 4-38, 4-39, 4-40 and 7-44. In these sūktas the word दधिक्रा (*dadhikrā*) occurs twelve times and the word दधिक्रावन् (*dadhikrāvan*) ten times. Elsewhere in the other

### Printed p. 257
Vedas it is not especially mentioned. In the Nirukta the word दधिक्रा (*dadhikrā*) is included among the names of the horse (Ni. 1-14). This horse is very swift (Ṛ. Saṃ. 4-38-2-9; 4-39-1); it is the first horse in the line of many chariots (7-44-4); it destroys chariots and runs with the speed of the wind (4-38-3); all people praise its speed (4-38-3, 9); it rushes even at the turns of the roads (4-40-4). It also has wings and is like a bird, and its wings are compared to the wings of the hawk (4-40-2 & 3). In some places it is called the hawk itself (4-38-2 & 5). In Ṛ. Saṃ. 4-40-5 it is described with epithets that apply to Agni: the swan dwelling in the light, the Vasu in the atmosphere, the Hotṛ seated on the altar, the guest seated comfortably in the house, and so on.

Dadhikra is also a hero. He destroys the Dasyu peoples and is victorious (Ṛ. Saṃ. 4-38-1, 2, 3 & 7). When he fights against thousands, the enemies fear him; he takes spoil in battle; in battles the various tribes cry out to him (4-38-5, 9, 4). They belong to all the tribes. As the sun covers the water with his radiance, so he by his might has covered the five tribes (4-38-2, 4, 10). Mitra and Varuṇa gave this victorious horse to the Pūrus (4-39-2; 4-38-1, 2). For the favour of men they gave the horse called Dadhikrā.

At the kindling of the fire at the time of the dawn's rise, the horse Dadhikrāvan is praised (7-41-6). Together with the goddess Dawn he too is invoked (4-39-1; 4-40-1). The Dawns are also entreated to come towards the sacrifice as Dadhikrāvan does (7-41-6). This deity is praised along with the goddess Dawn, Agni, the Aśvins, the sun and other deities (3-20-1; 7-44-1 to 4; 10-101-1). But the first invocation is to Dadhikrāvan himself (7-44-1).

From the derivation of the word nothing is learned of the nature and characteristics of this horse. क्रा (*krā*) may mean "scattering", so that he would be the one who scatters the curd-like frost seen at sunrise. Some are of the opinion that the rising disc of the sun itself may be this deity Dadhikrā. Dadhikrā has a special relation with the goddess Dawn. Since the sun too is often called a horse or a bird, and in some places is said to have the nature of a warrior, it may be said that Dadhikrā is the solar disc itself. Some hold that Dadhikrā is a special form of Agni. Others say that it is not a deity at all but an excellent horse.

### Printed p. 258
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द॒धि॒क्रामु॒ नम॑सा बो॒धयं॑त उ॒दीरा॑णा य॒ज्ञमु॑पप्र॒यंतः॑  
> इळां॑ दे॒वीं ब॒र्हिषि॑ सा॒दयं॑तो॒ऽश्विना॒ विप्रा॑ सु॒हवा॑ हुवेम ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दधिक्रां । ऊं इति । नमसा । बोधयंतः । उत्ईराणाः । यज्ञं । उपप्रयंतः ।  
> इळां । देवीं । बर्हिषि । सादयंतः । अश्विना । विप्रा । सुहवा । हुवेम ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दधिक्रामेतन्नामकमश्वविशेषं देवं नमसा स्तोत्रेण बोधयंतः प्रज्ञापयंतः उदीराणाः प्रेरयंतो यज्ञं यागमुपप्रयंत उपक्रममाणा वयं बर्हिषीळां हवीरूपां देवीं सादयंत आस्थापयंतः सुहवा शोभनाह्वानौ विप्रा विप्रौ मेधाविनावश्विनाश्विनौ देवौ हुवेम । आह्वयाम । उ इति पूरणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We, awakening with praise (नमसा (*namasā*), by hymn) the horse-deity of this name, Dadhikrā, urging him on, setting about the sacrifice, and seating on the sacred grass the goddess Iḷā in the form of the oblation, invoke the two Aśvins, who are wise (विप्रा (*viprā*)), intelligent and of good invocation (सुहवा (*suhavā*)). The word उ (*u*) is a mere filler.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दधिक्रां (*dadhikrāṃ*)—the horse-deity named Dadhikrā
- नमसा (*namasā*)—with praises
- बोधयंतः (*bodhayaṃtaḥ*)—awakening
- उदीराणाः (*udīrāṇāḥ*)—urging on
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—the sacrifice
- उपप्रयंतः (*upaprayaṃtaḥ*)—beginning
- बर्हिषि (*barhiṣi*)—on the sacred grass
- देवीं (*devīṃ*)—the divine
- इळां (*iḷāṃ*)—goddess Iḷā
- सादयंतः (*sādayaṃtaḥ*)—seating
- (वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we)
- सुहवा (*suhavā*)—worthy of good invocation
- विप्रा (*viprā*)—wise
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin deities
- हुवेम (*huvema*)—we call with entreaty

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Awakening with praises the horse-deity called Dadhikrā, urging him on, beginning the sacrifice, and seating the divine goddess Iḷā on the sacred grass, we call with entreaty the Aśvin deities, who are worthy of good invocation and wise.

##### English translation
Arousing and animating Dadhikra, proceeding diligently with the sacrifice: seating the divine Ila on the sacred grass, let us invoke the intelligent and worthily-invoked Ashwins.

### Printed p. 259
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द॒धि॒क्रावा॑णं बुबुधा॒नो अ॒ग्निमुप॑ ब्रुव उ॒षसं॒ सूर्यं॒ गां ।  
> ब्र॒ध्नं मं॑श्च॒तोर्वरु॑णस्य ब॒भ्रुं ते विश्वा॒स्मद्दु॑रि॒ता या॑वयंतु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दधिक्रावाणं । बुबुधानः । अग्निं । उप । ब्रुवे । उषसं । सूर्यं । गां ।  
> ब्रध्नं । मंश्चतोः । वरुणस्य । बभ्रुं । ते । विश्वा । अस्मत् । दुःइता । यवयंतु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दधिक्रावाणमश्वविशेषं बुबुधानः स्तोत्रेण बोधयन्नहमग्निं देवमुप ब्रुवे । उपस्तौमि । तथा चोषसमुषोदेवतां सूर्यं सर्वस्य प्रेरकं देवं गां भूमिं । वाग्देवतां वा । एवंभूतान्देवानहमुपस्तौमि । मंश्चतोः । मन्यमानान् स्तुवतो जनांश्चेतयते जानातीति यद्वाभिमन्यमानांश्चाशयते नाशयतीति मंश्चतुः । तस्य वरुणस्य ब्रध्नं महांतं बभ्रुं पिंगलवर्णमश्वं तस्यानयनार्थमहमुप ब्रुवे । ते देवा अस्मदस्मत्तो विश्वा विश्वानि सर्वाणि दुरिता दुरितानि पापानि यवयंतु । पृथक्कुर्वंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

I, arousing with praise the horse Dadhikrāvan, address Agni, the deity, in praise. Likewise I praise Uṣas, the deity of dawn; Sūrya, the god who impels all; and Gō, the earth, or else the goddess of speech. Maṃścatu is explained as one who makes aware (चेतयते (*cetayate*)) the people who think and praise, or else one who destroys (नाशयतीति (*nāśayatīti*)) those who are arrogant. I call upon the great, tawny-coloured horse of that Varuṇa, for the purpose of bringing him. May those gods separate from us (put apart) all sins.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दधिक्रावाणं (*dadhikrāvāṇaṃ*)—the particular horse named Dadhikrā
- बुबुधानः (*bubudhānaḥ*)—I, who awaken with praises
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- उप ब्रुवे (*upa bruve*)—I praise
- उषसं (*uṣasaṃ*)—Uṣas the goddess
- सूर्यं (*sūryaṃ*)—Sūrya the god
- गां (*gāṃ*)—the goddess of speech
- मंश्चतोः (*maṃścatoḥ*)—of the one who stimulates the praisers
- वरुणस्य (*varuṇasya*)—of Varuṇa
- ब्रध्नं (*bradhnaṃ*)—of great form
- बभ्रुं (*babhruṃ*)—golden-coloured; I praise that horse
- ते (*te*)—all these deities
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—from us
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- दुरिता (*duritā*)—sins
- यावयंतु (*yāvayaṃtu*)—let them separate (destroy)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I, who awaken with praises the horse Dadhikrā, praise Agni. I also praise Uṣas, Sūrya, the goddess of speech, and the great, golden-coloured horse of Varuṇa, who stimulates the praisers. May all these deities remove from us all sins (destroy them).

### Printed p. 260

##### English translation
Propitiating Dadhikravan, I glorify Agni, Ushas, the sun, the earth, the great brown horse of Varuna, who is mindful of his adorers : may they put far away from us all, iniquities.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द॒धि॒क्रावा॑ प्रथ॒मो वा॒ज्यर्वाग्रे॒ रथा॑नां भवति प्रजा॒नन् ।  
> सं॒वि॒दा॒न उ॒षसा॒ सूर्ये॑णादि॒त्येभि॒र्वसु॑भि॒रंगि॑रोभिः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दधिक्रावा । प्रथमः । वाजी । अर्वा । अग्रे । रथानां । भवति । प्रजानन् ।  
> संविदानः । उषसा । सूर्येण । आदित्येभिः । वसुभिः । अंगिरःभिः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रथमः सर्वेषामश्वानां मुख्यो वाजी शीघ्रगाम्यर्वा गमनशीलो दधिक्रावाश्वरूपो देवः प्रजानन् रथसंयोजनीयांस्तानश्वानधिष्ठाय ज्ञातव्यानि सम्यग्जानन् रथानामग्रे प्रमुखो भवति । कीदृशोऽश्वः । उषसोषोदेवतया सूर्येण सर्वस्य प्रेरकेण देवेनादित्येभिरादित्यैश्च वसुभिश्चांगिरोभिर्देवैः सह स्तोतव्यैर्ऋषिभिश्च संविदानः सम्यग्जानन् । ऐकमत्यं प्राप्त इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Dadhikrāvan, the god in the form of a horse, is first, chief of all horses, swift (वाजी (*vājī*)) and a goer (अर्वा (*arvā*)). Presiding over the horses to be yoked to the chariots and knowing well what is to be known, he becomes foremost in front of the chariots. What kind of horse? One who is in accord (संविदानः (*saṃvidānaḥ*)) with Uṣas the deity, with Sūrya the god who impels all, with the Ādityas, the Vasus and the Aṅgirases, gods together with the sages to be praised; that is, he has attained unity of mind.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उषसा (*uṣasā*)—with Uṣas
- सूर्येण (*sūryeṇa*)—with Sūrya
- आदित्येभिः (*ādityebhiḥ*)—with the Ādityas
- वसुभिः (*vasubhiḥ*)—with the Vasus
- अंगिरोभिः (*aṃgirobhiḥ*)—with the Aṅgirases
- संविदानः (*saṃvidānaḥ*)—one united
- प्रथमः (*prathamaḥ*)—the chief among all horses
- वाजी (*vājī*)—swift
- अर्वा (*arvā*)—a goer
- दधिक्रावा (*dadhikrāvā*)—the god presiding over the horse named Dadhikrā
- प्रजानन् (*prajānan*)—knowing all that is to be known
- रथानां (*rathānāṃ*)—of the chariots
- अग्रे (*agre*)—in the front part
- भवति (*bhavati*)—shines

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The god presiding over the horse Dadhikrā, united with Uṣas, Sūrya, the Ādityas, the Vasus and the Aṅgirases, the chief of all horses, swift and a goer, knowing all that is to be known, shines at the front of the chariots (ahead).

### Printed p. 261

##### English translation
Dahikravan, the swift steed, the first (of horses), knowing (his office), is in the front of the chariots (of the gods), consentient with Ushas, with Surya, with the Adityas, with the Vasus, with the Angirasas.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॑ दधि॒क्राः प॒थ्या॑मनक्त्वृ॒तस्य॒ पंथा॒मन्वे॑त॒वा उ॑ ।  
> शृ॒णोतु॑ नो॒ दैव्यं॒ शर्धो॑ अ॒ग्निः शृ॒ण्वंतु॒ विश्वे॑ महि॒षा अमू॑राः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । दधिक्राः । पथ्यां । अनक्तु । ऋतस्य । पंथां । अनुएतवै । ऊं इति ।  
> शृणोतु । नः । दैव्यं । शर्धः । अग्निः । शृण्वंतु । विश्वे । महिषाः । अमूराः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दधिक्रा अश्वरूपो देव ऋतस्य यज्ञस्य पंथां पंथानं मार्गमन्वेतवा अनुगंतुं प्रवृत्तानां नोऽस्माकं पथ्यां पदवीमानक्तु । उदकेनासिंचतु । उ इति पूरणः । दैव्यं शर्धो देवसंबंधि बलमिद्यग्रूपोऽग्निर्नोऽस्मदीयं हवं शृणोतु । अमूरा अमूढा महिषा महांतो विश्वे सर्वे देवा अस्मदीयं हवं शृण्वंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Dadhikrā, the god in the form of a horse, is asked to sprinkle with water the path (पथ्यां (*pathyāṃ*)) of us who have set out to follow the way of the sacrifice (ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)). उ (*u*) is a filler. May Agni, in the form of the divine strength (शर्धः (*śardhaḥ*)) belonging to the gods, hear our invocation. May all the great, unbewildered (अमूराः (*amūrāḥ*)) gods hear our invocation.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दधिक्राः (*dadhikrāḥ*)—the god Dadhikrā
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice
- पंथां (*paṃthāṃ*)—the path
- अन्वेतवै (*anvetavai*)—to follow
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us who have striven
- पथ्यां (*pathyāṃ*)—the path fit for travel
- आ अनक्तु (*ā anaktu*)—let him sprinkle with water
- दैव्यं (*daivyaṃ*)—belonging to the gods
- शर्धः (*śardhaḥ*)—in the form of strength
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—our invocation
- शृणोतु (*śṛṇotu*)—let him hear
- अमूराः (*amūrāḥ*)—the wise
- महिषाः (*mahiṣāḥ*)—the great-souled
- विश्वे (*viśve*)—all deities
- शृण्वंतु (*śṛṇvaṃtu*)—let them hear our invocation

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the god Dadhikrā sprinkle with water a path fit for travel for us, who have striven to follow the path of the sacrifice. May Agni, strength to the gods, hear our invocation. May all the wise and great-souled deities hear our invocation.

### Printed p. 262

##### English translation
May Dadhikra sprinkle our path (with water), that we may follow the road of sacrifice : may Agni, the strength of the gods, hear our (invocation) : may the mighty, unperplexed, universal deities hear it.

## Sūkta 45 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आ देवो यात्विति चतुर्ऋचं द्वादशं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं सवितृदेवताकं त्रैष्टुभं । आ देवश्चतुष्कं सावित्रमित्यनुक्रांतं ॥ व्यूळ्हे दशरात्रे चतुर्थेऽहनि वैश्वदेवशस्त्र इदं सावित्रनिविद्धानं । सूत्र्यते हि । चतुर्थेऽहन्या देवो यातु.... । आ. ४-८ ॥ ........इत्येषा वपानुवाक्या । सूत्रितं च । आ देवो यातु सविता सुरत्नः स घा नो देवः सविता सहावा । आ. ३-७ । इति ॥ अश्वमेधेऽनुसवनं तिस्रः सावित्र्य इष्टयः । तत्र द्वितीयस्यामिष्ट्या याज्येयं । सूत्रितं च । य इमा विश्वा जातान्या देवो यातु । आ. १०-६ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta आ देवो यातु (*ā devo yātu*) is a hymn of four Ṛks, the twelfth sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its deity Savitṛ, its metre Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī says आ देवश्चतुष्कं सावित्रं (*ā devaścatuṣkaṃ sāvitraṃ*). Viniyoga: in the Vyūḍha Daśarātra, on the fourth day, in the Vaiśvadevaśastra, this is the Sāvitra nividdhāna. It is stated in the sūtra: चतुर्थेऽहन्या देवो यातु.... । आ. ४-८ ॥ (*caturthe 'hanyā devo yātu.... | ā. 4-8 ‖*); this is the vapā-anuvākyā, and it is set out in the sūtra आ देवो यातु सविता सुरत्नः स घा नो देवः सविता सहावा । आ. ३-७ (*ā devo yātu savitā suratnaḥ sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sahāvā | ā. 3-7*). In the Aśvamedha, at the after-pressing (anusavana), there are three Sāvitrī oblations (iṣṭi); in the second of them this is the yājyā, and it is set out in the sūtra य इमा विश्वा जातान्या देवो यातु । आ. १०-६ (*ya imā viśvā jātānyā devo yātu | ā. 10-6*).

Anuvāda: this sūkta आ देवो यातु (*ā devo yātu*) is the twelfth sūkta in the third anuvāka, with four Ṛks. Its seer is Vasiṣṭha, deity Savitṛ, metre Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī says आ देवश्चतुष्कं सावित्रं (*ā devaścatuṣkaṃ sāvitraṃ*). In the ten-night sacrifice called Vyūḍha, when reciting the mantras of the Vaiśvadeva śastra on the fourth day, this sūkta is to be added and recited as the nividdhānīya sūkta addressed to Savitṛ, as explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra चतुर्थेऽहन्या देवो यातु....... (*caturthe 'hanyā devo yātu.......*) (Ā. 4-8). In the ...... the first Ṛk of this sūkta is to be recited as the anuvākyā mantra in the vapā offering, as explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra आ देवो यातु सविता सुरत्नः स घा नो देवः सविता सहावा (*ā devo yātu savitā suratnaḥ sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sahāvā*) (Ā. 3-7). In the Aśvamedha sacrifice, in the anusavana rite, three iṣṭis connected with Savitṛ are to be performed; in that context, in the second iṣṭi this Ṛk is to be recited as the yājyā mantra, as explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra य इमा विश्वा जातान्या देवो यातु (*ya imā viśvā jātānyā devo yātu*) (Ā. 10-6).**NOTE**

Sūkta 45; Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 3; Aṣṭaka 5, Adhyāya 4, Varga 12; number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4; Ṛṣi: Vasiṣṭha; Devatā: Savitā; Chandas: Triṣṭubh.

### Printed p. 263
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ दे॒वो या॑तु सवि॒ता सु॒रत्नो॑ऽंतरिक्ष॒प्रा वह॑मानो॒ अश्वैः॑ ।  
> हस्ते॒ दधा॑नो॒ नर्या॑ पु॒रूणि॑ निवे॒शयं॑च्च प्रसु॒वंच्च॒ भूम॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । देवः । यातु । सविता । सुरत्नः । अंतरिक्षप्राः । वहमानः । अश्वैः ।  
> हस्ते । दधानः । नर्या । पुरूणि । निवेशयन् । च । प्रसुवन् । च । भूम ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुरत्नः शोभनरत्नोपेतोऽंतरिक्षप्राः स्वकीयेन तेजसांतरिक्षस्य पूरयिताश्वैः स्वकीयैर्वाहैर्वहमान उह्यमानः सविता सर्वस्य प्रेरको देव आ यातु । आगच्छतु । कीदृशः । नर्या नर्याणि मनुष्यहितानि पुरूणि बहूनि धनानि हस्ते पाणौ दधानो दातुं धारयन् भूम भूतानि निवेशयंश्च रात्रिषु स्वे स्थाने स्थापयंश्च प्रसुवंश्चाहःसु प्रेरयंश्च । एवंभूतः सविता देव आ यातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May the god Savitā, the impeller of all, come: he is possessed of fine jewels (सुरत्नः (*suratnaḥ*)), fills the firmament (अंतरिक्षप्राः (*aṃtarikṣaprāḥ*)) with his own radiance, and is borne by his horses. How is he? Holding in his hand (हस्ते (*haste*)) many treasures (नर्या (*naryā*), good for men), meaning to give them; settling (निवेशयन् (*niveśayan*)) beings (भूम (*bhūma*)) in their own places at night, and impelling (प्रसुवन् (*prasuvan*)) them to action by day. Such a Savitā, the god, may he come.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नर्या (*naryā*)—beneficial to men
- पुरूणि (*purūṇi*)—abundant treasures
- हस्ते (*haste*)—in the hand
- दधानः (*dadhānaḥ*)—holding
- भूम (*bhūma*)—all beings
- निवेशयंश्च (*niveśayaṃśca*)—and settling in their own places at night-times
- प्रसुवंश्च (*prasuvaṃśca*)—and making them intent on work by day
- सुरत्नः (*suratnaḥ*)—possessed of excellent wealth
- अंतरिक्षप्राः (*aṃtarikṣaprāḥ*)—pervading all the firmaments with his own radiance
- अश्वैः (*aśvaiḥ*)—by horses
- वहमानः (*vahamānaḥ*)—borne
- सविता (*savitā*)—the impeller of all
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the god Savitṛ
- आ यातु (*ā yātu*)—may he graciously come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Holding in his hand abundant treasures beneficial to men, settling all beings in their own places at night and making them intent on work by day, possessed of excellent wealth, pervading all the firmaments with his own radiance, borne by horses, the impeller of all, may the god Savitṛ graciously come. (May he rise.)

##### English translation
Borne by his steeds, may the divine Savitri, who ie possessed of precious treasure, and filling the firmament (with radiance), come hither, holding in his hands many things good for man, and (both) tranquillizing and animating living beings.

### Printed p. 264
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उद॑स्य बा॒हू शि॑थि॒रा बृ॒हंता॑ हिर॒ण्यया॑ दि॒वो अंता॑ अनष्टां  
> नू॒नं सो अ॑स्य महि॒मा प॑निष्ट॒ सूर॑श्चिदस्मा॒ अनु॑ दादप॒स्यां ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । अस्य । बाहू इति । शिथिरा । बृहंता । हिरण्यया । दिवः । अंतान् । अनष्टां ।  
> नूनं । सः । अस्य । महिमा । पनिष्ट । सूरः । चित् । अस्मै । अनु । दात् । अपस्यां ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शिथिरा शिथिलौ दानार्थं प्रसारितौ बृहंता बृहंतौ महांतौ हिरण्यया हिरण्मयौ सुवर्णमयावस्य सवितुः संबंधिनौ बाहू हस्तौ दिवोऽंतरिक्षस्यांतान् पर्यंतानुदनष्टां । ऊर्ध्वं संतौ व्याप्नुवतां । नूनमद्यास्येदृग्भूतस्य सवितुः स
> तादृशो महिमा महत्त्वं पनिष्ट । अस्माभिः स्तूयते । सूरश्चित् सूर्योऽप्यस्मै सवित्रेऽपस्यां कर्मेच्छामनु दात् । अनुददातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The two arms (बाहू (*bāhū*)) of this Savitā, relaxed (शिथिरा (*śithirā*), stretched out for giving), vast, golden, reach up (उदनष्टां (*udanaṣṭāṃ*)) to the ends of the sky (दिवः (*divaḥ*), the firmament), pervading while above. Now the greatness (महिमा (*mahimā*)) of such a Savitā is praised (पनिष्ट (*paniṣṭa*)) by us. May Sūrya also grant (अनु दात् (*anu dāt*)) to this Savitā the wish for action (अपस्यां (*apasyāṃ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Savitṛ
- शिथिरा (*śithirā*)—spread out for giving
- बृहंता (*bṛhaṃtā*)—extensive
- हिरण्यया (*hiraṇyayā*)—golden
- बाहू (*bāhū*)—the arms
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the firmament
- अंतान् (*aṃtān*)—the end parts
- उदनष्टां (*udanaṣṭāṃ*)—have spread covering
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- अस्य (*asya*)—of such a Savitṛ
- सः (*saḥ*)—that famous
- महिमा (*mahimā*)—greatness
- पनिष्ट (*paniṣṭa*)—is praised
- सूरश्चित् (*sūraścit*)—Sūrya too
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—to this Savitṛ
- अपस्यां (*apasyāṃ*)—the wish for action
- अनु दात् (*anu dāt*)—may he grant

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)
